<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准原始-对偶内点法在解附近线性系统会变得病态，这在双精度下尚可接受，但在单精度下会引发灾难性后果，阻碍内点法充分利用现代机器学习中加速硬件的优势。需要一种适用于低精度计算的可微内点法。

Method: 采用替代互补性表示（alternative complementarity representation）替代标准互补性条件，确保线性系统即使在解附近也保持谱有界性，从而避免算术异常并支持准确梯度计算。

Result: 该方法使得内点求解器能够在单精度下可靠求解和微分先前仅能在双精度下处理的优化问题，通过消融实验与标准内点法对比验证，并在双层学习和端到端学习场景中展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的可微内点法通过缓解低精度下的病态问题，弥合了传统优化方法与现代机器学习硬件加速之间的鸿沟，为在受限精度条件下可微求解约束优化问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Primal-dual interior-point methods solve constrained convex optimization problems to tight tolerances with speed and robustness. Their solutions are also efficiently differentiable with respect to the problem data through the implicit function theorem. However, the standard treatment of primal-dual complementarity makes the underlying linear systems increasingly ill-conditioned near the solution. While this ill-conditioning is often benign in double precision, it can be catastrophic in single precision, preventing interior-point methods from fully exploiting the accelerated hardware that underpins modern machine learning. This paper introduces a differentiable interior-point method designed for low-precision arithmetic. By using an alternative complementarity representation, we ensure that the underlying linear systems remain spectrally bounded -- even near the solution -- a property that is essential for computing accurate gradients and avoiding arithmetic exceptions. As a result, our method enables interior-point solvers to reliably solve and differentiate optimization problems in single precision that were previously confined to double precision. We demonstrate the approach through an ablation study against the standard interior-point formulation and applications in bilevel and end-to-end learning settings where differentiating through constrained optimization is essential. The source code is available at https://github.com/qpax-solver/qpax.

</details>


### [97] [Quantitative Fredholm backstepping and rapid stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17941)
*Ludovick Gagnon,Amaury Hayat,Swann Marx,Shengquan Xiang,Christophe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对自伴和斜伴算子A，证明了阶数严格大于1的Fredholm反步变换的存在性，克服了经典框架的两大局限，并获得了定量快速镇定和小时间零可控性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fredholm反步变换框架在处理高阶算子时存在局限性，且传统方法依赖紧性论证和Riesz基机制，无法获得明确的同构结构及定量估计。

Method: 明确识别了变换T构造中使用的同构结构，从而绕过紧性论证和Riesz基机制，推导出变换范数关于衰减率λ的定量尖锐估计。

Result: 获得了算子范数∥T∥和∥T^{-1}∥关于衰减率λ的定量尖锐估计，并由此得到一类算子的定量快速镇定和小时间零可控性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了高阶算子Fredholm反步变换的存在性理论，提供了明确的同构结构，提升了估计的定量精度，拓展了反步法在算子控制理论中的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the existence of Fredholm backstepping transformations for self-adjoint and skew-adjoint operators $A$. Under suitable assumptions on the operator $A$ and the possibly unbounded control operator $B$, we prove the existence of a Fredholm backstepping transformation for operators of order strictly greater than $1$.
  This work overcomes two major limitations of the classical Fredholm backstepping framework. One of the main contributions is the explicit identification of the underlying isomorphism used in the construction of the transformation $T$, thereby bypassing the compactness arguments and Riesz basis mechanisms traditionally used in the literature. This explicit structure enables us to derive quantitative and sharp estimates for $\|T\|_{\mathcal{L}(H;H)}$ and $\|T^{-1}\|_{\mathcal{L}(H;H)}$ with respect to the decay rate $λ$. As a consequence, we obtain quantitative rapid stabilization and small-time null controllability results for a broad class of operators.

</details>


### [98] [From Saddle Points Toward Global Minima: A Newton-Type Method on Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17963)
*Razvan-Andrei Lascu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton (WSFN)，一种在Wasserstein空间上最小化非凸泛函的二阶方法。该方法通过正则化的平方Wasserstein Hessian平方根预处理Wasserstein梯度，在保持正曲率方向吸引力的同时诱导负曲率方向排斥力，从而克服标准Wasserstein牛顿动力学易被鞍点吸引的问题。在良性景观假设下，WSFN能在多项式时间内逃离鞍点区域并到达全局极小点的α邻域，且在该邻域内以线性收敛速度收敛至非退化全局极小点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扰动Wasserstein梯度方法只能避免良性景观中的鞍点，但本质上仍是一阶方法，一旦迭代进入全局极小点邻域后无法提供快速局部收敛。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton方法，利用正则化的平方Wasserstein Hessian平方根预处理梯度，实现正曲率吸引与负曲率排斥。

Result: 在良性景观假设下，WSFN在多项式时间内逃离鞍点区域并到达全局极小点α邻域；进入邻域后，在L²-Wasserstein距离下线性收敛至非退化全局极小点。

Conclusion: WSFN作为二阶方法，相比一阶扰动方法在鞍点参数依赖上具有改进，并实现了快速局部收敛，还给出了基于粒子的实现方法。

Abstract: We study the minimization of non-convex functionals over the Wasserstein space. While recent work has showed that perturbed Wasserstein gradient methods can avoid saddle points for benign landscapes, existing approaches remain essentially first-order and do not provide fast local convergence once the iterates enter a neighborhood of a global minimizer. We propose Wasserstein Saddle-Free Newton (WSFN), a second-order method that preconditions the Wasserstein gradient by a regularized square root of the squared Wasserstein Hessian. This construction preserves attraction toward directions of positive curvature while inducing repulsion along directions of negative curvature, thereby overcoming the tendency of standard Wasserstein Newton dynamics to be attracted to saddles. We also establish second-order sufficient optimality conditions on Wasserstein space for strict local minimality. Under regularity and benign landscape assumptions, we prove that WSFN escapes saddle regions and reaches an $α$-neighborhood of a global minimizer in polynomial time, with improved dependence on saddle parameters compared with prior perturbed first-order methods. Once inside this neighborhood, we show that WSFN converges linearly in $L^2$-Wasserstein distance to a non-degenerate global minimizer. Finally, we present a particle-based implementation of the method.

</details>


### [99] [Control-Channel Informativity for Koopman EDMDc under Behavior-Policy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17966)
*Yue Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究EDMDc在控制通道信息不足时的失效问题，提出条件干预证书（CIC）作为控制通道可辨识性的充要条件，并给出闭环统计界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDMDc通常由行为策略或反馈控制器生成的轨迹训练，这类数据能准确预测观测行为，但无法识别新的输入命令如何改变提升状态。现有方法忽视了这种控制通道信息不足的问题。

Method: 将控制通道信息不足问题建模为控制通道可辨识性问题；引入条件干预证书（CIC），定义为输入数据投影到活跃提升状态特征张成空间后的残差输入协方差，即EDMDc信息矩阵中提升状态块的Schur补；证明CIC严格正定是有限样本下提升控制通道块可辨识性的充要条件；利用可预测回归量、条件次高斯转移噪声和正则化Schur补给出闭环统计界。

Result: 严格证明了CIC正定性与控制通道可辨识性的充要关系；CIC为零时不同模型在观测轨迹上一致但与反事实输入不一致；标量反馈示例表明抖动反馈下残差干预信息随抖动幅度二次增长，控制通道误差随干预信噪比倒数下降；实验在线性系统、Duffing和Van der Pol基准上验证了标度律；更大的EDMDc采集网格表明状态覆盖、联合回归条件和干预激励是互补的诊断指标而非可互换的性能度量。

Conclusion: 控制通道的可辨识性需要足够的干预激励，仅靠状态覆盖或回归条件无法替代；条件干预证书是诊断EDMDc控制通道信息不足的有效工具。

Abstract: Extended dynamic mode decomposition with control (EDMDc) is often trained from trajectories generated by a behavior policy or a pre-existing feedback controller. Such data can predict the observed behavior accurately while failing to identify how new input commands change the lifted state. This paper studies that failure as a control-channel informativity problem. We introduce a conditional intervention certificate, defined as the residual input covariance after projecting the input data away from the active lifted-state feature span. The certificate is the Schur complement of the lifted-state block in the EDMDc information matrix. We prove that its strict positivity is necessary and sufficient for finite-sample sample- identifiability of the lifted control-channel block. If the certificate vanishes, distinct lifted models agree on every collected transition but disagree under counterfactual inputs. We then give a closed-loop statistical bound using predictable regressors, conditionally sub-Gaussian transition noise, and a regularized Schur complement. A scalar feedback example shows the unavoidable scaling: under dithered feedback, residual intervention information grows quadratically with the dither amplitude and the control-channel error decreases with the inverse intervention signal-to-noise scale. New experiments verify these scalings exactly in a linear system and diagnostically in controlled Duffing and Van der Pol benchmarks. A larger EDMDc acquisition grid further shows that state coverage, joint regression conditioning, and intervention excitation are complementary diagnostics rather than interchangeable performance score.

</details>


### [100] [Robust Optimal Reinsurance, Investment,and Surplus Allocation for Epstein-Zin Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18145)
*Junyi Guo,Jianxuan Li,Qianqian Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完全市场中具有Epstein-Zin递归偏好的保险人的稳健最优再保险、投资和内部盈余分配（消费）问题，在风险资产价格具有O-U过程随机漂移率的设定下，分别针对单位和非单位跨期替代弹性情形，通过动态规划方法推导出显式解，并与非稳健解及Campbell-Shiller近似解进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多基于期望效用理论或忽略模型不确定性，而保险人在实践中面临模型不确定性和模糊厌恶。本研究旨在将稳健控制思想与Epstein-Zin递归偏好结合，解决不完全市场中保险人的联合最优再保险-投资-消费问题，以更贴合现实决策情境。

Method: 采用经典动态规划方法，在Epstein-Zin递归偏好框架下，针对单位和非单位跨期替代弹性两种情形，求解HJB方程得到稳健最优策略的显式解。通过将稳健解与非稳健解及Campbell-Shiller近似解进行数值对比，验证解的性质与近似精度。

Result: 推导出了稳健最优再保险、投资和消费策略的显式表达式，证明了所得解确实满足最优控制问题。数值结果表明稳健解与非稳健解的差异符合经济直觉，且Campbell-Shiller近似方法在特定参数范围内具有一定精度。

Conclusion: 本文为不完全市场中具有递归偏好和模糊厌恶的保险人提供了联合决策的显式解，拓展了稳健最优保险金融决策理论，同时为Campbell-Shiller近似在该领域的适用性提供了评估依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the robust optimal reinsurance,investment,and internal surplus distribution (i.e., consumption) problem for an insurer with Epstein-Zin recursive preferences in an incomplete market. It is assumed that the insurer can allocate wealth to a financial market consisting of a risk-free asset and a risky asset, where the price process of the risky asset follows a diffusion process with a stochastic drift rate governed by an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (O-U) process. For both the unit and non-unit elasticity of intertemporal substitution (EIS) cases, by applying the classical dynamic programming approach, we derive explicit solutions for the optimal robust reinsurance, investment,and consumption strategies and also verify that the obtained solutions indeed solve the optimal control problem. Furthermore, we compare the robust solutions with their non-robust counterparts, and the comparative results shown in the figures are consistent with economic intuition. Finally, we contrast the exact solutions with the Campbell-Shiller approximation and assess the accuracy of the approximation method.

</details>


### [101] [Resolvent Moreau identities without monotonicity: theory and applications to Gabay duality, Douglas--Rachford and ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18158)
*Andrew Calcan,Jordan Collard,Alberto De Marchi,Scott B. Lindstrom*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非凸函数间存在一种新型对偶关系——并非函数本身，而是其预解算子之间满足类似Moreau恒等式的对偶性。该关系可推广至任意集值映射，并用于分析现有算子方案及设计新算法。研究发现Douglas-Rachford分裂与ADMM之间的对偶关系可扩展至非单调包含问题，并通过该关系提供了ADMM在若干开放情形下发散的显式反例。基于此，作者设计了一类收敛的预解同伦方案，用于求解非凸正则化最小绝对偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对偶理论局限于凸函数，非凸情形下传统对偶失效。本文旨在探索非凸函数间是否存在某种替代性对偶关系，并利用该关系分析现有算法（如ADMM）的收敛性边界，同时为难以求解的非凸正则化最小绝对偏差问题设计新算法。

Method: 从预解算子的对偶性出发，证明任意集值映射满足推广的Moreau恒等式；将Daniel Gabay发现的Douglas-Rachford分裂与ADMM之间的对偶关系拓展至非单调包含问题；利用更易分析的DR格式构建显式反例；设计一类收敛的预解同伦方案。

Result: 建立了非凸函数间基于预解算子的对偶关系；给出了ADMM在若干未解决情形下发散的显式反例；提出的预解同伦方案成功求解了非凸正则化最小绝对偏差问题。

Conclusion: 预解算子层面的对偶性突破了函数凸性的限制，为分析非凸优化算法提供了新工具。该方法不仅解释了现有分裂算法的内在联系，还能指导新算法的设计，尤其适用于目标函数中凸分量缺乏强凸性的困难问题。

Abstract: Duality is most often defined as a relationship between convex functions. If those functions are nonconvex, classical duality breaks down. Notwithstanding, we show that another kind of duality still exists, not between the functions themselves, but between the so-called resolvent operators used to solve associated problems. In fact, this duality-like relationship holds for any set-valued mapping, and is a generalization of the Moreau's identity. We use this duality to study existing operator schemes and to design new ones. In particular, we show that the duality-like relationship Daniel Gabay illuminated between the Douglas--Rachford splitting (DR) and the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) extends to nonmonotone inclusion problems. We use this relationship to provide explicit counterexamples to the convergence of ADMM in several open cases, by studying the (easier to analyse) DR scheme. Motivated by our observations, we design a class of convergent resolvent homotopy schemes and use them to solve nonconvex-regularised least absolute deviations problems. This important problem class has received little attention in the literature, since the convex component of the objective does not enjoy strong convexity.

</details>


### [102] [Trajectory convergence and $o(t^{-2})$ rates for Nesterov accelerated primal-dual dynamics without Lipschitz gradient assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18236)
*Xin He,Nan-Jing Hang,Yi-Bin Xiao,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性约束凸优化问题的Nesterov加速原始-对偶动力系统，在有限维空间中证明了当α≥3时轨迹收敛到原始-对偶解，且无需∇f的Lipschitz连续性假设；当α>3时，建立了目标残差和可行性违例的o(t^{-2})改进收敛速率。该分析基于Bregman距离方法，并首次涵盖了临界情形α=3以及无需Lipschitz梯度的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Boţ和Nguyen, 2021）已在Hilbert框架下建立该动力系统轨迹的弱收敛性，但要求α>3且∇f满足Lipschitz连续性。本文旨在移除Lipschitz连续性假设，并将结果推广到α≥3，特别是临界情形α=3尚未被研究。

Method: 采用Bregman距离论证替代传统的Lipschitz连续性假设，在有限维空间中分析Nesterov加速原始-对偶动力系统的收敛性。同时将该策略扩展到时间尺度化的原始-对偶动力学系统。

Result: 1) 当α≥3时，轨迹收敛到原始-对偶解，无需∇f的Lipschitz连续性；2) 当α>3时，目标残差和可行性违例均达到o(t^{-2})的改进收敛速率；3) 首次建立了临界情形α=3的轨迹收敛性结果。

Conclusion: 本文在不假设Lipschitz梯度的条件下，首次证明了Nesterov加速原始-对偶动力系统在α≥3时的轨迹收敛性，并获得了更优的收敛速率。Bregman距离方法为该类问题的分析提供了新途径，时间尺度化系统的推广进一步验证了方法的普适性。

Abstract: We consider the Nesterov accelerated primal-dual dynamical system \[ \begin{cases} \ddot{x}(t)+\dfracα{t}\dot{x}(t) +\nabla f(x(t)) +A^\top\bigl(λ(t)+θt\dotλ(t)\bigr)+βA^\top(Ax(t)-b)=0,\\[0.6em] \ddotλ(t)+\dfracα{t}\dotλ(t) -\bigl(A(x(t)+θt\dot{x}(t))-b\bigr)=0, \end{cases} \] which is linked to the linearly constrained optimization problem $
  \min_{x\in\mathbb{R}^n} f(x),\ s.t.\ Ax=b, $ where $α\ge 3$ and $f$ is convex and continuously differentiable. In a Hilbert framework, the weak convergence of its trajectory was established by Boţ and Nguyen (J. Differential Equations, 303:369--406, 2021) under $α>3$ and the Lipschitz continuity assumption on $\nabla f$. In this paper, we prove in finite-dimensional spaces that the trajectory converges to a primal-dual solution for $α\ge3$, without assuming Lipschitz continuity of $\nabla f$. Moreover, when $α>3$, we establish improved $o(t^{-2})$ convergence rates for both the objective residual and the feasibility violation. Our analysis relies on Bregman-distance arguments, instead of the Lipschitz continuity of $\nabla f$. The same strategy can also be extended to time-scaled primal-dual dynamics to obtain analogous convergence results. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first results in this topic without Lipschitz gradient assumption. Our result also present the first work on the convergence of the trajectory of the accelerated primal-dual dynamical system for the critical case $α=3$.

</details>


### [103] [Optimizing vaccine allocation in an age-structured SIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18256)
*Luís Almeida,Romain Ducasse,Elisa Paparelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究疫情暴发期间的最优疫苗分配问题，采用具有非局部相互作用的年龄结构SIR模型，目标是有限疫苗供应下最小化伤亡人数，通过将动态优化问题转化为静态优化问题以获得最优分配方案的定性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在疫情暴发期间，疫苗供应有限，如何最优地分配疫苗以最小化疫情造成的伤亡是一个关键问题。由于不同年龄群体在感染率和死亡率上存在异质性，需要考虑年龄结构的影响。

Method: 采用年龄结构SIR模型描述疫情动态，包含非局部相互作用；将随时间与年龄变化的疫苗接种作为控制函数；将原始动态优化问题等价转化为一个静态优化问题，进而分析最优疫苗分配的性质。

Result: 证明了原动态最优控制问题等价于一个静态优化问题，并利用该等价性推导出有限疫苗供应下最优分配方案的定性性质。

Conclusion: 通过问题转化与定性分析，为疫情中考虑年龄异质性的最优疫苗分配策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem where the objective is to find the best vaccine allocation during an epidemic outbreak. The epidemic dynamics is described by an age-structured SIR model with nonlocal interactions. Both the infection and death rates depend on the age of the individuals, reflecting the effect of heterogeneities within the population. Our model includes a vaccination term, depending on time and age, which serves as a control function. The aim is to minimize the impact of the epidemic, that is, the number of casualties, under the constraint of limited vaccine supply. In a first part, we show that our optimization problem is equivalent to another static optimization problem. We then use this new optimization problem to obtain qualitative properties for the optimal allocations of vaccines.

</details>


### [104] [Null Controllability for Degenerate Parabolic Equations with Internal Control Applied on a Measurable Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18307)
*Donghui Yang,Mengze Gu,Baozhu Guo,Ghadir Shokor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文延续前期工作，利用分离变量法推导了另一类退化抛物方程的Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并证明了其在可测子集内控制下的零能控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在拓展前期工作中提出的分离变量法，为另一类退化抛物方程建立谱不等式并证明其零能控性，从而为退化抛物方程的可控性研究提供一种替代方法。

Method: 采用分离变量法推导退化抛物方程的Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并基于该不等式证明方程在内控制作用于可测子集时的零能控性。

Result: 成功获得了所考察退化抛物方程的Lebeau-Robbiano谱不等式，并建立了该方程在可测子集上内部控制下的零能控性。

Conclusion: 分离变量法可作为证明退化抛物方程可控性结果的一种有效替代途径。

Abstract: This work serves as a continuation of our preceding paper [28]. In that study, we presented a separable variable method to derive the Lebeau-Robbiano spectral inequality for a specific degenerate parabolic equation and subsequently employed it to demonstrate the null controllability of said equation when internal control is applied to an open subset. In the current paper, we reapply the separable variable method to attain the Lebeau-Robbiano spectral inequality for a different degenerate parabolic equation, and we substantiate the null controllability of this equation with internal control acting on a measurable subset. This approach may offer an alternative means of proving controllability results for degenerate parabolic equations.

</details>


### [105] [Attention-based PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18315)
*Rodrigo Maulen-Soto,Claire Boyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从无监督学习中的主成分分析(PCA)视角研究了注意力机制。证明在训练于高斯数据时，softmax和线性注意力层学习到的参数与协方差矩阵的主特征向量对齐，建立了与PCA的直接联系。覆盖了有限和无限prompt两种场景，在无限prompt极限下收敛到全局最优解，在有限prompt下存在采样效应。并在spiked Wishart协方差的情境学习设置中验证了注意力能恢复信号方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从PCA这一经典无监督问题出发，揭示注意力机制的本质计算原理，为注意力在表示学习中的能力提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析：将注意力（softmax和线性注意力）的训练动力学与PCA进行类比。考虑两种场景：无限prompt极限（收敛到全局最优）和有限prompt设置（存在采样效应）。并扩展至spiked Wishart协方差的情境学习设置。

Result: 注意力层学习到的参数与协方差矩阵的主特征向量对齐；无限prompt下收敛到全局最优的谱方向；有限prompt下行为类似但有采样效应；在spiked Wishart协方差下能恢复信号方向。

Conclusion: 注意力机制在无监督目标下本质上执行了类似PCA的计算，这为其表示学习能力提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study attention mechanisms through the lens of a canonical unsupervised problem: principal component analysis (PCA). We show that, when trained on Gaussian data, both softmax and linear attention layers learn parameters that align with the principal eigenvectors of the covariance matrix, thereby establishing a direct and explicit connection with PCA. Our analysis covers both finite and infinite prompt regimes. In the infinite-prompt limit, we prove convergence to globally optimal solutions aligned with the leading spectral direction, while in the finiteprompt setting we show that the same behavior emerges up to sampling effects. We further extend the analysis to an in-context setting with spiked Wishart covariances, where attention successfully recovers the underlying signal direction. These results demonstrate that attention inherently performs PCA-like computations under unsupervised objectives, providing a theoretical foundation for its representation-learning capabilities.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Model for CVRPTW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18393)
*Imran Meghazi,Éric Bourreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Grover Search框架的量子算法，用于解决带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题(CVRPTW)，采用先路线后聚类的经典分解思路，并引入一种高效的量子比特分裂启发式建模，相比标准TSP量子公式仅增加线性数量的决策量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邮政包裹递送等时效敏感场景中常面临CVRPTW问题，经典"先路线后聚类"方法存在固有次优性，而量子计算有望克服这一局限。

Method: 基于Grover Search框架，采用受经典"先路线后聚类"启发的门模型量子计算机实现，提出一种量子比特高效的split启发式建模，使得决策量子比特数量相比标准TSP量子公式仅呈线性增长。

Result: 所提方法在NISQ时代以线性数量的额外决策量子比特即可处理时间窗和容量约束，降低了硬件资源需求。

Conclusion: 该量子算法为CVRPTW提供了一种在近期量子设备上可行的求解方案，通过量子比特高效建模缓解了NISQ时代量子比特数量有限的瓶颈。

Abstract: This paper proposes a quantum algorithm for the capacitated vehicle routing problem with time windows (CVRPTW) based on Grover Search framework. This problem is often faced by Postal services in the context of package delivery or other time-sensitive operations. We provide an implementation on gate based quantum computer of a model inspired by classical route first, cluster second technique. The quantum paradigm allows to overcome suboptimality inherent property of this decomposition. In the current NISQ (Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum) era, the most important limitation is the number of available qubits which makes time windows and capacity constraints hard to tackle. We introduce a qubit-efficient split-inspired modeling which adds only a linear number of decision qubits to standard quantum formulations for Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP).

</details>


### [107] [Solving a large oral examination timetabling problem using a multidimensional knapsack MILP formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18402)
*Cyrille Briand,Jean-Pierre Belaud*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对法国顶尖工程师学院的口试排课问题（OETP），将其建模为多维背包问题（MKP）并采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解。通过将考生分配到预验证的日程方案而非单独时间段，大幅降低组合复杂度。在2025年真实数据上，SCIP求解器在20分钟内成功排定7,804名考生中的7,796人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法国工程师学院联合招生口试涉及7,000余名考生、四周时间跨度，面临考试冲突、地理来源、签证要求等复杂约束，传统人工排课效率低下，亟需自动化优化方案。

Method: 将OETP建模为多维背包问题（MKP），采用MILP形式化表达；核心策略是将考生分配至预先生成并验证的完整日程方案（而非单独时间段），从而降低搜索空间的组合爆炸。

Result: 基于2025年真实数据集，使用SCIP求解器在20分钟时间限制内，成功为7,804名考生中的7,796人（99.9%）安排了可行的口试日程。

Conclusion: 所提MILP-MKP方法能有效解决大规模口试排课问题，在极短时间内实现接近100%的覆盖率，验证了预验证日程方案策略在降低组合复杂度方面的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Oral Examination Timetabling Problem (OETP) for France's prestigious engineering schools, an organization managed by the Service des Concours Communs Polytechniques (SCCP). The scheduling is highly complex, involving over 7,000 candidates across a four-week period while accounting for constraints such as exam overlaps, geographic origin, and visa requirements. To manage this scale, the paper shows how to model the problem as a Multidimensional Knapsack Problem (MKP) using a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation. Their strategy reduces combinatorial complexity by assigning candidates to a predetermined set of pre-validated schedules rather than individual time slots. Experimental results using the SCIP solver on a real 2025 data instance successfully accommodated 7,796 out of 7,804 candidates within a 20-minute time limit.

</details>


### [108] [The distance-based formation controller design for multi-agent systems in port-Hamiltonian form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18502)
*Jingyi Zhao,Yongxin Wu,Héctor García de Marina,Yuhu Wu,Yann Le Gorrec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对受端口-哈密顿动力学控制的多智能体系统，提出了一种融合距离编队控制与碰撞避免的统一控制框架。通过构建有向无环图的符号关联矩阵、设计仅含吸引势能的人工势场以及安全屏障强制碰撞避免，解决了传统方法中的局部极小值问题，并利用LaSalle不变性原理保证了闭环系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编队控制中智能体之间的碰撞可能导致实体损坏，因此需要在实现编队控制的同时确保碰撞避免。传统人工势场法存在局部极小值这一固有问题，亟需新的设计思路来克服。

Method: ①构建具有满列秩性质的符号关联矩阵（对应有向无环图）；②提出仅含吸引势能的人工势场设计，规避传统势场的局部极小值问题；③利用安全屏障强制实现碰撞避免；④设计统一的控制器同时处理速度跟踪、编队获取和智能体间安全；⑤基于LaSalle不变性原理证明闭环系统稳定性。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提控制策略的有效性与可行性，表明该统一控制器能够同时实现速度跟踪、目标编队形成以及智能体间防碰撞。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于吸引势能+安全屏障的框架有效解决了编队控制中的碰撞避免问题，并克服了传统人工势场法的局部极小值缺陷，为端口-哈密顿多智能体系统的安全编队控制提供了理论保障与实用方案。

Abstract: Based on the practical scenario where collisions in formation control may lead to agent damage, this paper investigates the integrated problem of distance-based formation control and collision avoidance for multi-agent systems governed by port-Hamiltonian dynamics. A foundational step involves constructing a signed incidence matrix, which, by design, corresponds to a directed acyclic graph and possesses the full column rank property. To overcome the prevalent issue of local minima in traditional artificial potential fields, a novel design utilizing attraction-only potentials is introduced, with collision avoidance rigorously enforced by safety barriers. This framework leads to a unified controller that concurrently manages velocity tracking, target formation acquisition, and inter-agent safety. The stability of the resulting closed-loop system is guaranteed through LaSalle's invariance principle. Numerical simulations demonstrate the validity and effectiveness of the proposed control strategy.

</details>


### [109] [Boundary Stabilization for the Rayleigh Beam System under Event-triggered Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18520)
*Siwen Wang,Yi Cheng,Yuhu Wu,Yongxin Wu,Wen Kang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对瑞利梁系统的边界镇定问题，提出了两种结合事件触发机制的控制律，并通过积分乘子技术和能量摄动法构建了参数确定条件，保证了闭环系统的指数稳定性，数值算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决瑞利梁系统的边界镇定问题，并引入事件触发机制以减少控制更新频率，同时保证系统的指数稳定性。

Method: 提出了两种事件触发控制律；利用积分乘子技术和能量摄动法构建参数确定条件；精确确定期望的指数衰减率。

Result: 建立了保证闭环系统指数稳定性的充分条件，能够精确确定指数衰减率；数值算例验证了事件触发控制方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的事件触发控制律能有效解决瑞利梁的边界镇定问题，在减少控制更新的同时保证系统指数稳定，且指数衰减率可精确确定。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two event-triggered control laws incorporating an eventtriggering mechanism to tackle the boundary stabilization for the Rayleigh beam system. Under this event-triggered controls, a sufficient condition for parameter determination is constructed to guarantee the exponential stability of the closed-loop system by using the integral multiplier technique and energy perturbation method, wherein the desired exponential decay rate can be precisely determined. Numerical examples are presented to demonstrate the efficacy of the event-triggered control methodology.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Gradient Methods for Distributed Saddle Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18604)
*Ruichen Luo,Anton Rodomanov,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了分布式鞍点问题（SP）的设定，提出了一种基于多阶段归约到残差范数解耦最小化的新方法，在零尊重框架下实现了最优通信复杂度，并通过匹配下界证明了梯度跨度算法族内的通信最优性；同时将该方法扩展到变分不等式问题（VIP），取得了新的最先进通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式鞍点问题已成为机器学习和多智能体系统中众多现代应用的重要框架，但其理论基础尚未被充分建立，现有方法的通信复杂度存在改进空间。

Method: 提出一种解耦方法，通过多阶段归约将原问题转化为残差范数的解耦最小化问题，在零尊重框架下实现通信成本优化。

Result: 该方法在零尊重框架下达到最优通信代价，优于已知最好的通信代价和Extragradient方法的长期oracle代价；通过匹配下界证明在梯度跨度算法族中通信最优；在VIP上取得新的最先进通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布式鞍点问题理论基础的空白，提出的解耦方法在通信效率上具有最优性，并成功推广至更一般的变分不等式问题。

Abstract: The distributed setting for Saddle Problems (SPs) has recently emerged as a framework for various modern applications in machine learning and multiagent systems. Despite its relevance, the theoretical foundations of this setting have not yet been thoroughly established. In this paper, we advance this research direction by formalizing the distributed setup for SPs and providing rigorous definitions of communication and computational costs. Our main result is a novel decoupled method that achieves optimal communication cost within the zero-respecting framework. Our method is based on a multi-stage reduction to the decoupled minimization of residual norms, which yields strict improvements over the best known communication cost for the class and the long-standing oracle cost of the Extragradient method. Further, we show by a matching lower bound that our method is communication-optimal within the family of gradient-span algorithms. Finally, we study the extension of distributed SP into Variational Inequality Problem (VIP), which generalizes two-player zero-sum games to multiplayer general-sum games. We show that our decoupled method achieves a new state-of-the-art communication complexity for this broader class.

</details>


### [111] [Singleton Optimality in Standard Quadratic Programs with the GOE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18620)
*Xin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯正交系综（GOE）下标准二次优化问题在单纯形上的全局最优解支撑大小，证明了支撑大于1的概率渐近于2√(2π) √(log n)/n，且最小对角元顶点以趋于1的概率为全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机二次优化问题在单纯形上的全局最优解结构尚不清晰，特别是当目标矩阵来自GOE时，全局最优解的支撑大小及其概率分布有待精确刻画。

Method: 结合精确的两坐标边沿改进条件与基于对角顺序统计量的乘积公式，通过边界层估计识别主要贡献项，并证明支撑大小≥3的情形可忽略。

Result: 证明了Prob(κ_n>1) ~ 2√(2π) √(log n)/n，即全局最优解支撑大于1的概率以该速率趋于0；最小对角元顶点为全局最优的概率趋于1，并给出了显式一阶修正。

Conclusion: 在GOE矩阵设定下，标准二次优化问题的全局最优解几乎必然是单点支撑（即唯一顶点），且该顶点为最小对角元对应的顶点，其概率趋近于1。

Abstract: We study the standard quadratic optimization problem over the simplex when the objective matrix is drawn from the Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE). Let \(κ_n\) denote the support size of the almost surely unique global optimizer. We prove \[ \Prob(κ_n>1)\sim 2\sqrt{2π}\,\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}. \] The proof combines an exact two-coordinate condition for edge improvement with a product formula obtained by conditioning on the diagonal order statistics. Boundary-layer estimates identify the leading contribution and show that supports of size at least three are negligible. Consequently, the minimum-diagonal vertex is globally optimal with probability tending to one, with an explicit first-order correction.

</details>


### [112] [Robust Optimization Under Objective Functional Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18669)
*Yue Song,Yuxi Lu,Gang Li,Kairui Feng,Qi Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的鲁棒优化(RO)框架——目标函数不确定性鲁棒优化(ObRO)，采用双层min-max结构，内层在连续函数空间中寻找最坏情况目标函数，外层在欧几里得空间中找到最优决策。设计了交替求解算法，通过算子理论证明了收敛到"半全局"鞍点。基于分段线性化(PWL)近似提出了数值求解器，并应用于配电网中考虑退化的电池充电调度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒优化仅考虑参数不确定性，但实际应用中目标函数本身可能存在不确定性（如电池退化成本函数难以精确建模），现有方法无法处理这种目标函数形式的不确定性。

Method: 1) 提出ObRO的min-max数学框架；2) 设计交替求解算法，交替生成当前决策下的最坏目标函数和当前目标函数集合下的最优决策；3) 利用算子理论证明算法的"半全局"鞍点收敛性；4) 提出基于分段线性化(PWL)近似的数值求解器，并证明其数值一致性。

Result: 证明了交替算法收敛到ObRO问题的半全局鞍点；证明了PWL近似问题与原ObRO问题在数值上一致；将方法应用于配电网中考虑电池退化的充电调度问题。

Conclusion: ObRO框架有效解决了目标函数形式不确定性下的鲁棒优化问题，理论保证充分，且在电池充电调度等实际应用场景中具有可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new robust optimization (RO) formulation namely the RO under objective functional uncertainty (ObRO). The ObRO adopts a min-max structure where the inner problem finds the worst-case objective function in a continuous function space to maximize the cost, and the outer problem finds the optimal decision in a Euclidean space to minimize the cost. A solution algorithm is designed to alternately generate the worst-case objective function at the current decision and the optimal decision for the current collection of objective functions. Using operator theory, we prove that this algorithm converges to the defined ``semi-global'' saddle point of the ObRO problem. In addition, we propose a numerical solver based on the piece-wise linearization (PWL) approximation of objective functions. The PWL approximate problem is proved to be numerically consistent with the original ObRO problem. The obtained results are applied to the degradation-aware battery charging scheduling in distribution networks.

</details>


### [113] [Can Adaptive Gradient Methods Converge under Heavy-Tailed Noise? A Case Study of AdaGrad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18694)
*Zijian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了AdaGrad在重尾梯度噪声下的非凸优化收敛率（尾指数4/3<p≤2），无需预先知道p值，并建立了算法相关的下界，表明现有极小化最优率不可达；同时分析了AdaGrad-Norm变体在更宽松条件下的改进收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中梯度噪声常呈现重尾分布，现有方法（如梯度裁剪、归一化）虽能保证收敛，但自适应梯度方法（如Adam、AdaGrad）无需这些操作也能表现良好。本文旨在回答自适应梯度方法是否能在无算法修改的情况下，在重尾噪声下收敛。

Method: 以AdaGrad为研究对象，在非凸优化中证明其在重尾噪声（尾指数4/3<p≤2）下的收敛率；同时构造算法相关下界，证明AdaGrad无法达到重尾优化的极小化最优率；进一步分析AdaGrad-Norm变体在更弱假设（1<p≤2）下的改进收敛率。

Result: 1. 首次证明了AdaGrad在非凸优化、重尾噪声（4/3<p≤2）下的收敛率，且无需先验知识p。2. 建立了算法依赖的下界，表明AdaGrad无法达到重尾优化的极小化最优率。3. AdaGrad-Norm在额外温和假设下，对任意1<p≤2均有改进的收敛率。

Conclusion: 自适应梯度方法（以AdaGrad为代表）可在无任何额外操作（如梯度裁剪）的情况下，在重尾噪声下收敛，但存在固有的算法依赖下界，无法达到最优极小化率。

Abstract: Many tasks in modern machine learning are observed to involve heavy-tailed gradient noise during the optimization process. To manage this realistic and challenging setting, new mechanisms, such as gradient clipping and gradient normalization, have been introduced to ensure the convergence of first-order algorithms. However, adaptive gradient methods, a famous class of modern optimizers that includes popular $\mathtt{Adam}$ and $\mathtt{AdamW}$, often perform well even without any extra operations mentioned above. It is therefore natural to ask whether adaptive gradient methods can converge under heavy-tailed noise without any algorithmic changes. In this work, we take the first step toward answering this question by investigating a special case, $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$, the origin of adaptive gradient methods. We provide the first provable convergence rate for $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$ in non-convex optimization when the tail index $p$ satisfies $4/3<p\leq2$. Notably, this result is achieved without requiring any prior knowledge of $p$ and is hence adaptive to the tail index. In addition, we develop an algorithm-dependent lower bound, suggesting that the existing minimax rate for heavy-tailed optimization is not attainable by $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$. Lastly, we consider $\mathtt{AdaGrad}\text{-}\mathtt{Norm}$, a popular variant of $\mathtt{AdaGrad}$ in theoretical studies, and show an improved rate that holds for any $1<p\leq2$ under an extra mild assumption.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Correlation-Driven Spin Reorientation via Competing Anisotropy Channels in CrPS4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16541)
*Raju Baral,Harald O. Jeschke,Igor I. Mazin,Jue Liu,David Mandrus,Stuart Calder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析揭示准一维范德华反铁磁体CrPS4中温度诱导自旋重取向的关联驱动机制，发现铁磁链内关联在远高于TN时仍存在，并结合DFT自旋哈密顿量阐明了单离子与交换各向异性通道的差异化重正化导致有效易轴旋转和有序态倾斜，同时指出在TN以上该机制受限于主链近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准一维范德华反铁磁体CrPS4中温度诱导自旋重取向的微观机制，特别是低维磁关联如何控制磁各向异性。

Method: 采用磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析解析局域自旋方向，结合DFT导出的自旋哈密顿量，对比单离子各向异性与交换各向异性在不同温度下的贡献。

Result: 发现铁磁链内关联在远高于奈尔温度（TN）时仍持续存在；单离子各向异性保持局域性，而交换各向异性随关联衰减被重正化，导致有效易轴旋转并解释有序态自旋倾斜；TN以上理论预测偏离实验，揭示了主链近似的适用极限。

Conclusion: mPDF导出的磁关联可作为微观哈密顿量的直接输入，低维关联能有效调控磁各向异性，为理解低维磁性材料的自旋重取向提供新机制。

Abstract: We identify a correlation-driven mechanism for the temperature-induced spin reorientation in the quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals antiferromagnet CrPS4. Magnetic pair distribution function (mPDF) analysis resolves the local spin direction and shows that ferromagnetic intrachain correlations persist far above TN. Combining these correlations with a DFT-derived spin Hamiltonian reveals competing single-ion and exchange-anisotropy channels, with single-ion anisotropy remaining local while exchange anisotropy is renormalized as intersite correlations decay. This differential renormalization rotates the effective easy axis and captures the ordered-state canting. Above TN, the continued rotation beyond the model prediction delineates the limits of the dominant-chain approximation. These results establish mPDF-derived correlations as direct inputs to microscopic Hamiltonians and show how low-dimensional correlations can control magnetic anisotropy.

</details>


### [115] [Revealing Hund superdispersion with tunneling spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16580)
*Luke C. Rhodes,Fabian B. Kugler,Olivier Gingras,Carolina Marques,Edgar Abarca Morales,Phil D. C. King,Antoine Georges,Peter Wahl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过隧道谱学在Sr₂RuO₄中观测到Hund耦合导致的“超色散”谱特征，结合DFT+DMFT计算证实这些特征与Hund金属中自能的非单调能量依赖密切相关，为Hund物理提供了直接实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轨道体系中，Hund耦合会产生超越传统Mott-Hubbard范式的独特谱特征（超色散），但目前缺乏直接的实验观测证据。

Method: 采用隧道谱学实验测量Sr₂RuO₄的谱函数，并结合密度泛函理论(DFT)、动力学平均场理论(DMFT)以及连续局域态密度计算进行理论分析。

Result: 实验观测到的谱特征与理论预测高度吻合，这些特征与Hund金属中自能实部的非单调能量依赖密切相关。

Conclusion: 研究提供了Hund诱导谱特征的直接实验证据，为探测量子材料中的关联效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In cuprate superconductors, electron-electron repulsion results in characteristic spectroscopic features known as `waterfalls', where the sharp quasiparticle dispersion transitions into broad Hubbard bands. However, in multi-orbital systems, the additional Hund coupling results in behavior that defies the conventional Mott--Hubbard paradigm, creating qualitatively distinct `superdispersive' features in the spectral function. Here, we use tunneling spectroscopy to reveal this signature of Hund physics in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$. By combining density functional theory, dynamical mean-field theory, and continuum local density of states calculations, we show that the experimental features are in excellent agreement with theoretical predictions and intimately linked to the non-monotonous energy dependence of the real part of the self-energy in a Hund metal. Our results provide direct experimental evidence for Hund-induced spectroscopic features and open a new route to probing correlation effects in quantum materials.

</details>


### [116] [Systematic dynamical mean-field theory study of 3d perovskite oxides with uniform Coulomb interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16771)
*Antik Sihi,Caden Ginter,Kristjan Haule,Subhasish Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高通量eDMFT框架，通过使用物理导出的固定库仑相互作用参数（金属$U=6$ eV，绝缘体$U=10$ eV）并结合精确双计数，在无需材料特定参数调优的情况下，实现了对强关联过渡金属氧化物ABO$_3$的准确光谱性质预测，并与光发射实验高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联过渡金属钙钛矿氧化物对电子结构理论和数据驱动的材料发现构成基础性挑战。DFT+DMFT虽能提供定量准确的描述，但其高通量应用受限于需要确定材料特定的库仑相互作用参数$U$，而cRPA等第一性原理方法预测的$U$值在化学相似的ABO$_3$钙钛矿中表现出高度非线性和不可转移性。

Method: 基于大能量窗口eDMFT框架，利用高度局域化轨道并在同一多体框架内自洽处理电子关联和屏蔽效应。采用物理导出的相互作用值：金属$U=6$ eV，绝缘体$U=10$ eV，并结合精确双计数（exact double counting）。对ABO$_3$化合物（A = Ca, Sr, La; B = V–Ni）进行系统的高通量eDMFT计算，并将光谱函数与光发射实验对比验证。

Result: 在eDMFT框架下，自洽屏蔽的库仑相互作用对于关联金属和绝缘体自然落在相对狭窄的范围内。使用固定$U$参数的高通量eDMFT计算得到的光谱函数与光发射实验总体吻合极好。

Conclusion: 电荷自洽eDMFT能够实现无需参数调优的高通量多体计算，为强关联材料建立预测性电子结构数据库提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Strongly correlated transition-metal perovskite oxides pose a fundamental challenge for electronic-structure theory and for large-scale, data-driven materials discovery. While DFT+DMFT provides a quantitatively accurate description of such systems, its high-throughput application is hindered by the need to determine material-specific Coulomb interaction parameters ($U$). First-principles approaches such as the cRPA predict a highly nonlinear and non-transferable evolution of the interaction strength across chemically similar ABO$_3$ perovskites. Here we show that this paradigm does not extend to the large-energy-window eDMFT, which employs highly localized orbitals and treats electronic correlations and screening self-consistently within the same many-body framework. As a result, spectral properties are governed primarily by the dynamical self-energy rather than by static interaction-induced energy shifts. Recent constrained-eDMFT calculations demonstrated that, for broad classes of $3d$ transition-metal oxides, the self-consistently screened Coulomb interactions naturally fall within relatively narrow ranges for correlated metals and insulators. Motivated by these findings, we implement a high-throughput eDMFT framework employing physically derived interaction values of $U=6$ eV for metals and $U=10$ eV for insulators together with $exact$ double counting. We test this framework using systematic high-throughput eDMFT calculations for ABO$_3$ compounds (A = Ca, Sr, La; B = V--Ni) and benchmark the resulting spectral functions against photoemission experiments, where we find overall excellent agreement. Our results establish that charge self-consistent eDMFT enables robust, parameter-tuning-free high-throughput many-body calculations for correlated oxides, opening a practical pathway toward predictive electronic-structure databases for strongly correlated materials.

</details>


### [117] [Finite-Temperature Spin Exchange-Correlation Kernel of the Uniform Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16888)
*Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用变分图解蒙特卡洛方法，计算了均匀电子气在金属密度下从量子简并到经典区间的静态自旋交换关联核，发现其与零温参数化结果平滑连接，高温下费米面自旋关联结构被抑制，并揭示了当前热局域自旋密度近似在温稠密区域的残余误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀电子气的有限温自旋响应是自旋极化/磁化电子液体（包括温稠密物质）的基础参考，但目前其约束程度远低于电荷响应，缺乏第一性原理的精确计算。

Method: 采用变分图解蒙特卡洛（variational diagrammatic Monte Carlo）方法，计算未极化均匀电子气在金属密度下（跨越量子简并、温稠密和经典区域）的静态自旋交换关联核 K_{xc}(q;T)。

Result: 1）低温和零温自旋响应参数化平滑连接；2）升温抑制费米面尺度的自旋关联结构并削弱Stoner增强；3）长波极限对热LSDA参数化中自旋刚度的检验显示低温一致但温稠密区存在残余误差；4）经典区自旋XC核在费米动量尺度上近乎局域，与电荷XC核形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 这些结果为有限温自旋响应理论和磁化温稠密物质建模提供了第一性原理基础。

Abstract: The finite-temperature spin response of the uniform electron gas (UEG) is a fundamental reference for spin-polarized and magnetized electron liquids, including warm dense matter (WDM), yet it remains far less constrained than charge response. Using variational diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we compute the static spin exchange--correlation (XC) kernel $K_{xc}(q;T)$ of the unpolarized UEG at metallic densities across the quantum-degenerate, warm-dense, and classical regimes. The kernel connects smoothly to zero-temperature spin-response parametrizations at low temperature, while heating suppresses the Fermi-surface-scale spin-correlation structure and weakens the XC-driven Stoner enhancement. Its long-wavelength limit provides a direct response test of the spin stiffness implied by thermal local-spin-density-approximation (LSDA) parametrizations, showing low-temperature consistency while exposing a resolved warm-dense residual in current LSDA parametrizations. In the classical regime, the spin XC kernel becomes nearly local on the Fermi-momentum scale, in sharp contrast to the corresponding charge XC kernel. These results provide a first-principles basis for finite-temperature spin-response theory and magnetized WDM modeling.

</details>


### [118] [Specific heat and susceptibility of S=1/2 antiferromagnets on square, triangular, and kagome lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17420)
*Chisa Hotta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用正弦平方变形（SSD）方法研究二维自旋-1/2反铁磁体的热力学性质，通过构造体态有效模拟热力学极限，发现三角晶格和kagome晶格反铁磁体在低温比热中呈现肩峰或双峰结构，且kagome晶格在极低温下磁化率显著增强，表明其低能激发主要由磁激发主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维自旋-1/2反铁磁体（特别是受挫体系如三角晶格和kagome晶格）的热力学性质随温度的变化，期望通过SSD方法在有限尺寸计算中有效逼近热力学极限，揭示不同晶格体系中低能激发的本质差异。

Method: 采用正弦平方变形（SSD）技术，对哈密顿量施加实空间包络函数，使局域能量尺度在边界处平滑降至零；SSD哈密顿量的量子本征态在系统中心表现出体态行为，从而在小尺寸计算中有效模拟热力学极限；利用这些赝体态计算能量密度、比热和磁化率随温度的变化。

Result: 三角晶格和kagome晶格反铁磁体在低温比热中均呈现肩峰或明显的双峰结构；kagome晶格在极低温度范围内磁化率显著增强；与正方晶格和一维情况的对比表明，尽管两个受挫体系都保留了相当数量的熵，但kagome晶格在~0.5J以下的低能激发主要由磁激发态主导，而三角晶格则不然。

Conclusion: SSD方法能有效模拟热力学极限，揭示了kagome晶格反铁磁体的低能物理以磁激发为主导，而三角晶格反铁磁体的低能激发中磁成分较弱，两种受挫体系在低温热力学行为上存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study the temperature dependence of the thermodynamic properties of spin-1/2 antiferromagnets on two-dimensional lattices. Our analysis employs the sine-square deformation (SSD), in which a real-space envelope function is applied to the Hamiltonian so that the local energy scale is smoothly reduced to zero at the system boundaries. The quantum eigenstates of the SSD Hamiltonian exhibit bulk-like behavior near the system center, effectively mimicking the thermodynamic limit even in small finite-size calculations. Using these fictitious bulk states, we compute the energy density, specific heat, and magnetic susceptibility as functions of temperature. We find that both the triangular- and kagome-lattice antiferromagnets show either a shoulder or a pronounced double-peak structure in the low-temperature specific heat, whereas the kagome case particularly shows a strong enhancement of magnetic susceptibility down to the lowest temperature range. These direct comparisons, together with the square-lattice and one-dimensional cases, reveal that although both frustrated systems retain a substantial amount of entropy, the low-energy excitations below ~ 0.5J of the kagome lattice are predominantly governed by the magnetic excited states, whereas not much for the triangular lattice.

</details>


### [119] [Partial Kondo Screening Solves the Mystery of Rare Earth Tetraborides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17469)
*Soumyaranjan Dash,Sanjeev Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于Kondo格子模型在Shastry-Sutherland晶格上的混合与半经典蒙特卡洛模拟，该工作发现了1/6至3/4多个分数磁化平台，揭示了动能、Kondo耦合与磁受挫三方竞争导致的局域Kondo屏蔽新机制，为稀土四硼化物R B₄长期未解的超磁性与异常磁输运现象提供了简洁解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释稀土四硼化物(RB₄)中观测到的多个磁化平台以及异常磁输运现象的微观机制，解决长期存在的超磁性谜题。

Method: 采用混合蒙特卡洛与半经典蒙特卡洛方法，在Shastry-Sutherland晶格上求解Kondo格子模型(KLM)。

Result: 发现了1/6、2/9、1/4、1/3、1/2、2/3和3/4饱和磁化强度的稳健磁化平台；多数平台态呈现部分Kondo屏蔽特征；成功解释了ErB₄和TmB₄中异常的磁输运行为。

Conclusion: 提出了由动能、Kondo耦合与磁受挫三方竞争调控的新机制，为R B₄体系中的磁化平台与异常磁输运提供了简洁而优雅的理论解，并为在受挫Kondo晶格中预测和发现新的关联物相开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We invoke a new mechanism to account for multiple magnetization plateaus observed in rare-earth tetraborides. Using a combination of hybrid and semiclassical Monte Carlo simulations of the Kondo lattice model (KLM) on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice (SSL), we find robust magnetization plateaus at fractions 1/6, 2/9, 1/4, 1/3, 1/2, 2/3 and 3/4 of the saturation magnetization. We find that most of the plateau states are partially Kondo screened and emerge from the field-tuning of a complex three-way competition between the kinetic energy, the Kondo coupling, and the magnetic frustration. Most remarkably, the unusual magneto-transport reported in ErB$_4$ and TmB$_4$ admits an unexpectedly simple explanation within our mechanism. This work not only provides an elegant and simple solution to the long-standing puzzle of metamagnetism and anomalous magnetotransport in RB$_4$, but also introduces a novel mechanism to predict and discover new correlated phases in frustrated Kondo lattices.

</details>


### [120] [Finite-frequency anomaly-induced electromechanical response of Dirac fermions in deformed graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17632)
*Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了石墨烯变形产生的几何电子-声子顶点，其中一种顶点充当声子规范场，可与电磁矢量势耦合到相同的狄拉克流。研究发现移动的形变可产生横向电流，形变模式可束缚电荷。响应系数是质量狄拉克锥的奇偶宇称流-流关联函数，绝缘锥的系数为单锥Chern-Simons值，掺杂锥在局域区域被贝里曲率因子m/|μ|压低。具体应用包括：行波弯曲波产生横向二次谐波电流；静态波纹与动态声子混合产生驱动频率下的横向电流；非共线模式可产生电荷调制。对于子晶格带隙石墨烯，两个谷相加而非抵消。实验上可观测到弯曲波频率两倍的横向电信号，其相位由子晶格带隙符号固定，栅压依赖从带隙平台交叉到1/|μ|衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索石墨烯变形产生的几何电子-声子耦合（特别是声子规范场）如何导致机电响应，揭示形变如何直接产生电荷输运和电荷调制，并给出可实验验证的清晰预测。

Method: 从低能狄拉克理论出发，识别出形变产生的几何电子-声子顶点，其中一种充当声子规范场。通过计算奇偶宇称流-流关联函数推导机电响应系数，分析绝缘锥和掺杂锥的不同行为。对具体形变模式（行波弯曲波、静态波纹+动态声子、非共线模式）应用响应理论，并考虑自旋和谷求和。

Result: 移动形变可产生横向电流，形变模式可束缚电荷。绝缘锥响应系数为单锥Chern-Simons值，掺杂锥被贝里曲率因子m/|μ|压低。行波弯曲波产生横向二次谐波电流；静态波纹+动态声子产生驱动频率横向电流；非共线模式产生电荷调制。子晶格带隙石墨烯中两谷相加。实验信号为弯曲波频率两倍的横向电信号，相位由带隙符号固定，栅压依赖从带隙平台到1/|μ|的交叉。

Conclusion: 建立了变形石墨烯中由反常诱导的机电响应通道，给出了方向、相位、频率和栅压选择规则，这些规则可作为该异常诱导效应的干净实验检验。

Abstract: A deformation of a graphene sheet changes more than the positions of the atoms. In the low-energy Dirac theory it also produces geometric electron-phonon vertices. One of these vertices acts as an emergent phonon gauge field, $\calA_μ$, which couples to the same Dirac current as the electromagnetic vector potential. This shared current vertex gives a direct route from mechanics to electronics: a moving deformation can generate a transverse electric current, and a deformation pattern with emergent phonon flux can bind electric charge. We show that the coefficient of this mixed electromechanical response is the parity-odd current-current correlator of a massive Dirac cone. For an insulating cone the coefficient is the one-cone Chern-Simons value, while for a doped cone in the local regime it is reduced by the Berry curvature factor $m/|μ|$. We apply the response to explicit deformations. A traveling flexural wave generates a transverse second-harmonic current; a static ripple mixed with a dynamic phonon generates a transverse current at the drive frequency; and two non-collinear modes can generate charge modulation through the emergent phonon flux. We keep the spin and valley sum explicit, so the paper shows when the one-cone anomaly becomes a charge current in graphene and when it instead appears in a valley, spin, or spin-valley channel. For sublattice-gapped graphene with a valley-odd deformation gauge coupling, the two valleys add rather than cancel. The experimentally sharp signature is a transverse electrical signal at twice the flexural-wave frequency, with a phase fixed by the sign of the sublattice gap and a gate dependence that crosses over from a gap plateau to a $1/|μ|$ decay. These direction, phase, frequency, and gate-voltage selection rules give clean tests of the anomaly-induced electromechanical channel in deformed graphene.

</details>


### [121] [Graph-Theoretic Detection of Hilbert Space Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17951)
*A. Rutkowski,M. Mierzejewski,J. Herbrych*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出基于谱图理论的无偏方法，用于识别量子多体系统中的希尔伯特空间碎片化现象，并通过图拉普拉斯谱、Fiedler向量和模块度等工具，将精确碎片化对应为图的不连通分量，近碎片化对应为弱连接社区，在t-J模型和Hubbard链中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特空间碎片化是强关联量子系统中一种重要的遍历性破缺机制，但现有识别方法通常依赖对守恒律的先验知识或模型特定的解析洞察，缺乏通用的、无偏的诊断工具。

Method: 将多体希尔伯特空间的基态视为图的顶点，哈密顿量矩阵元视为边，从而将希尔伯特空间的连通结构映射为图。利用谱图理论中的拉普拉斯谱、Fiedler向量和模块度等工具分析图的连通性：精确碎片化对应图的不连通分量，近碎片化表现为谱上可分辨的弱连接社区。

Result: 在一维t-J模型及其微扰中，图论诊断指标可靠地识别了碎片化和近碎片化希尔伯特空间结构，捕捉了支配系统演化的动力学时间尺度层级。将该方法应用于Hubbard链，揭示了与双子动力学和自旋构型相关的近解耦子空间的出现。

Conclusion: 谱图分析是一种通用的、可扩展的工具，可用于诊断复杂量子多体系统中的碎片化现象和近似动力学约束，无需依赖模型特定的先验知识。

Abstract: Hilbert-space fragmentation provides a mechanism for ergodicity breaking in quantum many-body systems even in the absence of disorder, leading to dynamically disconnected sectors and strong memory of initial conditions. However, identifying such structures is often challenging and typically relies on prior knowledge of conservation laws or model-specific analytical insight. Here we introduce an unbiased approach based on spectral graph theory and, within this framework, formulate the concept of nearly fragmented systems, in which perturbative processes couple otherwise fragmented sectors while preserving their dynamical imprint. By representing basis states as vertices and Hamiltonian matrix elements as edges, we map the connectivity structure of the many-body Hilbert space onto a graph and analyze it using tools such as the Laplacian spectrum, Fiedler vectors, and modularity. Exact fragmentation corresponds to disconnected graph components, while nearly fragmented systems manifest as weakly connected communities whose structure can still be resolved spectrally. Applying this framework to the one-dimensional $t$-$J$ model and its perturbations, we demonstrate that graph-theoretic diagnostics reliably identify both fragmented and nearly fragmented Hilbert-space structures and capture the hierarchy of dynamical time scales that governs the system's evolution. We further show that the method extends beyond kinetically constrained models by applying it directly to the Hubbard chain, where it reveals the emergence of nearly decoupled subspaces associated with doublon dynamics and spin configurations. Our results establish the spectral graph analysis as a general and scalable tool for diagnosing fragmentation and approximate dynamical constraints in complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [122] [Exact Organization of Density Matrices and Entanglement Structure in the Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18002)
*Chen-Chih Wang,Yi-Ping Huang,Sungkit Yip*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文给出了自旋-1/2 Kitaev量子自旋液体密度矩阵的精确形式，并揭示了其基于规范结构和1-form Wilson对称性的对称性分辨块对角结构，该结构导致纠缠谱的简并性，并将纠缠熵可分离为规范部分和物质部分，还扩展到了奇数格点子系统，发现了纠缠谱与费米子宇称的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子自旋液体中，尤其是在Kitaev蜂窝模型的自旋框架下，密度矩阵的精确形式及其纠缠结构尚不明确，需要严格的理论分析来揭示其背后的规范结构和对称性对纠缠的贡献。

Method: 通过自旋算子表示Kitaev自旋液体的密度矩阵，利用弦算子的等价类组织密度矩阵，结合精确的高斯定律和1-form Wilson对称性，分析约化密度矩阵的对称性分辨块对角结构，并推广到奇数格点子系统。

Result: 发现密度矩阵具有由规范结构决定的对称性分辨块对角结构，导致纠缠谱具有广泛简并性；纠缠熵可分离为规范部分和物质部分；奇数格点子系统下纠缠谱与费米子宇称存在关联。

Conclusion: Kitaev模型中的纠缠结构由规范对称性和1-form对称性主导，纠缠熵的分离性源于块对角结构，且费米子宇称在奇数子系统纠缠谱中扮演关键角色。

Abstract: We give an exact form of the density matrix of the spin-1/2 Kitaev spin liquid represented in terms of spin operators and study the entanglement structures of the Kitaev honeycomb model within the spin framework. We show that the density matrix is naturally organized by equivalence classes of string operators associated with the underlying gauge structure of the model. With the explicit form of the density matrix, plus the exact Gauss law of the emergent gauge theory and the exact 1-form Wilson symmetry in the Kitaev model, we demonstrate the existence of the underlying symmetry-resolved block-diagonal structure of the reduced density matrix, which gives rise to the extensive degeneracy in the entanglement spectrum. The block-diagonal structure is then proven to be responsible for the separability of the entanglement entropy into the gauge and matter parts. Furthermore, we extend the formalism to subsystems with an odd number of lattice sites, revealing a relation between the entanglement spectrum and the fermion parity that is seldom mentioned in the literature.

</details>


### [123] [Taming the 3D Wilson-Fisher Fixed Point via Nonlocal Effective Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18148)
*Hyeon Jung Kim,Seung-Jong Yoo,Jinmo Bok,Lemuel John Sese,Semin Park,Ki-Seok Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非局域有效作用量假设的重整化群框架，通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换解耦三维相对论性φ⁴理论的强耦合动力学。非局域传播子允许Δ_φ和Δ_φ作为完全独立的动力学变量，在三环阶自能和顶点涨落计算中，非局域性驱动了多环涨落间的精确结构交叉抵消。求解闭式双变量主方程得到鲁棒的非平庸物理固定点，其动态指数与量子蒙特卡洛和共形自举基准高度一致，成功消除了传统局域假设固有的系统截断误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局域作用量假设在处理三维φ⁴理论的强耦合动力学时存在系统性的截断误差，无法同时正确描述静态标度和热力学流。需要一种新的RG框架来克服局域处理的局限性，实现更精确的临界指数计算。

Method: (1) 基于非局域有效作用量假设构建RG框架；(2) 通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换将四阶相互作用解耦为主场φ和辅助场φ~φ²的系统；(3) 允许两个标度指数Δ_φ和Δ_φ作为完全独立的动力学变量；(4) 进行三环阶的自能和顶点涨落计算；(5) 求解闭式双变量主方程得到固定点。

Result: 得到非平庸物理固定点Δ_φ*≈0.981，Δ_φ*≈0.415；运动学反常维度η_φ≈0.038；能量算符维度Δ_{φ²}≈1.417；热关联长度指数ν≈0.6317，与高精度QMC和共形自举基准定量吻合。

Conclusion: 非局域自由度的释放成功消除了传统局域作用量假设的系统截断误差，同时解决了Wilson-Fisher普适类的静态标度和热力学流问题。

Abstract: We present a novel Renormalization Group (RG) framework based on a nonlocal effective action ansatz to tame the strong coupling dynamics of the three-dimensional relativistic $φ^{4}$ theory. By implementing a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation, we decouple the quartic interaction into a system of the primary field $φ$ and an auxiliary field $\varphi \sim φ^2$. Rather than freezing the intermediate scaling dimensions, the nonlocality of our effective action allows both exponents $Δ_φ$ and $Δ_{\varphi}$ to act as fully independent, unconstrained dynamical variables.This nonlocal propagator framework plays a critical role in the RG flow: evaluating self-energies and vertex fluctuations up to the three-loop order, the nonlocality drives precise structural cross-cancellations among multi-loop fluctuations near the Gaussian limit. Solving the resulting closed two-variable master equations isolates a robust, non-trivial physical fixed point at $Δ_φ^{*} \approx 0.981$ and $Δ_{\varphi}^{*} \approx 0.415$. These dynamic exponents yield a kinematic anomalous dimension $η_φ \approx 0.038$, an energy operator dimension $Δ_{φ^2} \approx 1.417$, and-via mass deformation-a thermal correlation length exponent $ν\approx 0.6317$, demonstrating exceptional quantitative agreement with high-precision Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) and conformal bootstrap benchmarks. Our results rigorously confirm that unfreezing the nonlocal degrees of freedom successfully eliminates the systematic truncation errors inherent to conventional local ansatz treatments, simultaneously resolving both the static scaling and thermodynamic flows of the Wilson-Fisher universality class.

</details>


### [124] [Pressure Effects on CeMnSi: Evolution of Ce 4f and Mn 3d Electronic States and Negative Thermal Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18230)
*Sae Nishiyama,Haruka Mima,Jun-ichi Hayashi,Keiki Takeda,Chihiro Sekine,Yoshiya Uwatoko,Hiroki Takahashi,Hiroshi Tanida,Yukihiro Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了压力对重费米子反铁磁体CeMnSi的影响，发现Mn的反铁磁序在1.3 GPa被抑制，并在~97 K出现压力诱导的新异常；低压区出现非费米液体行为，5.7 GPa以上呈现更金属化的行为；环境压力下的负热膨胀支持重费米子基态的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究压力对重费米子反铁磁体CeMnSi中Mn磁态、重费米子态稳定性以及电输运性质的影响。

Method: 利用电阻率测量和粉末X射线衍射，在不同压力下对CeMnSi进行研究。

Result: 1) Mn反铁磁序在P_c~1.3 GPa被抑制；2) 在~97 K出现压力诱导的新异常T_M，且随压力升高而升高；3) 0.7 GPa附近出现近T线性的非费米液体行为；4) P_s~5.7 GPa以上电阻率显著降低，呈现更金属化行为；5) 环境压力下40 K以下存在负热膨胀。

Conclusion: 压力可调控Mn的磁态并影响重费米子态的稳定性；负热膨胀为CeMnSi中重费米子基态的存在提供了支持证据。

Abstract: We investigated pressure effects on the nontrivial heavy-fermion antiferromagnet CeMnSi by means of electrical resistivity and powder X-ray diffraction. With increasing pressure, the antiferromagnetic order of Mn (T_N ~ 240 K at ambient pressure) is rapidly suppressed and disappears at P_c ~ 1.3 GPa. Instead, a pressure-induced anomaly appears at T_M ~ 97 K and shifts to higher temperatures with increasing pressure. The switching of the Mn magnetic state may reflect a modification of the magnetic symmetry of the system, which could influence the stability of the heavy-fermion state. In the low-pressure region, non-Fermi-liquid-like behavior characterized by nearly T-linear resistivity is observed around 0.7 GPa. In addition, the resistivity shows a marked reduction below T_M and a qualitative change toward more metallic behavior above the structural transition pressure P_s ~ 5.7 GPa. At ambient pressure, CeMnSi exhibits negative thermal expansion below ~40 K, which is absent in LaMnSi, supporting the formation of a heavy-fermion ground state.

</details>


### [125] [Frustration from Localized Zhang-Rice States: A Unified Theory of Doping-Driven Magnetic Transitions in Cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18453)
*Xiaodong Wang,Ping Xu,Jiong Mei,Shao-Hang Shi,Zi-Xiang Li,Mingpu Qin,Kun Jiang,Hui-Ke Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出空穴掺杂在铜氧化物中形成局域化Zhang-Rice单态，并通过引入新的超交换相互作用（J₂和J₃）产生磁阻挫，从而解释了反铁磁序的快速坍塌和自旋玻璃相的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解掺杂空穴破坏铜氧化物反铁磁序的微观机制是领域内的基本问题，现有理论将掺杂空穴简单视为非磁性空位，无法充分解释实验观察到的Néel反铁磁序快速坍塌及电子-空穴掺杂不对称性。

Method: 提出统一微观理论：掺杂空穴形成空间局域化的Zhang-Rice单态，这些局域态主动介导出次近邻（J₂）和第三近邻（J₃）的超交换相互作用，而非仅作为非磁性空位存在。

Result: 该掺杂诱导的交换路径产生了显著的磁阻挫，自然解释了Néel反铁磁序的快速坍塌以及在空穴掺杂侧自旋玻璃相的出现。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解轻掺杂铜氧化物中掺杂驱动的复杂磁相变和磁学电子-空穴不对称性提供了全面的理论解释。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism by which doped holes disrupt the antiferromagnetic order is one of the fundamental questions in cuprates. In this work, we propose a unified microscopic theory in which doped holes form spatially localized Zhang-Rice singlets which actively mediate emergent spin exchange. Rather than acting as simple non-magnetic vacancies, these localized states introduce emergent next-nearest $J_2$ and third-nearest $J_3$ neighbor superexchanges. This dopant-induced exchange pathway generates significant magnetic frustration, naturally explaining the rapid collapse of the Néel AFM order and the emergence of a spin-glass phase on the hole-doped side. Our findings provide a comprehensive framework for understanding the complex doping-driven magnetic phase transitions and magnetic electron-hole asymmetry in lightly doped cuprates.

</details>


### [126] [Interlayer electronic coherence links magnetism and superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18524)
*Feiyang Liu,Lixing Chen,Enkang Zhang,Ying-Jie Zhang,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度六端子直流输运测量，系统研究了双层和三层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐单晶的面内与面外电阻率，揭示了强电子各向异性、层间输运相干-非相干 crossover，并发现超导转变温度与电阻率各向异性呈反相关，表明层间电子相干性是影响镍酸盐磁性、密度波有序和超导电性的关键组织参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) 镍酸盐中电子维度对磁性和超导电性的影响机制尚不明确，亟需高精度的本征输运测量来澄清。

Method: 采用六端子几何结构，在高质量双层和三层RP镍酸盐单晶上自洽地同时测定本征面内电阻率 ($ρ_\parallel$) 和面外电阻率 ($ρ_\bot$)，并引入高压实验研究超导转变温度的变化。

Result: (1) 双层和三层镍酸盐均表现出强本征电子各向异性；(2) $ρ_\bot$ 呈现非单调温度依赖，表明层间输运存在普遍性相干-非相干 crossover；(3) 高压下最大超导转变温度 ($T_c$) 与环境压力下电阻率各向异性呈反相关；(4) $ρ_\bot$ 对磁性和密度波有序比 $ρ_\parallel$ 敏感得多，表现出显著异常信号。

Conclusion: 层间电子相干性是关联RP镍酸盐磁性、密度波有序和超导电性的关键组织参数，为镍酸盐高温超导微观理论提供了严格的约束。

Abstract: The extent to which electronic dimensionality influences magnetism and superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates remains unsettled. Here we report high-precision crystallographic-axis-resolved dc transport measurements on high-quality single crystals of bilayer and trilayer RP nickelates. Using a six-terminal geometry, we self-consistently determine the intrinsic in-plane ($ρ_\parallel$) and out-of-plane ($ρ_\bot$) resistivities on the same crystal, while minimizing uncertainties associated with current redistribution in highly anisotropic conductors. We uncover strong intrinsic electronic anisotropy in both bilayer and trilayer nickelates, in contrast to the weak anisotropy inferred from conventional four-probe measurements. Moreover, $ρ_\bot$ exhibits a nonmonotonic temperature dependence, revealing a universal coherent-to-incoherent crossover in interlayer transport. Across the RP nickelate series, the maximum superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$) observed under pressure is inversely correlated with the ambient-pressure resistivity anisotropy, suggesting that stronger interlayer electronic coherence is favorable for superconductivity. In addition, $ρ_\bot$ serves as an exceptionally sensitive and selective probe of magnetic and density-wave orders, exhibiting pronounced anomalies, whereas only weak signatures are observed in $ρ_\parallel$. Our results highlight interlayer coherence as a key organizing parameter that both tracks the relevant magnetic correlations and is closely tied to superconductivity, providing stringent constraints on microscopic theories of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in nickelates.

</details>


### [127] [Charge order on a triangular lattice with Mott physics and arbitrary charge density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18575)
*Aleksey Alekseev,Agnieszka Cichy,Konrad Jerzy Kapcia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究三角晶格扩展Hubbard模型中的电荷有序现象，发现了包含钉球液相（PL）、变阶相变、强粒子-空穴不对称等丰富特征的相图，并通过简单平均场近似（MFA）和原子极限模型较好地解释了DMFT结果，同时DMFT还揭示了MFA未能预测的中间金属相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格体系因阻挫和强关联效应而备受关注，本文旨在通过研究扩展Hubbard模型中的电荷有序现象，揭示其丰富的相图特征和物理机制。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究三角晶格扩展Hubbard模型，考虑最近邻格点间相互作用，并辅以简单平均场近似（MFA）和原子极限模型进行比较分析。

Result: 获得了包含大量特征、相和相变的丰富相图，包括电荷转移驱动和Mott局域化驱动的钉球液相（PL）、变阶相变（从连续到不连续）、强粒子-空穴不对称性，以及DMFT发现的电子掺杂侧的小区域中间金属相。

Conclusion: MFA和原子极限模型能够较好地预期DMFT相图的主要特征并帮助解释相图，但DMFT也揭示了MFA未能预测的新特征（如中间金属相），表明DMFT对于完整描述三角晶格系统的电荷有序现象至关重要。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice systems attract a lot of attention due to various frustration-induced and strongly correlated effects. Here, we focus on the charge-ordering phenomenon by means of investigation of the extended Hubbard model with dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). By considering the intersite nearest-neighbor interaction we have found a very rich phase diagram that contains large number of features, phases, and phase transitions. Among them are pinball-liquid (PL) phases where we distinguish charge-transfer-driven and Mott-localization-driven PLs; phase transitions that change their order as model parameters evolve (from discontinuous to continuous); very strong particle-hole asymmetry. Various features of the phase diagram are found to be better understood by means of the simple mean-field approximation (MFA). Moreover, besides helping with interpretation of the phase diagram, the MFA results together with results for the atomic-limit model are found to be able to set rather good expectations on how the DMFT phase diagram should look like. Nevertheless, a few features were not expected and are found within the DMFT, such as a small-region intermediate metallic phase on an electron-doped side of the phase diagram.

</details>

### [128] [Search for the nonresonant production of a pair of additional Higgs bosons in the Type-X two-Higgs-doublet model in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16655)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用138 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，搜寻通过Z玻色子产生的双额外希格斯玻色子衰变至$\tau$轻子对的过程，未发现超出标准模型背景的偏差，排除了Type-X双希格斯二重态模型作为缪子反常磁矩理论-实验差异解释的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Type-X双希格斯二重态模型是否能为缪子反常磁矩（$g-2$）的理论值与实验值之间的潜在张力提供解释。

Method: 利用CMS探测器采集的138 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，搜索通过离壳Z玻色子产生的两个额外希格斯玻色子（$Z^* \to HA$）并衰变至$\tau$轻子对（$H/A \to \tau^+\tau^-$）的信号过程。

Result: 未观测到超出标准模型背景的显著信号偏差，在Type-X两希格斯二重态模型对准场景下设定了排除限。

Conclusion: 该结果排除了Type-X双希格斯二重态模型作为缪子反常磁矩实验值与理论值之间潜在张力的解释。

Abstract: A search is presented for the production of two additional Higgs bosons from an off-shell Z boson, where both additional particles decay to $τ$ lepton pairs. The search is performed with a data sample collected with the CMS detector from proton-proton collisions at the LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. No deviation from the standard model background is observed. Exclusion limits are set on the Type-X two-Higgs-doublet model alignment scenario. These results rule out this model as an explanation to the potential tension between the experimental and theoretical values of the muon anomalous magnetic moment.

</details>


### [129] [Global Fit of KamLAND Data and the Daya Bay Antineutrino Energy Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16915)
*Guihong Huang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个弱依赖于反应堆中微子通量模型的全局分析框架，使用大亚湾实验独立测量的铀和钚裂变反中微子谱结合KamLAND公开数据，发现当用大亚湾实测谱替代Huber-Müller模型后，质量平方差Δm²₂₁和混合角tan²θ₁₂的最佳拟合值均下降，与JUNO最新测量结果更一致，表明反应堆反中微子谱的差异可能是两个实验间张力的重要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUNO实验测量的太阳中微子振荡参数与KamLAND 2013年结果存在差异，同时短基线反应堆实验在5 MeV附近观测到反中微子谱与Huber-Müller模型预测的显著偏差，需要探究这些谱偏差对反应堆中微子振荡参数测量的影响。

Method: 构建弱依赖于反应堆反中微子通量模型的全局分析框架，基于大亚湾实验独立测量的²³⁵U和²³⁹Pu裂变反中微子谱，结合KamLAND公开数据，先用Huber-Müller模型复现KamLAND 2013结果，再用大亚湾实测谱替换模型进行联合分析。

Result: 使用Huber-Müller模型成功复现KamLAND 2013结果至0.1σ内；使用大亚湾实测谱后，Δm²₂₁从7.53⁺⁰·¹⁷₋₀.₁₆×10⁻⁵ eV²降至7.50⁺⁰·¹⁹₋₀.₁₈×10⁻⁵ eV²，tan²θ₁₂也呈下降趋势，与JUNO最新测量更一致。

Conclusion: 反应堆反中微子谱的模型差异可能是导致KamLAND与JUNO之间张力的重要原因，使用实测谱可改善两者一致性。

Abstract: Recently, the JUNO experiment published its measurement of the solar neutrino oscillation parameters $Δm^2_{21}$ and $\sin^2θ_{12}$ based on 59 days of data, with central values differing by $0.2σ$ from those released by the KamLAND experiment in 2013. Meanwhile, short-baseline reactor neutrino oscillation experiments such as Daya Bay, RENO, and Double Chooz have observed significant deviations between the measured antineutrino spectrum and the Huber-Müller model prediction around 5~MeV. To further investigate the impact of these deviations on the measurement of reactor neutrino oscillation parameters, we construct a global analysis framework that is weakly dependent on the reactor antineutrino flux model. This framework is based on the independently measured $^{235}\mathrm{U}$ and $^{239}\mathrm{Pu}$ fission antineutrino spectra from the Daya Bay experiment, combined with the public data from KamLAND. First, using the Huber-Müller model, we successfully reproduce the KamLAND 2013 results to within $0.1σ$. Then, replacing the Huber-Müller model with the Daya Bay measured antineutrino spectra in a combined analysis, we find that the best-fit value of the mass-squared difference $Δm^2_{21}$ decreases from $7.53^{+0.17}_{-0.16}\times10^{-5}\,\mathrm{eV^2}$ to $7.50^{+0.19}_{-0.18}\times10^{-5}\,\mathrm{eV^2}$, while the best-fit value of the mixing angle $\tan^2θ_{12}$ also shows a decreasing trend. This result is in better agreement with the latest JUNO measurement, suggesting that differences in the predicted reactor antineutrino spectra may be an important cause of the tension between the two experiments.

</details>

Zheng,X. R. Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,X. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约27.12亿ψ(3686)事件，首次观测到η_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0强子衰变，并发现分支比显著依赖于η_c(1S)与非η_c(1S)过程的干涉模式。在相消和相长干涉情景下，分支比分别测定为(2.59±0.14±0.44)×10^{-3}和(1.18±0.12±0.21)×10^{-3}。对于h_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变未观测到显著信号，设定90%置信度上限为1.02×10^{-4}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次通过ψ(3686)的辐射跃迁过程寻找并观测η_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0强子衰变，以及探测h_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变，以检验量子色动力学（QCD）在粲偶素强子衰变中的理论预言，特别是干涉效应对分支比测量的影响。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10^6 ψ(3686)事件样本，通过ψ(3686)→γη_c辐射跃迁过程研究η_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变；通过ψ(3686)→π^0 h_c强子跃迁过程研究h_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变。采用最大似然拟合方法提取信号产额，并考虑η_c(1S)与非η_c(1S)过程间的干涉效应。

Result: 首次观测到η_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变信号。在相消干涉情景下分支比为(2.59±0.14(stat)±0.44(syst))×10^{-3}，在相长干涉情景下为(1.18±0.12(stat)±0.21(syst))×10^{-3}。h_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0未观测到显著信号，90%置信度上限为1.02×10^{-4}。

Conclusion: 首次实现了η_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变的观测，揭示了干涉效应对分支比测量值有显著影响，这为理解粲偶素衰变中的QCD动力学提供了重要实验约束。对h_c→Σ^0\bar{Σ}^0衰变的上限设定也为相关理论模型提供了限制。

Abstract: Using $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6~ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the hadronic decay $η_{c}\toΣ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}$ is observed for the first time via the radiative transition from $ψ(3686)$. It is found that the branching fraction has a significant dependence on the interference pattern between $η_c(1S)$ and non-$η_c(1S)$ processes. They are determined to be $\displaystyle\mathcal{B}(η_c(1S) \to Σ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}) = (2.59 \pm 0.14(stat) \pm 0.44(syst)) \times 10^{-3}$ and $(1.18 \pm 0.12(stat) \pm 0.21(syst)) \times 10^{-3}$, for the destructive and constructive interference scenarios, respectively. No significant signal is observed for the decay $h_{c}\toΣ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}$ in the hadronic transition $ψ(3686)\toπ^0h_{c}$, and an upper limit on its branching fraction is set to be $1.02\times 10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level.

</details>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Boundary Value Problems on $p$-Adic Analytic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16590)
*Patrick Erik Bradley*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了p-adic解析流形上边界值问题的最新进展，特别是利用标架丛构造的新型坐标Laplacian，用于构建椭圆算子并解决相关的Dirichlet问题，推广了p-adic n-空间紧子域上的结果，最后展望了数论应用及向超度量解析流形的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展p-adic解析流形上的边界值理论，建立扩散过程与p-adic分析之间的联系，并推广已有结果到更一般的流形设置中。

Method: 利用标架丛（frame bundles）在p-adic解析n-流形上构造新型坐标Laplacian，进而构建椭圆算子，并在此基础上系统阐述和求解相关的Dirichlet问题。

Result: 成功构造了p-adic解析流形上的坐标Laplacian和椭圆算子，并解决了相关Dirichlet问题，将p-adic n-空间紧子域上的结果推广到了更一般的流形。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅推广了已有结果，还为数论应用以及向更广泛的超度量解析流形扩展提供了前景方向。

Abstract: An account is given on newest developments on $p$-adic boundary value problems on $p$-adic analytic manifolds and their relationship with diffusion. In particular, novel coordinate Laplacians on $p$-adic analytic $n$-manifolds constructed with the help of frame bundles, are introduced in this context. These are used to construct elliptic operators. Related Dirichlet problems are formulated and solved, generalising results on compact subdomains of $p$-adic $n$-space. In the end, an outlook towards number-theoretic applications as well as extensions of this theory to ultrametric analytic manifolds is given. This is a substantial upgrade of the presentation given at Branko's 80-th Birthday Conference in Belgrade, May 2025.

</details>


### [132] [Factorization of Additive Polynomials and van der Geer--van der Vlugt curves in characteristic 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16729)
*Tetsushi Ito,Daichi Takeuchi,Takahiro Tsushima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过加法多项式分解的方法，给出了特征2下van der Geer--van der Vlugt曲线的Frobenius特征值的新公式。该公式比先前工作更简洁，并用于构造极大和极小曲线，同时研究了曲线的周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作中给出的Frobenius特征值公式依赖于许多选择，导致公式复杂。为了简化公式并便于显式计算，作者试图寻找一种新的方法。

Method: 采用加法多项式分解（factorization of additive polynomials）的方法，取代之前通过考虑曲线适当商的方法，推导出新的Frobenius特征值公式。

Result: 得到了一个更简单且便于显式计算的公式；基于该公式给出了构造极大和极小van der Geer--van der Vlugt曲线的方法，并证明每条这类曲线都能由此构造得到；计算了若干例子并研究了它们的周期。

Conclusion: 新公式优于旧公式，不仅简化了理论表达，还在构造极大/极小曲线和周期计算等实际应用上具有重要价值。

Abstract: In our previous work, we gave a formula for the Frobenius eigenvalues of van der Geer--van der Vlugt curves in characteristic 2 by considering suitable quotients of the curve. Although the formula is explicit, it depends on many choices, which makes the formula complicated. In this article, we take a different approach using a factorization of additive polynomials, and prove a new formula. The resulting formula is simpler and is useful for explicit computations. As applications, we provide a method for constructing maximal and minimal van der Geer--van der Vlugt curves, and show that every such curve arises from this construction. We also compute various examples of van der Geer--van der Vlugt curves and study their periods.

</details>


### [133] [Explicit Formulas for the Casimir Eigenvalues of $SL(n,\mathbb{Z})$-Maass Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16803)
*Vishal Muthuvel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为SL(n,ℤ)上的Maass形式的Casimir算子特征值提供了基于Langlands参数的显式公式，并通过有向边序图的图论方法完成了证明，其中m=2时还原了经典的Laplace特征值公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Maass形式的Casimir算子特征值与Langlands参数建立显式联系，填补该方向的理论空白，并为m=2的特殊情形还原已有结果。

Method: 采用图论方法，将GL(n,ℝ)上m阶基本微分算子的作用与具有m条边、至多m个顶点的有向边序图的划分相联系，从而推导出特征值公式。

Result: 给出了任意1 ≤ m ≤ n下Casimir算子D_{m,n}关于Maass形式φ的特征值公式，该公式由φ的Langlands参数表达；当m=2时，该公式还原为Terras的Laplace特征值公式。

Conclusion: 成功建立了Casimir算子特征值与Langlands参数之间的显式关系，图论方法为相关问题的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Maass forms for $SL(n,\mathbb{Z})$ are defined to be eigenfunctions of the Casimir operators $\mathcal{D}_{m,n}$ of orders $1 \leq m \leq n$ for $GL(n,\mathbb{R})$. For any $1 \leq m \leq n$ and Maass form $φ$ for $SL(n,\mathbb{Z})$, we provide a formula for the eigenvalue of $\mathcal{D}_{m,n}$ associated with $φ$ in terms of the Langlands parameters of $φ$. In the case $m=2$, we recover the formula for the Laplace eigenvalue of a Maass form due to Terras, the Casimir differential operator of order $2$ being the Laplacian. Our proof takes a graph-theoretic approach, relating the action of every elementary differential operator of order $m$ for $GL(n,\mathbb{R})$ to the partitions of a directed, edge-ordered graph with $m$ edges and at most $m$ vertices.

</details>


### [134] [Non-admissibility of some universal supersingular representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17836)
*Zachary Feng,Heejong Lee,Ray Li,Vaughan McDonald,Nischay Reddy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究$K/\mathbf{Q}_p$非分歧扩域上$\mathrm{GL}_n(k)$的不可约表示$\sigma$对应的万有超奇异表示。对于$n\ge 3$且$\sigma$充分一般并满足某些技术条件时，证明了该表示非容许且具有无限长度，推广了$n=2$和$K/\mathbf{Q}_p$非平凡有限扩张时的结果。方法上采用权重循环论证和Serre权重猜想的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将$\mathrm{GL}_n$上$p$进局部Langlands纲领中关于权重$\sigma$的万有超奇异表示的非容许性和无限长度性质从$n=2$情形推广到更高维数$n\ge 3$。

Method: 权重循环论证（weight cycling argument）结合Serre权重猜想的最新进展。

Result: 对于$n\ge 3$，当$\sigma$充分一般并满足某些技术条件时，万有超奇异表示是非容许的且具有无限长度。

Conclusion: 将$\mathrm{GL}_2$上已知结果推广至$\mathrm{GL}_n$ ($n\ge 3$)，进一步揭示了$p$进局部Langlands纲领中高维情形下超奇异表示的结构复杂性。

Abstract: Let $K/\mathbf{Q}_p$ be an unramified extension of degree $f$ with residue field $k$. Let $σ$ be an irreducible representation of $\mathrm{GL}_n(k)$ over $\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p$. For $n\ge 3$, we prove that the universal supersingular representation of weight $σ$ is non-admissible and of infinite length when $σ$ is sufficiently generic and satisfies certain technical conditions. This generalizes the previous results for $n=2$ and a non-trivial finite extension $K/\mathbf{Q}_p$. Our method employs a weight cycling argument together with recent progress on the Serre weight conjectures.

</details>


### [135] [One-level densities of large even and odd orthogonal families of automorphic L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17012)
*Vorrapan Chandee,Xiannan Li,Micah B. Milinovich*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在广义黎曼假设(GRH)下，研究了级为q的原始形式的L-函数的一级密度，其中q在无平方因子数上平均。分别处理了偶正交族和奇正交族，并将测试函数傅里叶变换的支撑集扩展到(-3,3)。该扩展支撑集在GRH条件下，为这些L-函数族及其导数在中心点的非消失性提供了目前最强的已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究L-函数族在中心点的分布性质，特别是其一阶密度统计量，并期望通过扩展测试函数支撑集来获得更强的非消失性结果。

Method: 在广义黎曼假设(GRH)条件下，针对级为q的原始形式L-函数，在无平方因子数q上取平均，分别对偶正交族和奇正交族进行一级密度分析，并将测试函数傅里叶变换的支撑集扩展到(-3,3)。

Result: 成功将测试函数傅里叶变换的支撑集扩展到(-3,3)，在GRH条件下得到了目前已知最强的非消失性结果。

Conclusion: 扩展支撑集至(-3,3)能够获得这些L-函数族及其导数在中心点处最强的已知非消失性结论，但该结论依赖于广义黎曼假设。

Abstract: We prove one-level density results for L-functions attached to primitive forms of level q, averaged over square-free q, conditional on the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH). We treat the even and odd orthogonal families separately and extend the support of the Fourier transform of the test function to (-3,3). This extended support yields the strongest known non-vanishing results for these families of L-functions and their derivatives at the central point, conditional on GRH.

</details>


### [136] [Gaps of Binary Numerical Semigroups and of Binary Inclusion-Exclusion Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17157)
*Gennady Bachman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文分析了模p线性置换u·n (mod p)中“主导对”(dominant pair)的性质，并将其应用于二项包含-排除多项式Q_{p,q}的间隙集以及数值半群⟨p,q⟩中相邻元素距离的完整描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模p线性置换中主导对的性质，并将其应用于二项包含-排除多项式（包括二项分圆多项式Φ_{pq}作为特例）的间隙集描述，以及数值半群⟨p,q⟩中相邻元素距离的完整刻画。

Method: 定义并分析了模p线性置换中“主导对”(dominant pair)的概念，通过不等式条件刻画其性质，然后将这些性质应用于两个具体问题。

Result: (i) 完整描述了二项包含-排除多项式Q_{p,q}的间隙集（包括二项分圆多项式Φ_{pq}作为特例）；(ii) 完整描述了数值半群⟨p,q⟩中相邻元素之间的所有可能距离。

Conclusion: 通过对线性置换中主导对性质的分析，成功解决了两类相关组合数论问题，获得了完全的分类描述。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a given modulus, let $u$ be prime to $p$, and consider the linear permutation $u\cdot n\pmod p$ of the residue system modulo $p$. Writing $\langle x\rangle_p$ to denote the least nonnegative residue of $x$ modulo $p$, we say that a pair of integers $(a,b)$ is a dominant pair of this permutation if either the inequality $\max(\langle ua\rangle_p,\langle ub\rangle_p)<\min_{a<n<b}\langle un\rangle_p$, or the inequality $\min(\langle ua\rangle_p,\langle ub\rangle_p)>\max_{a<n<b}\langle un\rangle_p$ hold. The main technical part of this work gives analysis of this property of linear permutations of residue systems. We then apply this analysis to the problems that motivated it, and give (i) complete description of the gapsets of binary inclusion-exclusion polynomials $Q_{\{p,q\}}$ (which include binary cyclotomic polynomials $Φ_{pq}$ as its principal special case), and (ii) complete description of all possible distances between consecutive elements of a numerical semigroup $\langle p,q\rangle$.

</details>


### [137] [Counterexamples to integer-coefficient criteria for recurrence along functions from a Hardy field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17529)
*Kangbo Ouyang,Leiye Xu,Shuhao Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Bergelson、Moreira和Richter关于Hardy域函数递归的两个问题给出了否定答案。针对特定函数对 \(f_1(t)=t^{3/2}\) 和 \(f_2(t)=\lambda t^{3/2}+t\) (\(\lambda\notin\mathbb{Q}\))，证明了所有 \(F\in\nablaz(f_1,f_2)\) 满足 \(\lim_{t\to\infty}|F(t)|\in\{0,\infty\}\)，但存在正自然密度集 \(E\) 使得 \(R_{f_1}(E)\cap R_{f_2}(E)\) 是分段syndetic而非厚的。进一步证明即使在整数导数跨度条件下，公共返回时间集也可能为空，否定了联合交截性蕴含递归结论的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Bergelson、Moreira和Richter提出的关于Hardy域函数沿函数递归的两个开放问题。

Method: 利用初等Bohr集构造反例，通过分析特定函数对 \(t^{3/2}\) 和 \(\lambda t^{3/2}+t\) 的递归性质进行证明。

Result: (1) 整数系数导数跨度条件不蕴含厚度；(2) 即使在整数导数跨度条件下，公共返回时间集可能为空，即联合交截性不蕴含递归结论。

Conclusion: 对Bergelson-Moreira-Richter提出的两个问题给出了否定答案，揭示了Hardy域函数递归性质的复杂性。

Abstract: We give negative answers to two questions of Bergelson, Moreira, and Richter concerning recurrence along functions from a Hardy field. For the pair \(f_1(t)=t^{3/2}\) and \(f_2(t)=λt^{3/2}+t\), where \(λ\in\mathbb R\setminus\mathbb Q\), singled out in their integer-coefficient derivative-span question, we prove that every \(F\in\nablaz(f_1,f_2)\) satisfies \(\lim_{t\to\infty}|F(t)|\in\{0,\infty\}\). Nevertheless, there is a set \(E\subset\mathbb N\) of positive natural density such that \(R_{f_1}(E)\cap R_{f_2}(E)\) is piecewise syndetic and not thick. Thus the proposed integer-coefficient replacement does not imply thickness.
  We further show that, even under the same full integer derivative-span condition, the common return-time set may be empty.
