This stronger obstruction also gives a negative answer to their question asking whether the recurrence conclusion of Theorem A follows from joint intersectivity of the integer polynomials in \(\operatorname{poly}(f_1,\ldots,f_k)\). The constructions use elementary Bohr sets.

</details>


### [138] [A Degree-Two Hilbert--Pólya Realisation by Causal Riemann-Surface Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17645)
*Kejun Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在朗兰兹二次度框架下，构造了局部欧拉因子版本的Hilbert–Polya实现。算子为J-自伴解析束，谱参数定义在平方根函数的Riemann曲面上。标准情形N=2时，谱曲线为椭圆曲线E₀: y² = x³ + 8x（导子256），通过显式离壳基点从预解式中恢复L(E₀,s)的局部欧拉因子。该形式通过封闭二次匹配系统扩展到椭圆曲线族，得到朗兰兹二次度下的局部Hilbert–Polya实现。结构判别式恒等式Δ_p + D_p = 4p²在规范参数点(τ,δ,Δ)=(2,0,2)成立。论文还证明了λ偶性定理、闭式识别了Pontryagin指数，并记录了该构造无法下降至zeta(s)的障碍。对一般N，谱曲线亏格g_N = (N-1)²。结果是椭圆L函数的有限、局部、逐素对应，而非全局Hilbert–Polya实现。J-自伴约束使椭圆匹配成立，但也阻断了向zeta(s)的下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现朗兰兹二次度下椭圆L函数局部欧拉因子的Hilbert–Polya型谱实现，即通过J-自伴解析束的预解式恢复局部L因子，建立有限、局部、逐素的对应关系。

Method: 构造J-自伴解析束，谱参数定义在平方根函数的Riemann曲面上；采用分数核作为因果响应的Laplace变换，利用KK解析性产生支点覆盖；通过封闭二次匹配系统将标准N=2情形扩展到椭圆曲线族；利用Hasse–Weil界保证基点的实性。

Result: 标准N=2情形：谱曲线为椭圆曲线E₀: y² = x³ + 8x（导子256），通过离壳基点从预解式恢复局部欧拉因子；结构判别式恒等式Δ_p + D_p = 4p²在(τ,δ,Δ)=(2,0,2)成立；证明了λ偶性定理并闭式识别了Pontryagin指数；一般N下谱曲线亏格g_N = (N-1)²。

Conclusion: 建立了椭圆L函数局部欧拉因子的有限、局部、逐素对应，但J-自伴约束使该构造无法下降至zeta(s)的全局Hilbert–Polya实现。

Abstract: We study a local Euler-factor version of the Hilbert--Polya idea at Langlands degree two. The operators are J-self-adjoint analytic pencils whose spectral parameter lives on the Riemann surface of the square-root function. The fractional kernel is the Laplace transform of a causal response, so KK analyticity forces the branch-point cover on which the RSCO pencil lives. For the N=2 canonical case, the spectral curve is the elliptic curve E_0: y^2 = x^3 + 8x (LMFDB 256b2, conductor 256) and the local Euler factors of L(E_0,s) are recovered by an explicit off-shell basepoint in the resolvent. The basepoint is real because the Hasse--Weil bound supplies the needed inequality. The formalism extends to a family of elliptic curves through a closed-form quadratic matching system, giving a local Hilbert--Polya-type realisation at Langlands degree two. A structural discriminant identity Delta_p + D_p = 4p^2 holds at the canonical parameter point (tau,delta,Delta)=(2,0,2). The paper also proves a lambda-evenness theorem, identifies the Pontryagin index in closed form, and records obstructions to descending the construction to zeta(s). For general N, the spectral curve genus g_N equals (N-1)^2. The result is a finite, local, prime-by-prime correspondence for elliptic L-functions, not a global Hilbert--Polya realisation. The J-self-adjoint constraint that makes the elliptic matching work also blocks descent to zeta(s).

</details>


### [139] [On the finite transcendence of Frobenius traces for abelian varieties over $\mathbb{Q}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17674)
*Yuto Tsuruta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了椭圆曲线（非CM情形）以及多种主极化阿贝尔簇在Q上的Frobenius迹的有限超越性，推广了Luca-Zudilin仅针对CM情形的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Luca-Zudilin关于CM椭圆曲线Frobenius迹有限超越性的结果推广至更一般的非CM椭圆曲线及主极化阿贝尔簇情形。

Method: 利用Luca-Zudilin的方法，并将其应用于非CM椭圆曲线和更一般的主极化阿贝尔簇上。

Result: 给出了非CM椭圆曲线以及多种主极化阿贝尔簇在Q上的Frobenius迹的有限超越性。

Conclusion: 成功将Frobenius迹的有限超越性从CM椭圆曲线推广到非CM椭圆曲线及更广泛的主极化阿贝尔簇上，拓展了该领域的研究范围。

Abstract: The first purpose of this paper is to give the fnite transcendence of Frobenius traces for elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$ without the assumption of complex multiplication (CM). This result generalizes the previous work by Luca and Zudilin, who obtained similar transcendence results specifically for the CM case. The second purpose is to give the finite transcendence of Frobenius traces for several principally polarized abelian varieties over $\mathbb{Q}$, by using Luca--Zudilin's method.

</details>


### [140] [Multiplicative independence in the sequence of $k$-generalized Pell numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17699)
*Cherif B. Deme,Kancou D. Fall,Khady Faye,Bernadette Faye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了k-广义Pell序列中不同项之间的乘法依赖性，确定了所有使得$P_n^{(k)}$与$P_m^{(k)}$存在乘法依赖关系的$(k,m,n)$组合，并证明这些解仅出现在非常小的参数范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究k-广义Pell序列中不同项之间乘法依赖关系的存在性，这是递推序列乘法性质研究中的一类基本问题。

Method: 利用Matveev关于对数线性形式的下界估计、Baker-Davenport约化算法以及计算搜索相结合的方法进行证明。

Result: 对于$k\ge2$，完全刻画了满足$n>m\ge0$且$P_n^{(k)}$与$P_m^{(k)}$乘法依赖的所有三元组$(k,m,n)$，这些解只出现在很小的参数范围内。

Conclusion: k-广义Pell序列中乘法依赖的情形极为罕见，仅存在于小参数情形，该结论完善了对此类递推序列乘法结构的认识。

Abstract: We study multiplicative dependence between terms of the $k$-generalized Pell sequence $(P_n^{(k)})_{n\ge 2-k}$, defined by the linear recurrence \[ P_n^{(k)} = 2P_{n-1}^{(k)} + P_{n-2}^{(k)} + \dots + P_{n-k}^{(k)}, \] with initial conditions $P_0^{(k)} = \dots = P_{-(k-2)}^{(k)} = 0$ and $P_1^{(k)} = 1$. For $k\ge 2$ we determine all pairs $(m,n)$ with $n>m\ge 0$ such that $P_n^{(k)}$ and $P_m^{(k)}$ are multiplicatively dependent. The main result states that the only solutions occur for very small $k,m,n$ (which are listed explicitly). The proof uses lower bounds for linear forms in logarithms (Matveev), the Baker-Davenport reduction algorithm, and a computational search.

</details>


### [141] [Linear independence of periods related to polylogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17756)
*Makoto Kawashima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了代数数域上多重多对数线性无关的判别准则，通过构造适用于多重多对数的Padé型逼近器，得到了代数数域上不同点处多对数乘积线性无关的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立代数数域上多重多对数值线性无关性的首个判别准则，填补该领域理论空白，并推广至多对数乘积情形。

Method: 基于为多重多对数量身定制的Padé型逼近器的显式构造方法。

Result: 获得了代数数域上不同点处多对数乘积线性无关性的新结论。

Conclusion: 成功建立了代数数域上多重多对数线性无关的首个判别准则，并推广到乘积情形。

Abstract: This paper provides the first criteria for the linear independence of multiple polylogarithm values over algebraic number fields. In particular, we derive novel results regarding the linear independence of products of polylogarithms at distinct points over an algebraic number field. Our approach is based on the explicit construction of Padé-type approximants tailored for multiple polylogarithms.

</details>


### [142] [An update on the Linnik--Goldbach and Romanov problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17825)
*Daniel R. Johnston,Tim Trudgian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在广义黎曼假设下证明，所有大偶数可表示为两个素数加6个2的幂之和；同时无条件证明超过25%的奇数可表示为素数加2的幂之和，更新了Romanov常数的已知上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Linnik-Goldbach问题，即用两个素数与固定数量的2的幂之和表示所有大偶数；同时改进Romanov常数的下界，探索奇数的素数加2的幂表示问题。

Method: 在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下分析Linnik-Goldbach问题，将所需2的幂数量降至6个；通过解析数论方法无条件更新Romanov常数的下界至超过25%。

Result: 在GRH下，所有大偶数可表示为两个素数加6个2的幂；无条件证明超过25%的奇数可表示为素数加2的幂。

Conclusion: 改进了两个经典数论问题的已知结果：降低了Linnik-Goldbach问题在GRH下所需2的幂数量，并提升了Romanov常数的下界。

Abstract: We consider the Linnik--Goldbach problem of writing all large even integers as the sum of two primes and a fixed number of powers of 2. We show that, under the generalised Riemann hypothesis, one can use 6 powers of two. In addition, we update the best known bounds on Romanov's constant, showing unconditionally that more than $25\%$ of odd numbers can be written as the sum of a prime and a power of 2.

</details>


### [143] [A note on the $q$-adic valuation of $σ_k(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17872)
*Olivier Bordellès*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对奇数素数q，获得了σ_k(n)的q-adic valuation精确公式，并推导出比Zhao先前结果渐近更优的上界，主要利用LTE引理和分圆多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Zhao给出的上界在n较大且k≥q-2时不够紧，需要更精确的估值公式来改进上界估计。

Method: 利用LTE引理（提升指数引理）和分圆多项式（cyclotomic polynomials）作为关键技术工具，推导σ_k(n)在奇素数q下的q-adic valuation精确表达式。

Result: 获得了σ_k(n)的q-adic valuation精确公式，并由此导出一个显式上界，该上界在n较大且k≥q-2时渐近优于Zhao先前的结果。

Conclusion: 通过LTE引理和分圆多项式的结合使用，成功改进了除数幂和函数在奇素数模下的p-adic估值上界。

Abstract: In this note, we obtain an exact formula for the $q$-adic valuation of $σ_k(n)$ where $q$ is an odd prime, allowing us to derive an explicit upper bound which is asymptotically better than the previous bound obtained by Zhao when $n$ is large and $k \geqslant q-2$. The key parts are played by the LTE lemma and the use of cyclotomic polynomials.

</details>


### [144] [Decay of weighted cusp counts for congruence subgroups of $SL_2$ over number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17972)
*Shengyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上SL₂的同余子群，证明了加权尖点总数与群指数之比被同余级范数的负幂次所界，推广了Cox-Parry在有理数域上的定理，支持尖点项在拓扑、算术和表示论公式中为次主导的启发式观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Cox-Parry在有理数域上关于同余子群尖点计数的结果推广到一般数域，并验证尖点项在相关公式中为次主导项这一启发式猜想。

Method: 通过在素数处局部化，将问题约化到有限商群上的计数问题；主要技术部分是在有限非约化主局部环上对SL₂的子群进行计数分析，采用类似加性组合学的论证方法。

Result: 证明了加权尖点总数与群指数之比被同余级范数的负幂次所界定。

Conclusion: 该结果支持了尖点项在拓扑、算术和表示论公式中为次主导项的启发式观点。

Abstract: For congruence subgroups commensurable with $\operatorname{SL}_2$ over number fields, we study cusp counts with certain multiplicities. We prove that the ratio of the total weighted cusp count to the group index is bounded by a negative power of the norm of the congruence level. This generalizes a theorem of Cox--Parry over $\mathbb Q$, and supports the heuristic that cusp terms occurring in topological, arithmetic and representation-theoretical formulas are subleading.
  The proof proceeds by localizing at a prime and reducing the problem to finite quotients, where it becomes a counting problem for finite groups. The main technical part is a counting problem for subgroups of $\operatorname{SL}_2$ over finite non-reduced principal local rings, proved by an analysis reminiscent of additive combinatorics.

</details>


### [145] [On some constancy of Hecke eigensystems for Drinfeld cuspforms of level $Γ_1(\mathfrak{n}\wp^r)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18016)
*Shin Hattori*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Drinfeld尖形式空间中有限℘-斜率的Hecke本征系统在水平Γ₁(𝔫℘ʳ)中出现当且仅当它在水平Γ₁(𝔫℘)中出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Drinfeld尖形式空间中Hecke本征系统在不同水平下的出现规律，特别是有限℘-斜率的情形。

Method: 通过分析Hecke算子在Drinfeld尖形式空间上的作用，建立水平Γ₁(𝔫℘ʳ)与Γ₁(𝔫℘)之间本征系统的对应关系。

Result: 有限℘-斜率的Hecke本征系统在S_k(Γ₁(𝔫℘ʳ))中出现当且仅当它在S_k(Γ₁(𝔫℘))中出现。

Conclusion: 对于Drinfeld尖形式空间，有限℘-斜率的Hecke本征系统在水平Γ₁(𝔫℘ʳ)上的出现完全由其在较低水平Γ₁(𝔫℘)上的出现所决定。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a rational prime, let $q>1$ be a $p$-power integer, let $\mathbb{F}_q$ be the field of $q$ elements and let $A=\mathbb{F}_q[t]$ be the polynomial ring over $\mathbb{F}_q$. Let $\mathfrak{n}\in A$ be a nonzero element and let $\wp\in A$ be a monic irreducible polynomial of positive degree. Let $k\geq 2$ and $r\geq 1$ be integers. Let $S_k(Γ_1(\mathfrak{n}\wp^r))$ be the space of Drinfeld cuspforms of level $Γ_1(\mathfrak{n}\wp^r)$ and weight $k$. In this paper, we show that a Hecke eigensystem of finite $\wp$-slope appears in $S_k(Γ_1(\mathfrak{n}\wp^r))$ if and only if it appears in $S_k(Γ_1(\mathfrak{n}\wp))$.

</details>


### [146] [On the Periods of Ikeda-Yamana Lift for the Unitary Group I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18294)
*Jin Higashitani*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究完全实域F的二次CM扩域E上的Hermitian模形式提升（I_n）的周期，并将其与Hecke本征形式f的L-函数特殊值建立联系，推广了Katsurada关于Ikeda猜想的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Katsurada关于Ikeda猜想的结果从更一般的情形推广到完全实域的二次CM扩域上的酉群（unitary group）上，建立Hermitian模形式提升的周期与L-函数特殊值之间的关系。

Method: 利用Yamana构造的将权为(w_λ)_λ、水平为1的Hermitian模形式提升到酉群上非分歧自守形式（记为I_n）的方法，通过对Hecke本征形式f计算I_n(f)的内积，将其表达为某类L-函数的特殊值。

Result: 成功将I_n(f)的周期⟨I_n(f), I_n(f)⟩用f的某类L-函数的特殊值表示出来。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了Katsurada关于Ikeda猜想的结果，为Hermitian模形式理论与自守L-函数之间建立了新的联系。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a totally real field and $E$ be a quadratic CM extension field of $F$. Let $n$ be a positive integer. We denote by $\mathrm{I}_n$ the lift of the Hermitian modular form of weight $(w_λ)_λ$ with level 1 to unramified automorphic forms defined on the unitary group constructed by Yamana. We then express the period $\langle{\mathrm{I}_n(f), \mathrm{I}_n(f)}\rangle$ of $\mathrm{I}_n(f)$ for Hecke eigenforms $f$ in terms of special values of some types of $L$-functions of $f$. This is an extension of Katsurada's result concerning Ikeda's conjecture.

</details>


### [147] [Sum of consecutive powers as a perfect power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18348)
*Angelos Koutsianas,Nikos Tzanakis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究丢番图方程 x^k + (x+1)^k = y^n (n≥3) 在 k≡2(mod 4) 条件下的解，证明仅当 x=0 或 -1 时存在解，适用于 6≤k≤100 或 k 的奇素因子皆满足 ≡3(mod 4) 的情形。方法涉及对数线性形式、模方法和Thue方程求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究指数丢番图方程 x^k + (x+1)^k = y^n 在特定模条件下的整数解问题，这类方程与费马大定理、Catalan猜想等经典问题相关，具有数论理论价值。

Method: 综合运用三种数论工具：1) 对数线性形式（Baker理论）估计指数上界；2) 模方法（如Galois表示、模性定理）约化求解空间；3) 将方程转化为Thue方程进行代数数域求解。

Result: 在 k≡2(mod 4) 条件下，当 6≤k≤100 或 k 的所有奇素因子均为 ≡3(mod 4) 的素数时，方程仅有的整数解为 (x,y) = (0, ±1) 或 (-1, ±1)。

Conclusion: 该方程在特定模条件下解的结构被完全刻画，验证了经典数论方法（对数形式+模方法+Thue方程）在复杂指数丢番图问题中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper we study the equation $$
  x^k + (x+1)^k = y^n,\quad n\geq 3, $$ when $k\equiv 2\pmod{4}$. We prove that the only solutions are for $x=0, -1$ when $6\leq k\leq 100$ or for a $k$ with odd prime factors congruent to $3\pmod{4}$. We use linear forms in logarithms, the modular method and the resolution of Thue equations.

</details>


### [148] [Classicality of Hilbert modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18426)
*Yuanyang Jiang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于全实域上Hilbert模簇的完备上同调中出现的球Hecke代数特征，若其关联的Galois表示绝对不可约且为正规平行权的de Rham表示，则该特征是模的。作为应用，证明了全实域上GL₂的Langlands-Clozel-Fontaine-Mazur猜想的新情形。证明方法推广了[Pan26]的方法，计算了几何部分Fontaine算子，并研究了相关Koszul型部分de Rham复形的上同调。关键步骤是建立局部解析Jacquet-Langlands转移，其证明包含多个新成分，包括[DvHKZ26]构造的不同四元数Shimura数据的Igusa栈的比较，以及[Jiang26a]建立的局部解析无限级Shimura簇的Grothendieck-Messing理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将球Hecke代数特征与模性联系起来，证明其关联Galois表示在满足绝对不可约且为正规平行权的de Rham表示时该特征是模的；进而证明全实域上GL₂的Langlands-Clozel-Fontaine-Mazur猜想的新情形。

Method: 推广[Pan26]的方法，计算几何部分Fontaine算子，研究Koszul型部分de Rham复形的上同调；建立局部解析Jacquet-Langlands转移，包括比较不同四元数Shimura数据的Igusa栈，以及应用局部解析无限级Shimura簇的Grothendieck-Messing理论。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下球Hecke代数特征的模性，以及全实域上GL₂的Langlands-Clozel-Fontaine-Mazur猜想的新情形。

Conclusion: 推广了已有方法，结合几何Fontane算子、Koszul型部分de Rham复形以及局部解析Jacquet-Langlands转移等新工具，在Langlands程序相关猜想上取得了新进展。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a totally real number field. We prove that a character of the spherical Hecke algebra appearing in the completed cohomology of Hilbert modular varieties is modular if the associated Galois representation is absolutely irreducible, and de Rham of regular parallel weights. As an application, we prove some new cases of the Langlands-Clozel-Fontaine-Mazur conjecture of $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over totally real fields.
  For the proof, we generalize the method in [Pan26], calculate geometric partial Fontaine operators, and study the cohomology of the associated Koszul-type partial de Rham complexes. The key step is the establishment of a locally analytic Jacquet-Langlands transfer, whose proof consists of several novel ingredients including a comparison of Igusa stacks for different quaternionic Shimura data constructed by [DvHKZ26], and the Grothendieck-Messing theory for locally analytic infinite level Shimura varieties established in [Jiang26a].

</details>


### [149] [Three Brillhart-Lehmer-Selfridge primality proofs for Wagstaff numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18555)
*Alexey Dolotov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用经典 $N-1$ 方法（BLS 准则）而非 ECPP，为三个大瓦格斯塔夫素数 $W_{2617}$（788 位）、$W_{10501}$（3161 位）和 $W_{12391}$（3730 位）给出了完全可验证的素性证明。通过分解 $2^{p-1}-1$ 的 cyclotomic 因子，并结合 Cunningham 表与 FactorDB（因子均经再认证）的数据，同时独立验证了 Chua $N+1$ 同余条件。所有余因子由 APR-CL 无条件证明，整个流程完全可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瓦格斯塔夫数是梅森数的自然 $+1$ 对应物，但其素性检验长期依赖 ECPP（Atkin-Morain）或仅具充分性猜想的 Chebyshev/Lucas 型检验。对于 $p \geq 2617$ 的情况，缺乏独立于 ECPP 且完全严格的素性证明。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用 Brillhart-Lehmer-Selfridge (BLS) $N-1$ 准则，利用 cyclotomic 分解 $2^{p-1}-1 = \prod_{d \mid p-1} Φ_d(2)$，从 Cunningham 项目表（作证据）和 FactorDB（作发现辅助，因子经再认证）获取因子分解数据。选择 $p-1$ 足够光滑的指数（$p=2617,10501,12391$）。所有余因子由 APR-CL 无条件证明素性。另独立验证 Chua $N+1$ 同余 $\omega_3^{(W_p+1)/2} \equiv -1 \pmod{W_p}$ 作为一致性检查。

Result: 成功给出 $W_{2617}$（788 位）、$W_{10501}$（3161 位）和 $W_{12391}$（3730 位）的完全严格素性证明，独立于 ECPP，且不依赖于 Chebyshev 充分性猜想。发现截至 2026 年 1-4 月的数据中，$p=10501$ 和 $p=12391$ 是 $2617$ 以上仅有的满足 BLS $N-1$ 平滑性门槛的已知瓦格斯塔夫素数/可能素数指数。

Conclusion: 经典 $N-1$ 方法可成功证明大瓦格斯塔夫数的素性，为这些数提供了独立于 ECPP 的验证路径；所有步骤可复现，因子数据可靠，方法具有通用性。

Abstract: The Wagstaff numbers $W_p = (2^p + 1)/3$ for odd primes $p$ are the natural $+1$ companions of the Mersenne numbers. Known primality proofs for $W_p$ with $p \geq 2617$ rely on the elliptic-curve primality proving algorithm of Atkin-Morain; Chebyshev/Lucas-type tests, while available as compositeness criteria, remain conjectural on the sufficiency side. We present fully verified primality proofs of $W_{2617}$ (788 digits), $W_{10501}$ (3161 digits), and $W_{12391}$ (3730 digits), independent of ECPP and relying only on classical $N-1$ machinery. The proofs apply the Brillhart-Lehmer-Selfridge (BLS) $N-1$ criterion to the cyclotomic decomposition $2^{p-1} - 1 = \prod_{d \mid p-1} Φ_d(2)$, harvesting factored content from the Cunningham project tables (used as evidence) and FactorDB (used only as a discovery aid, with every retrieved factor re-certified). As an independent check on the $\mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{2}]$ arithmetic implementation, the Chua $N+1$ congruence $ω_3^{(W_p + 1)/2} \equiv -1 \pmod{W_p}$ -- the $a=3$ case of the Chua framework with $ω_3 = 3 + 2\sqrt{2}$, a necessary condition for Wagstaff primality -- is verified at each $W_p$.
  BLS $N-1$ requires $p-1$ sufficiently smooth that enough cyclotomic factors $Φ_d(2)$ are fully factored. On the factorisation data consulted (Cunningham project tables and FactorDB, January-April 2026), $p = 10501$ and $p = 12391$ are the only exponents above $2617$ in the known Wagstaff prime/probable-prime list meeting this ceiling. Every cofactor primality is certified unconditionally by APR-CL; the method is independent of the Chebyshev sufficiency conjecture, and every step is reproducible from the archived scripts.

</details>


### [150] [Covering systems where the prime divisors of all moduli are only $2$, $3$, or $5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18644)
*Joshua Harrington,Jonah Klein,Joshua Lowrance,Ognian Trifonov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了模数最小公倍数仅含素数2、3、5的互异覆盖系统，完整刻画了最小模数m=2至6时所有正整数四元组(m,a,b,c)的存在性，证明m≤9，并给出一个m=8的具体构造实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找所有正整数四元组(m,a,b,c)使得存在一个最小模数为m、模数最小公倍数为2^a·3^b·5^c的互异覆盖系统，并完整描述这些四元组的存在性条件。

Method: 通过整数规划方法以及一个新的关于同余系统覆盖集合密度的估计来证明覆盖系统的不存在性；对于存在的四元组则给出具体构造示例。

Result: 完整描述了m=2,3,4,5时所有四元组及m=6时除b=c=1外的所有情况；证明了若模数LCM仅含2、3、5作为素因子，则m≤9；构造了一个m=8,a=8,b=3,c=2的互异覆盖系统实例。

Conclusion: 对于给定素因子限制的互异覆盖系统，最小模数有上界9，且可通过整数规划和密度估计等方法判定四元组的存在性。

Abstract: We try to find all quadruples of positive integers $(m,a,b,c)$ with $a \geq b \geq c$ such that there exists a distinct covering system with minimum modulus $m$ and least common multiple of the moduli $2^a 3^b 5^c$. We obtain complete description of all such quadruples when $m=2,3,4,5$, or $6$, except when $m=6$ and $b=c=1$. We also show that if the LCM of the moduli has only $2$, $3$, or $5$ as prime divisors, then $m \leq 9$ and construct a distinct covering system with $m=8$, $a=8$, $b=3$, and $c=2$. When a covering system exists for a quadruple $(m,a,b,c)$ we provide an example. Nonexistence of covering systems is established via integer programming or by using a new estimate on the density of a set covered by a system of congruences.

</details>


### [151] [Linear equations in Piatetski-Shapiro primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18676)
*Xuancheng Shao,Yu-Chen Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了当γ充分接近1时，Piatetski-Shapiro素数集与任意零序列之间的去相关估计，并应用该结果证明了在γ > 1 - 2^{-Ck}条件下，该素数集中存在无穷多个非平凡k项等差数列，显著改进了Li和Pan的三重指数型范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前关于线性或多项式指数相位函数的工作，研究Piatetski-Shapiro素数集与任意零序列之间的去相关性，并应用于线性方程组解计数问题。

Method: 建立Piatetski-Shapiro素数集与任意零序列之间的去相关估计（discorrelation estimates），利用该估计分析有限复杂度线性方程组的解，并通过解析数论方法推导等差数列的存在性范围。

Result: 证明了当γ充分接近1时，Piatetski-Shapiro素数集中任意有限复杂度线性方程组（包括k项等差数列）的解的渐近公式成立；进一步，存在绝对常数C>0，当γ > 1 - 2^{-Ck}时，该素数集中存在无穷多个非平凡k项等差数列。

Conclusion: 将此前Li和Pan的三重指数型范围（即γ需极其接近1）大幅改进为指数型范围1 - 2^{-Ck} < γ < 1，显著放宽了条件。这一结果展示了去相关估计在处理素数集与结构性问题中的强大应用。

Abstract: We establish discorrelation estimates between the Piatetski-Shapiro prime set \[ \mathcal{P}_γ := \{p \text{ is prime and } p = \lfloor n^{1/γ} \rfloor \text{ for some } n \in \mathbb{N}\} \] and arbitrary nilsequences when $γ\in (0,1)$ is sufficiently close to $1$. This extends earlier works which treated linear or polynomial exponential phase functions. As an application, we establish an asymptotic formula for the number of solutions in $\mathcal{P}_γ$ to any "finite-complexity" system of linear equations, including for the number of $k$-term arithmetic progressions in $\mathcal{P}_γ$ up to a threshold $N$ for any given $k \geq 3$. Furthermore, we show that there exists an absolute constant $C>0$ such that if \[ 1 - 2^{-Ck} < γ< 1, \] then the Piatetski-Shapiro primes $\mathcal{P}_γ$ contain infinitely many non-trivial $k$-term arithmetic progressions. This significantly improves upon the previous range of $γ$ obtained by Li and Pan, which is of triple exponential type.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Haptic Rendering of Fractional-Order Viscoelasticity: Passivity and Rendering Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16389)
*Gorkem Gemalmaz,Harun Tolasa,Volkan Patoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对分数阶粘弹性模型在有限记忆离散化下的无源性和渲染性能进行了分析，推导了基于Grunwald-Letnikov导数的分数阶标准线性固体模型的无源性条件，提供了有效刚度和阻尼的符号表达式，并通过实验验证了理论边界及人类感知真实性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性材料的触觉渲染在医疗培训等应用中至关重要，分数阶模型能有效描述时间依赖动力学和记忆效应，但有限记忆离散化下的无源性和渲染性能尚未充分研究。

Method: 基于Grunwald-Letnikov导数的短记忆离散化方法，推导分数阶标准线性固体模型的无源性条件，给出有效刚度和阻尼的符号表达式，并通过实验验证和人类主观评估进行验证。

Result: 建立了统一的无源性条件框架，泛化了整数阶Kelvin-Voigt、Maxwell和SLS模型的结果；实验验证了理论无源性边界，人类评估验证了FO-SLS模型的感知真实性。

Conclusion: 本研究为分数阶粘弹性触觉渲染在短记忆离散化下建立了统一的理论框架和实验评估，证明了分数阶模型在保持无源性基础上可实现更真实的触觉渲染。

Abstract: Haptic rendering of viscoelastic materials that exhibit creep and stress relaxation is crucial for many applications, such as medical training with realistic biological tissue models. Fractional-order viscoelastic models provide an effective means of describing intrinsically time-dependent dynamics with few parameters, as these models can naturally capture memory effects. In this study, we present analyses of passivity and rendering performance for fractional-order viscoelastic models under finite-memory discretization. We derive closed-form expressions to ensure the passivity of haptic rendering with a fractional-order (FO) standard linear solid (SLS) model based on Grunwald-Letnikov derivative under short-memory discretization. We also provide symbolic expressions for the effective stiffness and damping of such FO-SLS models. The resulting passivity conditions constitute a unified framework that generalizes previously reported results for integer-order Kelvin-Voigt, Maxwell, and SLS models, since these results are special cases of the newly derived condition. Furthermore, we provide experimental validations of the theoretical passivity bounds and human-subject evaluations of perceived realism of FO-SLS models. Overall, this study establishes a unified theoretical framework and experimental evaluations for FO viscoelastic rendering under short-memory discretization.

</details>


### [153] [OrbiSim: World Models as Differentiable Physics Engines for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16395)
*Jiajian Li,Jingyuan Huang,Junru Gong,Qi Wang,Xiaokang Yang,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OrbiSim提出了一种全新的机器人仿真范式，将世界模型重新定义为全可微分的物理引擎，实现从场景资产、神经动力学到下游强化学习的端到端可微仿真，在预测保真度和控制性能上显著超越现有世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多局限于隐空间或视觉域的"无约束想象"，缺乏物理基础，无法支持可微接触建模、梯度策略优化等传统仿真难以处理的任务。

Method: 构建统一的物理基础通路，连接结构化场景资产、神经动力学与强化学习；实现涵盖显式状态转换到视觉观测生成的整个仿真循环的端到端可微性。

Result: 在预测保真度和控制性能上显著优于最先进的世界模型；对资产配置和物理参数具有一致的反应性。

Conclusion: OrbiSim有潜力作为可微工具增强机器人仿真与策略训练，特别是在稀疏奖励下的梯度策略优化和直观物理推理等任务中。

Abstract: We present OrbiSim, a novel robotic simulation paradigm that redefines world models as a fully differentiable physics engine for embodied intelligence. Unlike prior world models that focus on unconstrained imagination in latent or visual domains, OrbiSim establishes a unified, physically-grounded pathway that bridges structured scene assets, neural dynamics, and downstream reinforcement learning. By enabling end-to-end differentiability throughout the entire simulation loop -- spanning from explicit state transitions to visual observation generation -- OrbiSim supports tasks traditionally intractable for classical simulators, such as differentiable contact modeling, gradient-based policy optimization under sparse rewards, and intuitive physical inference. Empirical results demonstrate that OrbiSim significantly outperforms state-of-the-art world models in both predictive fidelity and control performance. Furthermore, its consistent responsiveness to asset configurations and physical parameters suggests its potential as a differentiable tool for enhancing robot simulation and policy training.

</details>


### [154] [Support-Safe Variational Hybrid Filtering for Contact-Mode and Sparse-Law Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16398)
*Marios Papamichalis,Regina Ruane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VHYDRO是一种变分混合动力学学习器，通过将学习到的提议与可行转移律混合，防止接触丰富的机器人动力学中的分支丢失，同时联合推断连续潜在状态和离散接触模式，并拟合稀疏端口-哈密顿律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人动力学具有混合特性（自由、冲击、粘滑），标准自适应滤波器无法为可行的接触转移赋予概率，导致真实分支永久丢失。

Method: 提出VHYDRO框架，在每一步将学习到的提议与可行转移律混合后进行采样和重要性加权；联合推断连续潜在状态和离散接触模式，并对每个恢复的模态拟合稀疏端口-哈密顿律。

Result: 在严重遮挡下支持安全滤波器保持可用；在ManiSkill和Sawyer/BridgeData任务上形成时间连贯的接触区域分割，ARI、变化点F1和分割纯度优于基线；在已知方程的混合系统上恢复活跃物理项。

Conclusion: 支持覆盖稳定滤波，滤波稳定化使离散接触后验集中在连贯模态上，模态纯段允许稀疏端口-哈密顿恢复，恢复误差可分解为滤波、导数、模态杂质和物理残差四部分。

Abstract: Contact-rich robot dynamics are hybrid: a single observation can match several latent states and contact regimes (free, impact, stick--slip). A standard amortized filter that places no probability on a feasible contact transition will permanently lose the branch the robot actually follows. We introduce VHYDRO, a variational hybrid dynamics learner that prevents this branch loss. At each step, VHYDRO mixes the learned proposal with a feasible transition law before sampling and importance weighting, ensuring that every transition retained by the model-feasible carrier remains covered. VHYDRO jointly infers a continuous latent state and a discrete contact mode, and fits a sparse port-Hamiltonian law to each recovered regime. On top of this, three guarantees connect: support coverage stabilizes filtering, the stabilized filter concentrates the discrete contact posterior on coherent regimes, and mode-pure segments admit sparse port-Hamiltonian recovery. The recovery error separates cleanly into filtering, derivative, mode-impurity, and physics-residual parts. Three empirical findings track the same mechanism. Under heavy occlusion the support-safe filter stays usable while a non-defensive proposal collapses. On ManiSkill demonstrations and on four Sawyer/BridgeData task families the discrete state forms temporally coherent contact-regime segments that the discrete state yields a stronger joint profile across ARI, change-point F1, and segment purity than post-hoc and mode-free baselines. On hybrid systems with known equations the mode-conditioned sparse fit recovers the active physical terms; purely predictive baselines do not.

</details>


### [155] [SCAR: Self-Supervised Continuous Action Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16412)
*Hongjia Liu,Fan Feng,Minghao Fu,Xinyue Wang,Haofei Lu,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAR框架，通过联合逆向前向动力学模型，从视觉状态变化中学习跨具身的统一潜在动作表示，并在Procgen和Robotwin数据集上证明其作为世界模型的条件接口优于传统具身特定动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作在具身智能中处于核心地位，但现有世界模型难以在跨具身、小样本场景下学习可迁移的动作表示，因为动作通常被视为辅助条件信号而非独立表征因子。

Method: 基于预训练生成骨干，联合逆动力学模型（从观测对推断潜在动作）和前向动力学模型（以潜在动作为条件预测未来状态），并通过KL正则化（约束潜在动作后验接近标准高斯先验）和对抗不变性（抑制具身与环境干扰因素）实现表示可迁移。

Result: 在Procgen和Robotwin数据集上，学习到的统一潜在动作表示作为世界模型的条件接口，比具身特定原始动作带来更优的跨具身低数据适应和跨任务迁移效果。

Conclusion: 动作可以被学习为跨具身的可控变化的共享表示，为更具迁移性和泛化性的世界模型提供统一接口。

Abstract: Despite the central role of action in embodied intelligence, learning transferable action representations from visual transitions remains a fundamental challenge, particularly when world models must generalize across embodiments under limited data. We argue that action is not merely an auxiliary conditioning signal, but a distinct representational factor that decouples the controllable change from embodiment-specific actuation. In this work, we propose SCAR, a joint inverse-forward dynamics framework for learning unified action representations across embodiments from visual transitions. Built on a pretrained generative backbone, SCAR uses an inverse dynamics model (IDM) to infer latent actions from latent observation pairs and a forward dynamics model (FDM) to predict future dynamics conditioned on them. To make the latent space transferable rather than a generic visual bottleneck, we regularize the latent action posterior toward a standard Gaussian prior to limit arbitrary visual encoding, and introduce adversarial invariance to suppress embodiment- and environment-specific nuisance factors. Experiments on the Procgen and Robotwin dataset show that the learned unified latent action representation serves as a stronger conditioning interface for world modeling than embodiment-specific raw actions, yielding improved cross-embodiment low-data adaptation and cross-task transfer. Taken together, these results suggest that action can be learned as a shared representation of controllable change across embodiments, providing an interface for more transferable and generalizable world models.

</details>


### [156] [MR-SLAM: Immersive Spatial Supervision for Multi-Robot Mapping via Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16432)
*Prakash Aryan,Cem Erdogdu,Kavinaya Kumarchokkappan,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-SLAM系统，利用Meta Quest 3头显在混合现实中通过透视图监督三台TurtleBot3机器人的多机器人SLAM任务，实现了实时占用网格合并与空间锚定的状态面板显示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人SLAM操作中，传统2D界面难以有效维护操作员对每台机器人位置和建图状态的空间感知，扩展性差，亟需更直观的交互方式。

Method: 操作员佩戴Meta Quest 3头显，通过带有真实世界遮挡的透视图远程操作三台模拟TurtleBot3机器人；每台机器人运行独立的SLAM Toolbox实例，占用网格在ROS 2后端实时合并，并通过空间锚定仪表板显示建图进度。

Result: 五次9分钟评估中：扫描频率8.83±0.16 Hz，合并占用面积17.9±0.8 m²，跨实例占用一致性94.7±0.5%；额外测试中位变换抖动6.3 ms，41 m²网格覆盖26.7 m²。

Conclusion: MR-SLAM可作为在消费级硬件上结合透视图混合现实监督与多机器人SLAM的参考实现。

Abstract: Operating a multi-robot fleet for simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) in applications such as building inspection or warehouse-aisle monitoring requires the operator to maintain spatial awareness of each robot's position and mapping state, a task that scales poorly on conventional 2D interfaces. We present MR-SLAM, a mixed reality (MR) system in which an operator wearing a Meta Quest 3 headset teleoperates three simulated TurtleBot3 robots through a passthrough view with real-world occlusion, while spatially anchored dashboard panels report mapping progress in situ. Each robot runs an independent SLAM Toolbox instance whose occupancy grid is merged in real time on a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) back end. Across five 9-minute evaluation sessions, the system delivered scans at 8.83 +/- 0.16 Hz, mapped 17.9 +/- 0.8 m^2 of merged occupancy, and reached 94.7 +/- 0.5% cross-instance occupancy consistency across robot pairs. An additional session recorded 6.3 ms median transform jitter and 26.7 m^2 coverage of a 41 m^2 grid. We position MR-SLAM as a reference implementation for combining passthrough mixed reality supervision with multi-robot SLAM on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [157] [Hierarchical Two-Stage Framework for Environment-Aware Long-Horizon Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16442)
*Ganeshaaraj Gnanavel,Tharindu Fernando,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层两阶段框架，结合粗粒度长期预测器与网格感知短期预测器，通过分层融合机制实现长时域船舶轨迹预测。该框架引入可学习Savitzky-Golay平滑层增强时间一致性，并集成环境模块处理洋流、风、浪等海洋参数。在澳洲CTS数据集上，该方法在ADE和FDE指标上分别超越SOTA 25%和17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实海洋条件下长时域船舶轨迹预测对避碰、交通管理和航线规划至关重要，但长程时间依赖和动态环境因素（如洋流、风、浪）使得精确预测极具挑战性。

Method: 提出分层两阶段框架：(1) 粗粒度长期预测器编码整体航行意图；(2) 网格感知短期预测器利用时空图Transformer在离散化海事网格上捕获局部动态；(3) 分层融合机制结合两者；(4) 环境模块通过跨模态注意力和特征级调制整合海面洋流、风向量和有效波高；(5) 可学习Savitzky-Golay平滑层增强融合轨迹的时间一致性。

Result: 在澳洲西北地区CTS数据集上，以3小时输入预测10小时，ADE降低25%，FDE降低17%，超越现有最优方法。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层融合框架有效解决了长时域船舶轨迹预测中远程时间依赖与环境动态性的挑战，显著提升了预测精度。

Abstract: Long-horizon vessel trajectory forecasting under real ocean conditions is critical for collision avoidance, traffic management, and route planning. However, achieving accurate predictions is challenging due to long-range temporal dependencies and dynamic environmental factors such as currents, wind, and waves. To address these issues, we propose a hierarchical two-stage framework that combines a coarse long-term predictor with a grid-aware short-term predictor through a hierarchical fusion mechanism. The short-term branch leverages a Spatio-Temporal Graph Transformer on discretized maritime cells to capture localized dynamics, while the long-term branch encodes overarching navigational intent. An integrated environmental module incorporates oceanographic parameters, including surface currents, wind vectors, and significant wave height, using cross-modal attention and feature-wise modulation for adaptive response to varying sea conditions. Additionally, a learnable Savitzky-Golay smoothing layer enhances temporal coherence in fused trajectories. We evaluate our approach on Australian Craft Tracking System (CTS) data from the North West region, aligned with Copernicus Marine Service products, using a 3-hour input and a 10-hour prediction horizon. Experimental results show that our framework outperforms the state-of-the-art by 25% in Average Displacement Error (ADE) and 17% in Final Displacement Error (FDE). Ablation studies further validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [158] [No Plan, Yet Human: A Reactive Robotics Model Predicts Human Planning Failures on a Clinical Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16514)
*Michael Migacev,Vito Mengers,Antonia Köngeter,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文应用原本为机器人操作设计的反应式梯度下降框架AICON到伦敦塔认知测试中，发现其无需前瞻规划即能复现人类难度排序，且在规划能力受限群体上的表现优于规划基线模型，揭示了人类在规划能力下降时行为向反应式模式转变的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解顺序规划问题为何存在难度差异需要超越平均性能的模型，应能捕捉特定问题的困难模式，并在规划能力受限时以与人类相同的方式失败。

Method: 将AICON（原为机器人操作设计的反应式梯度下降框架）应用于伦敦塔认知测试，在24个问题上比较其与结构任务参数和规划基线的表现，并进行留二交叉验证。

Result: AICON无需前瞻规划即能复现人类精细的难度排序，优于结构任务参数；在规划能力受限群体上优于规划基线，而规划基线更好地捕捉健康对照组；该分离效应与原AICON论文预测一致。

Conclusion: 随着人类规划能力下降，行为向AICON所建模的反应式模式转变；AICON从机器人领域迁移至感知、眼动和顺序规划等生物行为领域，表明其核心抽象反映了生物系统组织方式的某些真实特性。

Abstract: Understanding why some sequential planning problems are harder than others requires models that go beyond average performance. They should capture the specific pattern of which problems are hard, and ideally fail in the same way people do when planning capacity is reduced. We apply AICON, a reactive gradient-descent framework developed for robotic manipulation, to the Tower of London test, a cognitive test used to assess planning in Parkinson's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and stroke. Without any lookahead planning or knowledge of human cognition, AICON reproduces the fine-grained human difficulty ordering across 24 problems better than structural task parameters and generalizes to held-out problems in a leave-two-out evaluation. Crucially, AICON outperforms a planning baseline for groups with reduced planning capacity while the planning baseline better captures healthy controls. This dissociation was predicted by the original AICON paper, which noted that the model's failure modes resemble those of Parkinson's patients who struggle with goal hierarchies but not move counts. This suggests that as planning capacity is reduced, human behavior shifts toward the reactive mode AICON models. The finding extends a broader pattern: AICON, originally built for robotics, now captures aspects of biological behavior across perception, eye movements, and sequential planning, suggesting its core abstraction reflects something real about how biological systems are organized.

</details>


### [159] [A Mechanistic Model for Collective Motion from Sensorimotor Regularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16522)
*Vito Mengers,Bao Duc Cao,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于传感器运动层的集体行为模型，用机器人学框架替代传统自推进粒子模型，通过感知（方位角、表观尺寸）、状态估计和梯度下降控制社交距离，成功复现了极化运动、环状聚集等多种集体行为，并发现行为转变由可测量的生物参数（视场角、感知噪声、转向敏捷度、记忆）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自推进粒子模型虽然能再现集体行为现象，但本质上是描述性的，未回答集体行为如何实际产生。近期实证表明蝗虫并不与邻居对齐，而是通过感觉和认知机制介导交互，因此需要一个基于个体实际感知和执行的机制性模型。

Method: 采用机器人学建模框架，每个智能体通过有限视场内的方位角和表观尺寸感知邻居，维护不确定的内部状态估计，并通过在期望社交距离上的梯度下降选择动作——不使用任何预设的交互力。

Result: 该模型产生了多种集体行为：极化运动、旋转聚集、环状结构和子群分裂。全局灵敏度分析表明，行为转变由传感器运动参数（视场几何、感知噪声、转向敏捷度、记忆）控制。

Conclusion: 集体行为可以理解为交互的传感器运动规律涌现的结果，物种间差异可以理解为身体结构和环境差异的涌现结果。

Abstract: Collective behavior in animals has long been modeled through self-propelled particle models, which reproduce striking group-level phenomena through abstract interaction forces. Yet these models are fundamentally descriptive: they leave open the question of how collective behavior is actually produced. Recent empirical work makes this gap concrete: locusts do not align with neighbors, sensory and cognitive mechanisms mediate interaction instead. A mechanistic model must therefore operate at the sensorimotor level, grounded in what individual organisms can actually perceive, estimate, and physically execute. We present such a model based on a modeling framework from robotics, extended here to collective motion. Each agent perceives neighbors through bearing and apparent-size cues within a limited field of view, maintains uncertain internal state estimates, and selects actions through gradient descent on a desired social distance -- without any prescribed interaction forces. This simple model produces diverse collective behaviors including polarized motion, milling, ring formations, and subgroup fragmentation. A global sensitivity analysis shows that behavioral transitions are governed by sensorimotor parameters corresponding to measurable biological quantities: field of view geometry, sensory noise, turning agility, and memory. Collective behavior can therefore be understood as the emergent outcome of interacting sensorimotor regularities, and differences across species as the emergent outcome of differences in embodiment and environment.

</details>


### [160] [Policy Library CBF: Finite-Horizon Safety at Runtime via Parallel Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16588)
*Taekyung Kim,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出策略库控制障碍函数（PL-CBF），通过并行有限时域 rollout 评估多个备选策略，选择最不保守的安全模式，并利用二次规划最小化修改标称策略以保障安全性。在多个高维动态系统上验证了该方法相比单策略安全滤波器具有更好的安全覆盖率和毫秒级运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型自主系统在非结构化环境中面临在线安全认证的挑战，尤其是在约束条件动态变化的情况下，现有单策略安全滤波器难以保证充分的安全覆盖。

Method: 提出PL-CBF框架：1）维护一个策略库；2）通过并行有限时域rollout评估各策略；3）选择最不具侵入性的安全模式；4）利用二次规划最小化对标称策略的修改以强制执行安全性。

Result: 在平面双积分器（4状态）、高速公路变摩擦驾驶（8状态非线性车辆模型）、3D四旋翼动态拥挤环境导航（12状态）上验证，相比单策略安全滤波器，PL-CBF显著提升安全覆盖率，同时保持毫秒级运行时间。

Conclusion: PL-CBF方法通过策略库和并行rollout实现了更优的安全覆盖与实时性能的平衡，为在线安全认证提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Safety-critical autonomy in unstructured environments poses significant challenges for online safety certification under evolving constraints. We propose Policy Library Control Barrier Function~(PL-CBF), a runtime safety filter that evaluates a library of fallback policies via parallel finite-horizon rollouts, selects the least invasive safe mode, and enforces safety by solving a quadratic program that minimally modifies a nominal policy. We provide a theoretical analysis based on a finite-horizon language metric over closed-loop behaviors, characterizing policy-library coverage requirements for certifying finite-horizon safety. Simulations on a planar double-integrator (4 states), highway driving with abrupt friction changes using a realistic nonlinear vehicle model (8 states), and 3D quadrotor navigation in crowded dynamic environments (12 states) demonstrate improved safety coverage over single-policy safety filters while retaining millisecond-level runtime.

</details>


### [161] [DriveSafer: End-to-End Autonomous Driving with Safety Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16737)
*Shounak Sural,Raj Rajkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveSafer是一个面向端到端生成式规划器的安全框架，通过训练时安全约束和推理时安全引导，在NAVSIM基准上将灾难性故障（PDMS=0）减少48%，可行驶区域合规故障减少65%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式端到端规划器在安全关键场景中仍存在大量灾难性故障，这些故障源于违反物理约束和安全要求。现有方法主要关注提升平均规划质量，而非专门减少灾难性故障。

Method: 提出DriveSafer框架，包含两个核心组件：训练时的安全约束（training-time safety constraints）和推理时的安全引导（inference-time safety guidance），显式地将生成式规划器引导至安全行为。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上，与DiffusionDrive相比：灾难性故障（PDMS=0）减少48%，可行驶区域合规故障降低超过65%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性减少灾难性规划故障而非提升平均规划质量，可以显著提升端到端自动驾驶的安全性。

Abstract: End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving models have shown growing capability in recent years, with performance improving on increasingly challenging benchmarks. However, modern generative E2E planners still suffer from a substantial number of catastrophic failures in safety-critical scenarios. We find that many such failures arise from violations of physical constraints and safety requirements, leading to unsafe behavior. Motivated by this finding, in this paper, we focus on improving safety outcomes in generative end-to-end driving with a targeted reduction of catastrophic planning failures, instead of enhancing average planning quality. Towards this end, we propose DriveSafer, a failure-aware safety framework for end-to-end planners. DriveSafer explicitly steers generative planners towards safe behaviors leveraging both training-time safety constraints and inference-time safety guidance. Compared to the state-of-the-art DiffusionDrive model, on the NAVSIM benchmark, DriveSafer reduces the number of catastrophic failures (PDMS=0) by 48%, with over 65% reduction in drivable-area compliance failures.

</details>


### [162] ["I'm Not Mad, Just Focused'': Understanding Human Emotions in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16816)
*Seung Chan Hong,Dana Kulić,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的情绪识别系统，通过上下文理解提升人机协作中的情绪识别能力。实验表明，该系统在语义相似性和积极情绪对齐上优于传统CNN方法，且用户更偏好由其驱动的情绪自适应机器人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRC中的情绪识别模型依赖表演数据集和单模态输入（如面部表情），导致在真实协作场景中表现不佳，亟需更鲁棒且具上下文理解能力的ER系统。

Method: 提出VLM-ER系统，利用视觉语言模型进行上下文感知的情绪识别；先在现有HRC数据集上评估语义和情感相似性，再在服务机器人协作配送任务的用户研究中验证情绪自适应行为的效果。

Result: VLM-ER系统相比CNN基线在语义相似性和积极情绪对齐上表现更优；用户研究显示参与者更偏好该系统驱动的情绪自适应机器人行为。

Conclusion: VLM-ER系统通过上下文理解有效提升了HRC中的情绪识别效果，且情绪自适应行为能改善用户体验和人机协作质量。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) can benefit from robots' abilities to interpret human emotional states. However, current emotion recognition (ER) models in HRC often fall short, particularly due to their reliance on acted datasets and single-modality inputs like facial expressions. We propose a novel vision language model (VLM)-based ER system that leverages contextual understanding to improve emotion interpretation in HRC. We first evaluate the VLM-ER system by assessing its semantic and sentiment similarity with human annotations on an existing HRC dataset. Then, in a user study with a service robot in a collaborative delivery task, we evaluate the effects of modulating the robot's behaviour based on the user's emotional state inferred by the VLM-ER system. The results show that the proposed VLM-ER system achieves higher semantic similarity and positive sentiment alignment with human annotations compared to a baseline convolutional neural network-based system. Further, participants in the user study preferred emotion-adaptive robot behaviour facilitated by the VLM-ER system.

</details>


### [163] [Pedestrian-Aware LLM-Driven Behavioral Planning for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16858)
*Aidana Baimbetova,Haruki Yonekura,Hamada Rizk,Hirozumi Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动驾驶决策框架，将场景观测转化为自然语言推理提示，使LLM能够推断行人意图、预测风险并生成谨慎的战术驾驶决策。在SUMO仿真中，该框架在零样本评估中达到68%的无碰撞成功率（远超深度强化学习基线的17.7%），在少样本情景记忆下提升至96.0%，并展现出可解释、类人的决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自动驾驶控制系统在结构化交通中表现良好，但难以泛化到不可预测的行人交互和分布外场景，且依赖手工设计的奖励函数和黑箱决策，限制了其在行人人流密集的安全关键环境中的适用性。

Method: 将结构化场景观测转换为自然语言推理提示，输入LLM使其推断行人意图、评估风险并生成战术驾驶决策，再由运动规划器执行以生成平滑、运动学可行的控制信号。

Result: 零样本评估无碰撞成功率68%（DRL基线仅17.7%）；少样本情景记忆下单行人场景达96.0%（DQN为82.0%）；跨行为泛化中，从折返交互习得的记忆迁移至未见过的犹豫和双向穿越场景，成功率分别达82.0%和90.0%。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的决策框架在行人丰富的城市环境中显著优于深度强化学习基线，具备更早的响应时机、更宽的安全裕度和可解释的类人决策能力，为零样本泛化和跨行为迁移提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) must make reliable decisions in dense urban environments where pedestrian behavior is variable, sometimes abnormal, and often unseen during training. Reinforcement learning (RL)-based AV control systems perform well in structured traffic but struggle to generalize to unpredictable pedestrian interactions and out-of-distribution scenarios. Their reliance on handcrafted rewards and opaque decisions further limits their suitability for safety-critical, pedestrian-rich environments. To address these limitations, we introduce a Large Language Model (LLM)-based decision-making framework for pedestrian-aware behavioral planning. The system converts structured scene observations into natural-language reasoning prompts, enabling the LLM to infer pedestrian intent, anticipate risk, and generate cautious tactical driving decisions. These decisions are executed by a motion planner that ensures smooth, kinematically feasible control. We evaluate the framework in SUMO across multiple pedestrian-interaction scenarios, including unexpected jaywalking, turn-back crossing, hesitation, and bidirectional crossing. In zero-shot evaluation, the LLM-based agent achieves a 68% collision-free success rate, substantially outperforming deep RL baselines (17.7%). With few-shot episodic memory in a single-pedestrian scenario, performance increases to 96.0%, exceeding a custom DQN controller (82.0%). Cross-behavior evaluation further shows that memory derived from turn-back interactions transfers to unseen hesitation and bidirectional crossing scenarios, achieving 82.0% and 90.0% success, respectively. The system consistently initiates earlier responses, maintains wider safety buffers, and produces interpretable, human-aligned decisions.

</details>


### [164] [Plan First, Diffuse Later: Extrinsic Graph Guidance for Long-Horizon Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16863)
*Yaniv Hassidof,Adir Morgan,Yilun Du,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出XDiffuser，通过外部分数搜索（在去噪过程之外）先计算状态空间图上的计划作为轻量级局部连通性先验，再引导扩散模型生成单条轨迹，从而在长时域规划任务中实现更好的全局一致性，并在低质量数据和未见任务（如多智能体协调、TSP推理）上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合扩散模型在长时域规划中依赖内在搜索（intrinsic search）来改善全局连贯性，但这会带来重复评估计算密集模型的高昂成本。本文动机是探索外部分数搜索（extrinsic search）是否能在去噪过程之外更有效地进行探索，同时允许在测试时使用经典算法解决未见组合任务。

Method: XDiffuser采用两阶段方法：首先在状态空间图上计算一个计划（plan），该计划作为轻量级局部连通性先验（local connectivity oracle）供扩散模型使用；然后利用该计划引导去噪过程生成单条轨迹，将探索负担从去噪过程转移到外部搜索。

Result: XDiffuser在长时域任务上优于基于扩散的基线方法，在低质量数据场景和未见任务（包括多智能体协调和类TSP推理）上取得了特别大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 外部分数搜索比内在搜索更有效地实现了长时域规划中的探索，并能自然地融合经典算法解决测试时的新组合问题，为扩散模型在规划任务中的应用提供了一种更高效、更通用的范式。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion models offer a promising route to long-horizon planning by denoising multiple overlapping sub-trajectories while ensuring that together they constitute a global solution. However, enforcing local behavior over long chains is often insufficient for a coherent global structure to emerge. Recent works tackle this limitation through intrinsic search, which explores multiple paths during the denoising process. While intrinsic search improves global coherence, it comes at the cost of repeated evaluations of an already compute-heavy model. In this work, we argue that extrinsic search, performed outside the denoising process, offers a more effective mode of exploration for long-horizon planning while naturally enabling the use of classical algorithms to solve unseen combinatorial tasks at test time. Our eXtrinsic search-guided Diffuser (XDiffuser) first computes a plan over a state-space graph -- serving as a lightweight local connectivity oracle for the diffusion model. The plan is then used to guide denoising for a single trajectory, effectively offloading the burden of exploration. XDiffuser outperforms diffusion-based baselines on long-horizon tasks, with particularly large gains in the low-quality data regime and on unseen tasks beyond goal-reaching, including multi-agent coordination and TSP-style reasoning. Project website: https://yanivhass.github.io/XDiffuser-site/

</details>


### [165] [SADP: Subgoal-Aware Diffusion Policy for Explainable Robots Learned from Foundation Model Generated Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16871)
*Site Hu,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出子目标感知扩散策略（SADP），利用基础模型自动生成带有子目标标注的演示数据，训练扩散策略使其在长时程操作任务中能同时执行任务并暴露子目标级的决策过程，实验表明该方法在任务成功率和可解释性上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法仅依赖任务级演示，缺乏对子目标结构和执行进度的显式建模，且标准机器人学习数据集中缺乏子目标级监督，限制了机器人在长时程操作中向用户传达当前执行子任务的能力。

Method: 提出子目标感知扩散策略（SADP），包含三个关键组件：1）利用基础模型自动生成子目标标注的演示数据集；2）在动作生成时同时以任务级和子目标级描述为条件，使策略围绕人类可解释的子目标执行；3）轻量级辅助头预测子目标完成状态，用于暴露执行阶段和监控进度。

Result: 在RLBench仿真和UR5e机器人真实实验中，SADP在任务成功率上超过强任务条件扩散基线，并能提供子目标级执行信号用于进度监控和故障诊断。

Conclusion: 内建的可解释性（而非事后解释）可以与高任务性能共存，SADP证明了这一点。

Abstract: Explainable robots require not only successful task execution but also the ability to expose internal decision-making process in a user-friendly manner. However, most imitation learning methods are trained solely on task-level demonstrations, without explicitly modeling subgoal structure or execution progress. This limitation is further exacerbated by the scarcity of subgoal-level supervision in standard robot learning datasets, which restricts the development of robots that can convey the subtasks they are executing during long-horizon manipulation. To address this issue, this paper proposes Subgoal-Aware Diffusion Policy (SADP), a framework that leverages foundation models to autonomously generate subgoal-annotated demonstrations and trains diffusion policies on these datasets. SADP structures policy execution around human-interpretable subgoals by conditioning action generation on both task-level and subgoal-level descriptions. A lightweight auxiliary head further predicts subgoal completion states, allowing the robot to expose its current execution stage and monitor subgoal progression. Experiments in RLBench simulations and real-world evaluations on a UR5e robot demonstrate that SADP achieves higher task success rates than strong task-conditioned diffusion baselines, while providing subgoal-level execution signals for monitoring progress and diagnosing failures. These results highlight that built-in, rather than post-hoc, interpretability can coexist with high task performance.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Safety Filtering: Control Barrier Function-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Connected and Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16894)
*Jianye Xu,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的奖励设计方法，用于多智能体强化学习（MARL），通过在四向多车道交叉口场景中与两种启发式奖励基线进行对比，证明该方法在任务性能和超参数鲁棒性上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习中的奖励设计依赖手工启发式方法，难以调优，缺乏对安全约束的显式引导。

Method: 将控制障碍函数（CBF）约束值转换为奖励信号，显式指导多智能体强化学习中的安全学习。

Result: 在四向多车道交叉口场景中，与两种启发式奖励基线相比，该方法任务性能最高，且对奖励超参数不敏感，在测试超参数范围内保持一致的强性能。

Conclusion: CBF引导的奖励设计能有效提升MARL的任务表现和超参数鲁棒性，是一种优于手工启发式奖励的安全学习方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) uses rewards to guide learning, yet reward design is typically hand-crafted using heuristics that can be difficult to tune. We propose a Control Barrier Function (CBF)-informed reward design for Multi-Agent RL (MARL) that converts CBF constraint values under joint MARL actions into a reward signal that explicitly guides safe learning. We compare against two heuristic reward baselines in a four-way multi-lane intersection with connected and automated vehicles. Results show that our method achieves the highest task performance and is less sensitive to reward hyperparameters, yielding consistently strong performance across the tested hyperparameter range. Code for reproducing the experimental results and a video demonstration are available at https://github.com/bassamlab/SigmaRL.

</details>


### [167] [MORN: Metacognitive Object-Goal Regulation for Resource-Rational Long-Horizon Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16932)
*Xi Lin,Jiayi Li,Kangyi Wu,Jiaqiao Tang,Qingrong He,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MORN是一种受双过程理论启发的元认知目标导航架构，通过System 2元控制器监控System 1运动系统，利用潜力指数、持久性门控和证据积累三种神经认知状态动态调整任务调度，有效消除沉没成本谬误，在HM3D数据集上将目标完成率从0.23提升至0.30，并将浪费步数比例从0.90降至0.70。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本ObjectNav智能体基于VLM实现语义目标定位，但纯反应式系统缺乏全局资源感知能力，会在部分可观测环境下因沉没成本谬误而耗尽力竭资源（时间、电池），无法平衡局部探索与全局任务可行性。

Method: MORN架构：1）基于双过程理论，在冻结的导航主干（System 1）上叠加System 2元控制器；2）形式化三种神经认知状态——潜力指数、持久性门控和证据积累；3）根据进度速度和感知不确定性的在线估计动态调控任务调度。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上，MORN将目标完成率从0.23提升至0.30，浪费步数比例从0.90降至0.70。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的自主系统中，对全局资源的元认知感知与反应式导航能力同等重要。

Abstract: Robots deployed in unstructured human environments must frequently execute long-horizon missions, such as find the mug, then the chair, then the printer, under strict operational constraints. While contemporary zero-shot Object Navigation (ObjectNav) agents leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to effectively localize semantic targets, they operate as purely reactive systems that inherently lack global resource awareness. Consequently, these agents inadvertently exhaust critical budgets, including time and battery, on infeasible subgoals due to partial observability, failing to balance local exploration with global mission viability. To bridge this gap by injecting resource-rationality into the navigation loop, we present MORN (Metacognitive Object-goal Regulation Navigation), an executive architecture inspired by Dual-Process Theory in cognitive science. MORN augments frozen navigation backbones with a System 2 meta-controller that continuously monitors the System 1 locomotor. By formalizing three neuro-cognitive states, Potentiality Index, Persistence Gating, and Evidence Accumulation, MORN dynamically regulates the mission schedule based on online estimates of progress velocity and perceptual uncertainty. This mechanism effectively neutralizes the Sunk Cost Fallacy, enabling agents to abort zombie goals early and decisively commit to achievable ones. Extensive experiments on the HM3D dataset demonstrate that MORN improves Goal Completion Rate (CR) from 0.23 to 0.30 and reduces Wasted Step Fraction (WSF) from 0.90 to 0.70, establishing that in resource-constrained autonomy, the metacognitive awareness of global resources is as critical as the reactive ability to navigate.

</details>


### [168] [NORM-Nav: Zero-Shot Mobile Robot Navigation with Natural Language Behavioral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16979)
*Dongjie Huo,Junhui Wang,Chao Gao,Yan Qiao,Dong Zhang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NORM-Nav是一个零样本导航框架，通过LLM将自然语言行为约束解析为结构化约束，并利用实时视觉-激光雷达感知将其编码为多层costmaps（几何、语义、方向、速度），使标准网格规划器能生成遵循社会规范的轨迹，在仿真和真实实验中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于costmap的导航强调几何可行性，忽略了人类环境中的社会行为规范（如避让、礼仪等），导致机器人行为不自然。需要一种能将自然语言行为约束无缝融入规划的方法。

Method: 1. 使用LLM将自然语言指令解析为结构化约束；2. 通过实时视觉-激光雷达感知对约束进行环境接地；3. 将约束编码为多层costmaps（几何、语义、方向、速度）；4. 直接兼容标准网格规划器进行路径规划。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，NORM-Nav相比代表性基线方法，任务成功率更高，生成的轨迹更接近人类参考轨迹。

Conclusion: 提出了一种零样本、无需额外训练即可将自然语言行为约束集成到costmap导航中的框架，有效提升了机器人在人类环境中的社交合规性与任务表现。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating in human-centered environments must generate not only collision-free paths but also trajectories that follow local behavioral conventions. Conventional costmap-based navigation emphasizes geometric feasibility and often overlooks such requirements, which can result in socially inappropriate behaviors. This paper presents NORM-Nav, a zero-shot framework that integrates natural language behavioral constraints into costmap-based planning. An LLM parses each instruction into structured constraints and grounds them using real-time vision--LiDAR perception. These constraints are encoded as multi-layer costmaps that represent geometric, semantic, directional, and velocity cues and are directly compatible with standard grid-based planners. Simulation and real-world experiments indicate that NORM-Nav improves task success rates and produces trajectories closer to human references than representative baselines. The project website is available at https://ei-nav.github.io/NORM-Nav.

</details>


### [169] [Generalizable and Actionable Parts Pose Estimation with Symmetry Annotation-Free Learning Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17033)
*Wenxiao Chen,Xueyu Yuan,Liu Liu,Di Wu,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAFAG框架，无需对称注释即可实现通用可操作部件姿态估计，通过两步细化四元数回归和自监督学习处理对称性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用可操作部件（GAPart）姿态估计方法要么对对称性问题设计不足，要么需要大量对称注释，这严重阻碍了在数据缺乏场景下的精确估计。

Method: 提出两步细化候选到最终四元数回归框架，并将对称预测转化为概率分布问题，采用自监督学习策略处理对称性。

Result: 实验结果表明SAFAG具有优越的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作在具身智能系统的多个领域具有巨大的应用潜力。

Abstract: Urgently needed generalizable robot object interaction and manipulation requires high-quality Cross-Category object perception. As a pioneer of this area, Generalizable and Actionable Parts (GAParts) understanding has attracted increasing attention from relevant researchers. However, most recent works either have insufficient design regarding the symmetry issue or require rich symmetry annotation, which severely impedes precise GAPart pose estimation in data-lacking scenarios. In this paper, we propose SAFAG, a novel Symmetry Annotation-Free framework for Generalizable and Actionable Parts Pose Estimation. Specifically, we suggest a stepwise refinement two-stage framework for candidate-to-final quaternion regression, and tackle the symmetry prediction as a probability distribution problem with self-supervised learning strategy. The experimental results demonstrate the superior performance and robustness of our SAFAG. We believe that our work has the enormous potential to be applied in many areas of embodied AI system.

</details>


### [170] [How to Instruct Your Robot: Dense Language Annotations Power Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17077)
*Bosung Kim,Ruiyi Wang,David Acuna,Jaehun Jung,Alexander Trevithick,Brandon Cui,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DeMiAn（密集多维度标注）方法，通过VLM对已有演示片段进行密集重标注（涵盖物理运动、场景构成、手臂姿态和推理四个维度），并训练一个学习型指导器在部署时自适应选择任务合适的标注维度，在不收集新演示数据的情况下提升机器人策略学习性能。在超过100万机器人操作片段和5万人类第一人称视频上验证，DeMiAn显著提升了视觉-语言-动作策略和视频基础的世界动作模型，在RoboCasa上将成功率提升5个百分点，并改善了复合任务和分布外性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略学习的规模化受限于演示数据收集的高成本，而语言标注相对便宜。现有方法通常忽略或简单处理语言标注，未能充分利用已有演示数据中的语言信号。作者旨在通过密集语言标注作为杠杆，从固定的演示数据中提取更多有效信号，突破机器人学习的数据瓶颈。

Method: DeMiAn包含两阶段：1）重标注阶段：利用VLM对已有演示片段从四个互补维度（物理运动、场景构成、手臂姿态、推理）生成密集标注；2）部署阶段：训练一个学习型指导器，根据任务描述和初始场景快照，动态选择任务最适配的标注维度，并异步运行以隐藏生成延迟。

Result: 在100万+操作片段和5万EgoVerse人类视频上，DeMiAn提升了VLA策略和视频基础的世界动作模型。在RoboCasa上，指导器比仅用任务描述基线高出5个百分点的成功率，与每个任务有独立标注的oracle仅差3个百分点。没有单一固定标注维度在所有任务上占优，证明了选择合适的密集语言标注的重要性。DeMiAn还改善了复合任务和分布外性能，并在考虑标注FLOPs后优化了计算-性能边界。

Conclusion: 密集重标注是一种实用的机器人策略学习规模化杠杆，无需收集新演示数据即可显著提升性能。选择正确的密集语言标注比单一标注维度更有效，DeMiAn为固定数据集的信号提取提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling robot policy learning is bottlenecked by the cost of collecting demonstrations, while language annotations for existing demonstrations are comparatively cheap. We study language density as a lever for extracting more signal from a fixed robot or egocentric-video corpus. We introduce DeMiAn (Dense Multi-aspect Annotation), a two-stage approach that first re-labels demonstration segments with VLM-generated annotations along four complementary aspects: physical motion, scene composition, arm pose, and reasoning. A learned instructor then maps a task description and initial scene snapshot to a task-appropriate annotation at deployment, running asynchronously so generation latency is hidden behind policy execution. Across over 1M robot manipulation clips and 50K EgoVerse human-egocentric videos, DeMiAn improves both a vision-language-action policy and a video-based world-action model without collecting new demonstrations. On RoboCasa, the instructor raises success by 5 points over a task-only baseline and comes within 3 points of a per-task oracle. No fixed annotation aspect dominates across tasks, showing that selecting the right dense language matters. DeMiAn also improves composite-task and out-of-distribution performance, and shifts the compute-performance frontier in both mid-training and post-training after accounting for annotation-generation FLOPs. These results position dense re-annotation as a practical scaling lever for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [171] [Contrastive Conceptor Activation Steering (COAST): Unlocking Vision-Language-Action Models through Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17144)
*Miranda Muqing Miao,Subin Kim,Brandon Yang,Lyle Ungar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COAST通过概念算子（conceptor）将VLA模型的隐层表示向任务相关的成功子空间引导，无需额外训练即可显著提升机器人任务成功率，在仿真和实物实验中分别提升超过20%和40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然从大规模视觉-语言预训练中获得了强大的感知先验，但在实际机器人任务中表现脆弱，经常在简单任务上失败。作者希望找到一种轻量级、无需训练的方法来解锁VLA模型中已有的任务相关知识。

Method: 1) 基于"conceptor"概念，构建一个线性算子将数据软投影到目标分布的主成分上；2) 从少量成功和失败的 rollout 样本中识别出对目标任务关键的成功子空间；3) 推理时，将VLA的隐层表示向这些成功子空间引导（steering）。

Result: 在三种架构不同的神经策略（流匹配VLA、自回归VLA、扩散策略）上，COAST在仿真和实物机器人任务中分别将绝对平均任务成功率提升了超过20%和40%。

Conclusion: 1) 失败模式在不同任务间共享显著结构，而成功表征则具有任务特异性；2) 当任务共享相似失败模式时，预拟合的概念算子可直接迁移到新任务；3) 当前VLA的隐层表示中已包含大量任务相关知识，其动作解码瓶颈可通过向任务相关的子空间引导来缓解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage powerful perceptual priors from web-scale Vision-Language Model (VLM) pre-training, yet they remain surprisingly brittle in practice, frequently failing at simple robotic tasks. To mitigate this, we propose Contrastive Conceptor Activation Steering (COAST). COAST builds on the notion of a "conceptor", a linear operator that soft-projects data into the principal components of a target distribution. COAST uses conceptors to identify success-critical subspaces for a target robotic task from a few examples of success and failure rollouts. At inference time, it steers VLA latents into these identified success subspaces to improve task outcomes. Across three architecturally distinct neural policies (flow-matching VLA, autoregressive VLA, and Diffusion Policy), COAST improves absolute mean simulation and real-robot task success rate by over 20 and 40% respectively. The activation subspace geometry reveals that failure modes share substantial structure across tasks while success representations remain largely task-specific. When tasks share similar failure modes, this structure enables previously fitted conceptors to improve performance on new tasks without refitting. Ultimately, our results suggest that current VLAs retain substantial task-relevant knowledge in their latent representations, and that the action expert's decoding bottleneck could be mitigated by steering its residual stream toward task-relevant subspaces. COAST provides a lightweight, training-free path to unlocking these latent capabilities by steering the model towards its own "success" distributions.

</details>


### [172] [Event-Grounded Sparse Autoencoders for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17204)
*Xinchen Jin,Aditya Chatterjee,Pranav Kumar,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件锚定的SAE特征分析流程，用于可视-语言-动作（VLA）策略的机械可解释性。通过将末端执行器关键帧聚类到行为事件中，结合视觉、状态和时序线索，将SAE特征与行为事件及语义上下文关联起来。在两种仿真架构和真实机器人实验中，事件锚定排名对OpenVLA和π₀.₅产生最强的因果效应。SAE作为稀疏但非完美的干预基础，其可用性因架构和干预部位而异，激进干预揭示了安全性和可解释性的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言和视觉模型的机械可解释性工具无法直接迁移到VLA策略，因为VLA输出是机器人动作而非人类可读的token，且干预只能通过昂贵的闭环 rollout 测试。现有方法缺乏行为锚定的可解释性分析框架。

Method: 提出事件锚定可解释性流程：(1) 基于视觉、状态和时序线索对末端执行器关键帧进行聚类，形成行为事件；(2) 将SAE特征与行为事件关联，并通过可选的VLM标注链接到语义上下文；(3) 在闭环行为事件上评估SAE特征的因果效应。

Result: 在两种仿真架构和真实机器人实验中，事件锚定排名产生最强的因果效应，并支持从OpenVLA迁移到π₀.₅的连续动作块。但SAE的可用性因架构和干预部位而异，激进干预存在安全和可解释性风险。

Conclusion: 事件锚定的SAE分析可作为行为锚定VLA可解释性的实用起点，未来工作需关注非动作对齐坐标的SAE特征、更细粒度的闭环评估以及高风险VLA部署的安全干预方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies translate language and visual inputs into robot actions, where their hidden representations directly shape closed-loop behavior. However, mechanistic interpretability tools from language and vision-language models do not transfer cleanly to VLAs: outputs are robot actions rather than human-readable tokens, and interventions can only be tested via expensive closed-loop rollouts. We propose an event-grounded interpretability pipeline that anchors SAE feature analysis to behavioral events rather than text contexts. End-effector keyframes are clustered within each task using visual, state, and temporal cues, linking SAE features to behaviorally salient events and, via optional VLM annotations, to semantic context. To our knowledge, our pipeline is among the first to ground SAE-based VLA analysis in closed-loop behavioral events. Across two simulation architectures and a real-robot study, event-grounded ranking yields the strongest causal effects on OpenVLA and transfers to the continuous action chunks of $π_{0.5}$. SAE is a sparse but imperfect intervention basis: usability varies with architecture and intervention site, and aggressive intervention reveals safety and interpretability limits. Overall, event-grounded SAE analysis emerges as a practical starting point for behavior-anchored VLA interpretability, motivating future work on SAE features beyond action-aligned coordinates, finer-grained closed-loop evaluation, and safe interventions for high-stakes VLA deployments. Code is available at \url{https://github.com/xc-j/Event-SAE}.

</details>


### [173] [Generating Realistic Safety-Critical Scenarios for Vehicle-Pedestrian Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17229)
*Qingwen Pu,Kun Xie,Yuan Zhu,Guocong Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段框架（MA-SST-DDPG），结合真实世界数据与自适应仿真，生成大规模行为逼真的安全关键性车辆-行人交互数据集VPSCI（198,000+交互轨迹），在轨迹误差（ADE=0.072m, FDE=0.142m）和分布一致性上显著优于基线，图灵测试证明生成行为与真实交互不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统部署需要严格验证车辆-行人安全关键交互，但真实数据集缺乏高风险场景，仿真平台又缺少逼真行为建模，两者均无法满足验证需求。

Method: 三阶段框架：Stage 1使用MA-SST-DDPG在真实安全关键数据上预训练学习类人交互规避行为；Stage 2在CARLA仿真中在线强化学习泛化至多样场景；Stage 3利用精炼模型生成大规模VPSCI数据集。

Result: 精炼MA-SST-DDPG在轨迹预测中达到最低ADE（0.072m）和FDE（0.142m）；生成数据与真实数据在冲突严重度和行为响应上分布等价；图灵测试表明生成规避行为与真实交互不可区分。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效生成高保真安全关键数据，为自动驾驶系统开发和基于仿真的安全评估提供宝贵资源。

Abstract: Automated driving system deployment requires rigorous validation across safety-critical vehicle-pedestrian interactions, yet real-world datasets rarely capture high-risk scenarios while simulation platforms lack realistic behavior. In response, this study proposes a three-stage framework that combines real-world grounding with adaptive simulation to generate behaviorally realistic safety-critical scenarios at scale. Stage 1 pre-trains multi-agent state-space Transformer-enhanced DDPG (MA-SST-DDPG) agents on real-world safety-critical data to learn human-like interactive evasive behaviors through data-driven learning. Stage 2 deploys pre-trained multi-agents in CARLA for online reinforcement learning to generalize across diverse scenarios, integrating real-world knowledge with simulation experience to produce a refined MA-SST-DDPG model. Stage 3 uses CARLA with the refined model to generate over 198,000 high-resolution interaction episodes from eight intersection scenarios, culminating in the Vehicle-Pedestrian Safety-Critical Interaction (VPSCI) dataset. The Refined MA-SST-DDPG model outperformed baseline methods in reproducing realistic evasive behaviors, achieving the lowest trajectory errors (ADE = 0.072 m, FDE = 0.142 m). Statistical comparison confirmed distributional equivalence between the generated and real-world data in both conflict severity and behavioral response. A Turing test confirmed that the three-stage framework generated evasive behaviors were indistinguishable from real-world interactions. These results demonstrate the framework's effectiveness in producing high-fidelity safety-critical data, offering valuable sources for the development of ADS and simulation-based safety evaluations.

</details>


### [174] [Stretch-ICP: A Continuous-Trajectory Registration and Deskewing Algorithm in Scenarios of Aggressive Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17264)
*Simon-Pierre Deschênes,Veronica Vannini,Philippe Giguère,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIGS数据集（包含高速翻滚运动下的激光雷达和IMU数据），并提出了两种方法——SAAVE（饱和感知角速度估计）和Stretch-ICP（拉伸ICP配准与去畸变算法）——来提升剧烈运动下SLAM的鲁棒性，分别将角速度估计误差降低83.4%，以及将扫描边界处的线速度和角速度误差降低95.2%和94.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂环境（如不平整或湿滑地形）中，机器人容易失去稳定性导致剧烈翻滚，产生极端加速度和角速度。这种运动会破坏传感器测量值并降低状态估计精度，因此需要提高算法的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 构建TIGS数据集：采集机械激光雷达和IMU从山坡翻滚下的数据，角速度可达类似数据集的4倍。2. 提出SAAVE方法：在陀螺仪饱和时估计角速度。3. 提出Stretch-ICP方法：一种新的配准与去畸变算法，用于重建更平滑的6-DOF轨迹。

Result: SAAVE将角速度估计误差降低83.4%；Stretch-ICP将扫描边界处的线速度和角速度误差分别降低95.2%和94.8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的TIGS数据集、SAAVE和Stretch-ICP共同提升了剧烈运动下激光雷达-惯性状态估计的鲁棒性和一致性。

Abstract: Robust robotic autonomy remains challenging in complex environments, where loss of stability on uneven or slippery terrain can induce extreme accelerations and angular velocities. Such motions corrupt sensor measurements and degrade state estimation, motivating the need for improved algorithmic robustness. To investigate this issue, we introduce the Tumbling-Induced Gyroscope Saturation (TIGS) dataset, which consists of recordings from a mechanical lidar and an Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) tumbling down a hill. The dataset contains angular speeds up to four times higher than those in similar datasets and is publicly available. We then propose two complementary methods to improve Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM) robustness and evaluate them on TIGS. First, Saturation-Aware Angular Velocity Estimation (SAAVE) estimates angular velocities when gyroscope measurements become saturated during aggressive motions, reducing angular speed estimation error by 83.4%. Second, Stretch-ICP, a novel registration and deskewing algorithm, enables reconstruction of smoother 6-Degrees Of Freedom (DOF) trajectories under aggressive motions compared to classical Iterative Closest Point (ICP). Stretch-ICP reduces linear and angular velocity errors by 95.2% and 94.8%, respectively, at scan boundaries. Together, these contributions improve the robustness and consistency of lidar-inertial state estimation under aggressive motions.

</details>


### [175] [Task Capability Improvement Algorithm for Collaborative Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17293)
*Keshab Patra,Arpita Sinha,Anirban Guha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用额外力矩提升协作机械臂的操作能力，通过在非质心点施力产生额外力矩，使单个机械臂及整个协作组的能力得到增强，仿真显示能力提升5.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决协作机械臂在物体操作中能力不足的问题，探索如何利用通常被视为不利因素的额外力矩来提升整体协作能力。

Method: 在物体抓取点施力时，利用非质心施力产生的额外力矩作为增强因素，提升单个机械臂及协作组的能力，实现最优能力分配与最大容错。

Result: 仿真结果显示，与不使用力矩增强相比，能力提升5.86%。

Conclusion: 额外力矩可以有效提升协作机械臂的操作能力，并有助于实现最优资源分配和故障容错。

Abstract: This work introduces a cooperative task capability improvement utilizing additional moments. The manipulators apply forces at the object's grasp point. Applying forces at a point other than the object's center of gravity produces undesired moments. The undesired moment acts as an additional moment. It improves the capability of an individual manipulator and, hence, the entire collaborative group. Any improvements in task capability directly add up to the object and transportation capability. The group's enhanced capability also helps achieve optimal capability, optimal resource allocation, and maximum fault tolerance in object manipulation. Our simulation results show an improvement in the capability of 5.86 \% compared to when no moment is used to enhance the capability of the manipulators.

</details>


### [176] [HCLM: A Hierarchical Framework for Cooperative Loco-Manipulation with Dual Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17300)
*Qixuan Li,Chen Le,Jincheng Yu,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HCLM，一种用于双四足机器人协同全身操控的分层框架，通过解耦高层协同推理与低层鲁棒运动执行，实现通用型协同搬运、打包和交接任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同操控面临空间协调、鲁棒 locomotion 和闭环物理交互的冲突需求，现有方法难以有效兼顾。

Method: 高层使用SE(3)不变任务空间表示的集中式联合扩散策略；低层采用任务中心混合全身控制器，结合主动式运动学MPC与反应式执行层，并通过协同导纳控制调节内力。

Result: 在协同搬运、打包和交接等模拟场景中验证，并在真实环境中部署交接任务，展现出可靠任务执行、严格构型无关性及对严重物理扰动的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为多机器人具身协调提供了一条高鲁棒性的通路。

Abstract: We introduce HCLM, a hierarchical framework for general-purpose cooperative loco-manipulation with dual quadrupedal systems. Coordinating multi-robot collaborative manipulation across floating bases is highly challenging due to the conflicting demands of spatial coordination, robust locomotion, and closed-chain physical interactions. To resolve this, our architecture systematically decouples high-level collaborative reasoning from low-level robust motion execution. At the high level, a centralized Joint Diffusion Policy leverages an SE(3)-invariant task-space representation to learn coordinate-agnostic spatial coordination patterns. To translate these frame-agnostic references into physical motion, a task-centric hybrid Whole-Body Controller synergizes a proactive kinematic Model Predictive Control for collision-free velocity distribution with a reactive execution layer. Crucially, this reactive layer guarantees rapid responsiveness for precise end-effector tracking, while concurrently integrating active force regulation via a cooperative admittance scheme to safely resolve kinematic conflicts and strictly regulate internal stresses during closed-chain interactions. We validate the framework across progressively challenging simulated scenarios, including cooperative carrying, packing and handovers, and successfully deploy the latter in the real world. The results demonstrate reliable task execution, strict configuration agnosticism, and exceptional resilience against severe physical perturbations, offering a highly robust pathway for multi-robot embodied coordination.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Geometry: Efficient Topologically-Grounded Navigation in Complex 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17302)
*Yifan Du,Chengwei Zhang,Siyu Liao,Zhongfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种表面提取框架，通过约束地面支撑、顶部净空和种子连通性，显著减少3D导航状态空间，实现亚毫秒级A*寻路和100%规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂3D环境中地面机器人导航面临几何歧义（非可穿越结构与可导航地面的局部几何相似）以及体素空间搜索计算成本高昂的问题。

Method: 构建表面提取框架，通过强制执行地面支撑（ground support）、顶部净空（overhead clearance）和基于种子连通性（seed-based connectivity）的约束，将状态空间缩减为物理上可到达的站立位置。

Result: 在5个Matterport3D室内场景和3个PCT基准场景上，状态空间缩减超过80%，Matterport3D场景上实现亚毫秒级A*搜索，300次测试查询中100%规划成功。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了几何歧义和计算效率问题，实现了高效可靠的3D环境地面机器人导航。

Abstract: Ground robot navigation in complex 3D environments is often hindered by geometric ambiguity, where non-traversable structures such as furniture share local geometric properties with navigable ground. Furthermore, the computational cost of searching massive voxel spaces remains a significant challenge. To address these issues, we present a surface extraction framework that constructs a reduced state space of physically reachable standing positions by enforcing ground support, overhead clearance, and seed-based connectivity constraints. Evaluation across five Matterport3D indoor scenes and three PCT benchmark scenes demonstrates over 80\% state space reduction and sub-millisecond A* search on the Matterport3D scenes, with 100\% planning success across all 300 tested queries.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Feature-Free Initialization for Monocular Visual-Inertial Systems Using a Feed-Forward 3D Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17327)
*Yuantai Zhang,Jiaqi Yang,Huajian Zeng,Changhao Chen,Haoang Li,Liang Li,Dezhen Song,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前馈3D模型的无特征视觉-惯性初始化框架，无需视觉特征跟踪，可在1.2秒内实现90%以上成功率的单目VINS初始化，在视觉退化场景下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目VINS初始化方法严重依赖视觉特征对应，需要3-4秒传感器数据才能完成初始化，限制了其适用性和效率。作者希望利用前馈3D模型的点云预测能力，简化初始化流程并提升可靠性。

Method: 提出无特征初始化框架，使用前馈3D模型直接预测带尺度的点云，替代传统视觉特征跟踪与估计流程，降低系统复杂度。

Result: 在公开数据集上初始化成功率超过90%，所需数据时长缩短至1.2秒以下；在自采集数据集（涵盖室内外场景）上表现出鲁棒性，尤其在视觉退化环境中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 无特征初始化方法有效降低了系统复杂度，提高了初始化成功率和速度，在视觉退化场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Fast and reliable initialization is critical for monocular visual-inertial navigation systems (VINS), as it establishes the starting conditions for subsequent state estimation. Despite steady progress, most existing methods heavily rely on visual feature correspondences and require 3-4 seconds of sensory data for successful initialization, which limits their applicability and efficiency. With the advent of feed-forward 3D models that can directly predict point clouds from images, we revisit the visual-inertial initialization problem from a concise perspective. In this work, we propose a feature-free initialization framework that leverages up-to-scale point clouds predicted by a feed-forward 3D model, thereby obviating the need for visual feature tracking and estimation. This design substantially reduces system complexity and improves the reliability of initialization. Experiments on public datasets demonstrate that the proposed feature-free initialization method achieves the highest success rate, exceeding 90%, and significantly reduces the data duration required for successful initialization, typically to under 1.2 s. We further validate our method on a self-collected dataset covering various indoor and outdoor scenarios, demonstrating robust performance, particularly in visually degraded environments where existing methods often fail. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Yuantai-Z/FF-VIO-Init.

</details>


### [179] [Tactile-based Multimodal Fusion in Embodied Intelligence: A Survey of Vision, Language, and Contact-Driven Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17336)
*Zhixiang Cao,Di Tian,Runwei Guan,Yanzhou Mu,Xiaolou Sun,Shaofeng Liang,Daizong Liu,Tao Huang,Yutao Yue,Henghui Ding,Bin Fang,Alex Zhou,Qing-Long Han,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年第一季度的多模态触觉融合研究进行了全面综述，提出了一个包含多模态数据集和多模态方法两个主要维度的层次化分类体系，系统梳理了触觉-视觉、触觉-语言等数据集以及多模态感知与识别、跨模态生成、多模态交互三大核心方法支柱，并总结了触觉传感硬件、评估指标、基准设置及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知是具身智能的基础模态，但单模态触觉感知受限于空间覆盖稀疏和缺乏全局语义上下文。随着深度学习和大语言模型的发展，将触觉与视觉、语言融合以桥接物理交互与语义推理变得至关重要，然而现有研究在数据集、传感模态和任务上碎片化严重，缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 提出层次化分类体系：数据维度分为触觉-视觉、触觉-语言、触觉-视觉-语言、触觉-视觉-其他四类数据集；方法维度分为多模态感知与识别（物体理解与抓取预测）、跨模态生成（触觉、视觉、文本双向翻译）、多模态交互（反馈控制与语言引导操作）三大核心支柱。

Result: 系统梳理了多模态触觉融合领域的数据集和方法分类体系，总结了代表性触觉传感硬件、常用评估指标和基准设置。

Conclusion: 本文提供了多模态触觉融合领域的系统性综述与统一分类框架，指出了当前挑战和未来研究方向，为该领域后续研究奠定了结构化基础。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is a fundamental modality for embodied intelligence, offering unique and direct feedback on contact geometry, material properties, and interaction dynamics that remote sensors cannot replace. However, unimodal tactile perception is inherently limited by its sparse spatial coverage and lack of global semantic context. With the recent explosion in deep learning and large language models, integrating tactile with vision and language has become essential to bridge physical interaction with semantic reasoning, leading to the emergence of Multimodal Tactile Fusion. Despite rapid progress, the existing researches remain fragmented across disparate datasets, sensing modalities, and tasks, lacking a unified theoretical framework.
