To address this gap, this paper provides a comprehensive survey of multimodal tactile fusion research up to the first quarter of 2026. We propose a hierarchical taxonomy that organizes the field into two primary dimensions: multimodal datasets and multimodal methods. On the data side, we categorize resources ranging from Tactile-Vision datasets, Tactile-Language datasets, Tactile-Vision-Language datasets, and Tactile-Vision-Other datasets. On the method side, we structure prior work into three core pillars: (1) Multimodal Perception and Recognition, which focuses on object understanding and grasp prediction; (2) Cross-Modal Generation, focusing on bidirectional translation between tactile, vision, and text; and (3) Multimodal Interaction, emphasizing feedback control and language-guided manipulation. Furthermore, we summarize representative tactile sensing hardware, review commonly used evaluation metrics and benchmark settings, and discuss current challenges and promising future directions.

</details>


### [180] [MUSE: Multimodal Uncertainty Quantification of State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17421)
*Minkyung Kim,Henry Che,Bhargav Chandaka,Bhumsitt Pramuanpornsatid,Chengyu Yang,Sheng Cheng,Xiaofeng Wang,Naira Hovakimyan,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE，一种基于Mamba的实时学习框架，用于多模态传感器（视觉-惯性）状态估计的不确定性量化，在公开和自建数据集上展现了优于现有方法的可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉状态估计的精度量化和校准是一个根本性挑战，尤其在视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）中，问题的异方差性和多模态性使得不确定性量化尤为困难，现有方法难以有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MUSE框架，利用Mamba的强大且高效的序列建模能力，从多个异步传感器流中实时估计定位不确定性。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建数据集上的实验表明，MUSE相比现有不确定性量化方法具有更优的可靠性和鲁棒性，消融实验验证了关键设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: MUSE为VIO中的不确定性量化提供了一种实时、可靠且鲁棒的解决方案，Mamba的序列建模能力在多模态异步传感器融合中表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Accurate visual state estimation has been a central topic in robotics with a wide range of applications in robot navigation, autonomous driving, and autonomous flight. Recent advances in robot perception have led to significant improvements in the accuracy and robustness of state estimation, yet a fundamental challenge remains in how to quantify and calibrate its precision, i.e., how confident we are in an estimate and whether failures can be detected. This issue is particularly pronounced in visual-inertial odometry (VIO), where the heteroscedastic and multimodal nature of the problem makes uncertainty quantification especially difficult. This paper introduces MUSE (Multimodal Uncertainty Quantification of State Estimation), a novel real-time learning-based framework that leverages the strong and efficient sequential modeling capacity of Mamba to estimate localization uncertainty from multiple asynchronous sensor streams. Experiments on both public and in-house datasets demonstrate that MUSE achieves superior reliability and robustness compared to existing uncertainty quantification methods, and ablation studies justify the benefits of its key design choices.

</details>


### [181] [Rapid Vibration Suppression and Trajectory Tracking of a Serial Manipulator with Multi-Flexible Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17477)
*Chengyi Wang,Yilong Huang,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反步输出反馈和DeepONet神经算子的n自由度串联柔性机械臂振动抑制与末端跟踪控制框架，将连杆-关节建模为Timoshenko梁-ODE耦合系统并转化为标准双曲PDE，仅利用边界测量实现分布式阻尼注入，通过DeepONet近似反步核函数降低计算成本，双连杆实验验证了其相比LQR+前馈控制更快的振动抑制和轨迹收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机械臂的结构柔性会引起振动、加速疲劳、降低跟踪精度并限制运行速度，这些问题在多连杆串联构型中因总体长度增加而更加严重，亟需一种高效、实用且可扩展的振动抑制与轨迹跟踪控制方法。

Method: 将每个连杆-关节建模为Timoshenko梁与ODE的耦合系统，进而转化为标准双曲PDE模型；设计基于反步法的边界控制器，在关节处注入等效分布式阻尼，仅依赖边界测量实现快速振动抑制和轨迹跟踪；引入DeepONet神经算子近似反步核函数，降低计算复杂度，便于实时实现和扩展。

Result: 在双连杆柔性机械臂实验平台上，所提方法相比线性二次型调节器（LQR）加前馈控制，实现了更快的振动抑制和末端执行器到期望轨迹的收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的反步输出反馈框架结合DeepONet近似，为n自由度串联柔性机械臂提供了一种高效的振动抑制与轨迹跟踪解决方案，具备良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Flexible robotic manipulators (FRMs) offer advantages in lightweight design and large workspace, but their structural flexibility induces vibrations, accelerates fatigue, degrades tracking performance, and limits operational speed. These challenges are further amplified in multi-link serial manipulators, where increased overall length leads to greater structural flexibility. This article presents a backstepping output-feedback framework for fast vibration suppression and tip tracking of an n-degree-of-freedom serial flexible manipulator robot (nDSFMR), with a DeepONet-based approximation for practical deployment. Each link-joint is modeled as a Timoshenko beam coupled with an ODE and transformed into a canonical hyperbolic PDE with boundary dynamics. A backstepping-based boundary controller at the joint is developed to equivalently inject distributed damping along the beam, enabling rapid vibration suppression and trajectory tracking, only using available boundary measurements. To enable real-time implementation and scalability, a DeepONet neural operator is introduced to approximate the backstepping kernels, significantly reducing computational cost and facilitating fast controller updates under varying operating conditions. Experiments on a two-link flexible manipulator demonstrate faster vibration suppression and convergence of the end-effector to the desired trajectory, compared with a linear quadratic regulator (LQR) with feedforward control.

</details>


### [182] [DyGRO-VLA: Cross-Task Scaling of Vision-Language-Action Models via Dynamic Grouped Residual Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17486)
*Sixu Lin,Yunpeng Qing,Litao Liu,Ming Zhou,Ruixing Jin,Xiaoyi Fan,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DyGRO-VLA提出了一种两阶段优化框架，通过信息论原理捕获跨任务潜在表征，并利用混合RL残差动态优化策略，在保持VLA模型通用性的同时提升了多任务和分布偏移下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL优化器在微调VLA模型时倾向于任务特定优化，导致模型从通用控制器退化为过拟合窄任务集的策略，丧失了跨任务泛化能力。本文旨在解决这一退化问题。

Method: 提出DyGRO-VLA两阶段框架：(1) 基于信息论原理有效捕获跨任务潜在表征；(2) 通过混合RL残差（mixture-of-RL-residuals）动态细化策略优化，利用任务相关潜在信息同时减轻优化过程中对已学习表征的有害干扰。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin2基准测试以及真实世界实验中，DyGRO-VLA在多任务训练和分布偏移场景下均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 跨任务特征表征对提升VLA模型泛化性至关重要，DyGRO-VLA通过信息论驱动的跨任务表征学习与动态策略优化相结合，有效解决了RL优化导致的任务过拟合问题。

Abstract: Recent progress in Reinforcement Learning (RL) provides a principled approach to optimizing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, facilitating a shift from trajectory imitation to active learning in the task environment. Despite improvements in control precision, most RL optimizers remain task-specific, which reduces VLA models from generalist controllers to policies that overfit to a narrow set of tasks. In this study, we conduct an in-depth analysis of this phenomenon and highlight the importance of cross-task feature representations for improving the generalizability of VLA models. Motivated by this finding, we introduce DyGRO-VLA, a two-stage optimization framework that 1) effectively captures cross-task latent representations based on information-theoretic principles, and 2) dynamically refines policy optimization via a mixture-of-RL-residuals. DyGRO-VLA enables the RL optimizer to exploit task-relevant latent information while strategically mitigating adverse interference on the learned representations throughout the optimization process. We evaluate our approach on LIBERO, RoboTwin2 benchmarks, and further validate it on real world, demonstrating consistent improvements over strong baselines under multi-task training and distribution shift.

</details>


### [183] [RoboFlow4D: A Lightweight Flow World Model Toward Real-Time Flow-Guided Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17522)
*Sixu Lin,Junliang Chen,Huaiyuan Xu,Zhuohao Li,Guangming Wang,Yixiong Jing,Sheng Xu,Runyi Zhao,Brian Sheil,Lap-Pui Chau,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFlow4D 是一个轻量级的流世界模型，通过端到端的方式统一感知和规划，直接预测3D流来引导机器人操作，实现了实时、高效的闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D环境中的机器人操作规划方法依赖模块化流水线，堆叠多个子模型导致计算开销高、实时性差。亟需一种轻量级、端到端的统一框架来提升效率。

Method: 提出 RoboFlow4D，一个轻量级流世界模型，以端到端方式从视觉观察和文本指令直接预测多帧3D流，提供显式的基于流的规划来引导动作生成；采用慢-快协作机制，将流预测与动作控制协同工作，形成观察-规划-执行闭环。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，RoboFlow4D 在操作成功率和计算效率上均有一致提升，验证了其在具身智能中流引导规划的有效性。

Conclusion: RoboFlow4D 通过轻量化的流世界模型统一感知与规划，实现了实时高效的机器人操作，为具身智能中的流引导规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Planning and acting in 3D environments is a fundamental capability for robotic manipulation in the real world. Although prior work has explored predictive flow planners to guide 3D manipulation, existing approaches often rely on modular pipelines stacking multiple submodels, resulting in high computational overhead and limited real-time performance. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboFlow4D, a lightweight flow world model that unifies perception and planning by estimating temporal motion in physical 3D space. As an end-to-end framework, RoboFlow4D directly predicts multi-frame 3D flows from visual observations and textual instructions, providing explicit flow-based planning to guide action generation. This design allows seamless integration with general action policies, forming an efficient observation-planning-execution closed loop. Through slow-fast collaboration between flow prediction and action control, RoboFlow4D enables real-time and resource-efficient manipulation. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that RoboFlow4D consistently improves manipulation success rates and computational efficiency, advancing flow-guided planning for embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [184] [Visual Sculpting: Visually-Aligned Planning Representations for Long-Horizon Robot Clay Sculpting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17556)
*Peter Schaldenbrand,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在视觉对齐表示中对可变形材料进行动力学建模和机器人雕刻规划的方法，实现了超过100步的长时域陶土浮雕雕刻，并比较了视觉表示与3D表示在规划中的优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作工作要么需要为每个目标重新训练策略，要么依赖将状态表示为稀疏点云的动力学模型，无法很好地捕捉纹理等重要陶土特征。作者希望实现一种能够保持视觉对齐（捕捉光照和纹理特征）的动力学建模与规划方法。

Method: 将陶土雕刻建模为形状到形状的匹配问题；使用视觉对齐的表示来捕捉光照和纹理特征；将动作表示为单个末端执行器对陶土的参数化推压操作；在三种不同可变形材料上使用多种末端执行器进行实验。

Result: 所提出的动力学模型在性能上与现有最先进方法相当，且额外具有兼容视觉规划的优势；能够执行超过100步的长时域陶土浮雕雕刻任务。

Conclusion: 视觉对齐表示在规划中具有优势，但也存在比3D表示更具挑战性的问题，作者提供了相关分析证据。

Abstract: Clay sculpting is a nuanced, artistic task involving dexterous manipulation with long-horizon planning to achieve high-level goals. As a robotics problem, we formulate clay sculpting as a shape-to-shape matching challenge. Prior deformable object manipulation work either requires retraining a policy per goal or relies on dynamics models which represent state as sparse point clouds which do not capture important clay features, such as textures, well. We present a method for modeling the dynamics of deformable materials and planning for robotic sculpting in a representation that is visually-aligned, capturing lighting and texture features. With three different deformable materials and various end-effectors, we demonstrate that our dynamics model is comparable in performance to the state-of-the-art with the added benefit of being compatible with visual planning. Our actions are represented as parametrized pushes into clay with a single end-effector, which proved to be suitable for long-horizon (>100 actions) clay relief sculptures. Lastly, we show the benefits of planning in a visually-aligned representation, but also provide analysis providing evidence as to why this representation is challenging to plan in compared to 3D representations.

</details>


### [185] [Motion-Uncertainty-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Moving Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17593)
*Karen Li,Mattia Mantovani,Robert J. Wood,Lorenzo Sabattini,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运动不确定性感知的下一最佳视角（NBV）框架，用于在平面运动下重建未知刚性物体。该方法通过高斯过程平滑器预测物体未来状态的不确定性分布，并基于此评估候选视角的期望覆盖质量，从而在决策-执行延迟期间兼顾运动预测与重建覆盖。实验表明，该方法在重建完整性上优于非预测性NBV和纯预测跟踪方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能同时解决运动目标重建中的两个关键挑战：NBV规划器假设静态物体而忽略运动不确定性，运动感知方法则优先考虑跟踪/可见性而非重建覆盖。本文旨在弥合覆盖驱动的主动重建与预测驱动的跟踪之间的鸿沟。

Method: 使用固定滞后高斯过程平滑器从含噪位置测量中估计和预测物体状态，获得未来状态的概率信念。基于该信念在预测位置周围生成候选视角，通过可达性过滤，并估计每个视角的期望覆盖得分。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，该方法在重建完整性上优于非预测性NBV和仅预测跟踪方法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将运动不确定性纳入NBV规划，在运动目标重建任务中有效结合了覆盖优化与运动预测，提升了重建完整性。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction of moving objects requires selecting informative viewpoints while accounting for object motion uncertainty during the decision-to-execution delay. Existing methods address only parts of this problem: next-best-view (NBV) planners for object reconstruction typically optimize surface coverage but assume static objects, while motion-aware active perception for moving targets accounts for target motion but prioritizes tracking or visibility over reconstruction coverage. This work presents a motion-uncertainty-aware NBV framework for reconstructing an unknown rigid object undergoing planar motion, using noisy planar position measurements of the object and depth observations from a mobile robot. The key idea is to evaluate each candidate viewpoint by its expected observation quality over plausible future object states induced by motion and measurement uncertainty, rather than at a single predicted object pose. To obtain this predictive belief, a fixed-lag Gaussian Process smoother estimates and predicts the object state from noisy position measurements. The resulting belief is used to generate candidate viewpoints around the predicted object location, filter them by reachability, and estimate their expected coverage-driven scores. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction completeness over non-predictive NBV and prediction-only tracking methods, bridging coverage-driven active reconstruction and prediction-driven tracking.

</details>


### [186] [From a Single Demonstration to a General Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17601)
*Xing Li,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用环境约束作为归纳偏好的单次演示学习框架，通过将演示抽象为基于约束的行为序列，实现在多阶段接触式操作任务中的一次性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法依赖大量演示或难以泛化到新实例，需要一种能高效利用单次演示并在多阶段接触式任务中泛化的方法。

Method: 四阶段pipeline：①将单次演示抽象为环境约束基元；②通过自引导探索消歧约束类型；③整合人工修正处理分布外变化；④在线通过柔顺交互恢复抽象掉的细节。

Result: 在7个真实世界多阶段接触式操作任务上成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 环境约束可作为LfD中高效泛化的基本构建模块。

Abstract: We present a Learning from Demonstration (LfD) framework that achieves one-shot generalization in multi-stage, contact-rich manipulation tasks. Central to our approach is the utilization of environmental constraints as the inductive bias. By representing a demonstration as a sequence of behaviors that exploit environmental constraints, the robot separates task-general structure -- the constraint types and their transitions -- from instance-specific details such as exact demonstration trajectories, poses, and local geometries. Our four-stage pipeline builds a complete policy on this representation: the robot first abstracts a single demonstration into environmental-constraint primitives, then disambiguates them through self-guided exploration, next assimilates targeted human corrections that handle out-of-distribution variations, and finally recovers the abstracted-away details online through compliant interaction. Because the resulting policy follows constraints rather than mimics trajectories, it generalizes across object poses, local geometries, and unmodeled contact dynamics. We validate our approach on seven real-world multi-stage contact-rich manipulation tasks and achieve over 90% success. These extensive experimental results establish environmental constraints as fundamental building blocks for efficient generalization in learning from demonstration.

</details>


### [187] [Mono-Hydra++: Real-Time Monocular Scene Graph Construction with Multi-Task Learning for 3D Indoor Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17661)
*U. V. B. L. Udugama,George Vosselman,Francesco Nex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mono-Hydra++ 是一个基于单目RGB+IMU的实时度量语义建图与层次化3D场景图构建系统，专为资源受限的自主机器人平台设计。它通过结合多任务模型（M2H-MX）、深度特征视觉惯性里程计、稀疏预测深度约束、语义动态掩码以及位姿感知时序融合等技术，在不需要RGB-D或LiDAR深度传感器的情况下，实现了优于RGB-D基线的建图性能，并在Jetson Orin NX上以25.53 FPS运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统度量地图仅支持定位与避障，缺乏语义和关系结构；而3D场景图能连接几何与对象/房间级理解，但现有方案依赖RGB-D或LiDAR传感器，不适合受严格载荷、功耗和算力限制的空中及轻量级机器人平台。因此，需要一种仅依赖轻量级传感器（单目RGB+IMU）即可实现实时度量语义建图与场景图构建的方法。

Method: 系统包含前端和后端两部分。前端采用基于DINOv3的多任务模型M2H-MX同时估计深度和语义，并结合深度特征视觉惯性里程计（VIO）进行位姿估计，在VIO位姿图中加入稀疏预测深度约束，并使用语义掩码处理动态区域。后端通过位姿感知时序对齐后进行体素融合，构建层次化3D场景图。

Result: 在Go-SLAM ScanNet子集上，Mono-Hydra++的平均轨迹误差比最强的RGB-D基线低1.6%；在标定过的7-Scenes数据集上，平均ATE比最强标定基线低29.8%。在真实ITC建筑部署中，使用RealSense RGB+IMU验证了系统可行性，并在Jetson Orin NX 16GB上以25.53 FPS运行ONNX/TensorRT FP16量化的M2H-MX-L感知模型。

Conclusion: Mono-Hydra++证明了仅依赖单目RGB+IMU即可为资源受限的机器人平台提供实时度量语义建图和场景图构建能力，无需主动深度传感器，在精度上甚至超越了部分RGB-D基线方法，并具备嵌入式部署的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous agile robots need more than metric geometry: they must understand objects, rooms, places, and spatial relations for search, inspection, exploration, and human robot interaction. Conventional metric maps support localization and collision avoidance, but do not provide this semantic and relational structure. 3D scene graphs address this gap by connecting geometry with object level and room level understanding. Building such representations on agile platforms remains difficult because aerial and lightweight robots operate under strict payload, power, and compute limits, making RGB-D cameras and LiDAR sensors impractical for many onboard settings. We present Mono-Hydra++, a real time monocular RGB plus IMU pipeline for indoor metric semantic mapping and hierarchical 3D scene graph construction. The system combines M2H-MX, a DINOv3 based multi-task model for depth and semantics, with a deep feature visual inertial odometry front end, sparse predicted depth constraints in the VIO derived pose graph, semantic masking for dynamic regions, and pose aware temporal alignment before volumetric fusion in the Mono-Hydra backend. On the Go-SLAM ScanNet evaluation subset, Mono-Hydra++ achieves 1.6% lower average trajectory error than the strongest RGB-D baseline in our comparison, while using only monocular RGB plus IMU input. On calibrated 7-Scenes, it improves average ATE by 29.8% over the strongest competing calibrated baseline. We further validate Mono-Hydra++ in a real ITC building deployment using RealSense RGB plus IMU and demonstrate embedded feasibility by deploying the ONNX/TensorRT FP16 M2H-MX-L perception model at 25.53 FPS on a Jetson Orin NX 16GB. These results show that Mono-Hydra++ can provide real time metric semantic mapping and scene graph construction for resource constrained robotic platforms without relying on active depth sensors.

</details>


### [188] [PRIME: Physically-consistent Robotic Inertial and Motion Estimation for Legged and Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17681)
*Jiarong Kang,Kunzhao Ren,Tao Pang,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIME是一种最大后验估计（MAP）框架，通过可微分的接触动力学和摩擦模型，从机载运动学数据中联合估计物理一致的惯性参数和摩擦力接触力，生成满足刚体动力学的运动重建轨迹，在四足和人形机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动估计方法（如EKF或运动捕捉系统）仅恢复运动学信息，无法观测接触力、接触时序和惯性参数，导致接触丰富运动中的重建轨迹违反刚体动力学约束，亟需一种能够从机载传感器实现物理一致运动估计的方法。

Method: 采用MAP优化框架，将测量到的运动学与执行器命令细化为动力学一致的轨迹；引入可微分接触动力学（带平滑互补约束）和Anitescu摩擦模型，形成光滑优化问题以处理多样化接触转换；联合估计摩擦力接触力和物理一致的惯性参数。

Result: 在四足机器人（如Unitree G1人形机器人）的接触丰富 locomotion 任务中，PRIME实现了改进的轨迹一致性和准确的惯性参数识别，生成了包含力和接触标注的运动重建数据。

Conclusion: PRIME不仅通过校准的惯性参数改进了状态估计和反馈控制，还为下游学习应用（如大规模行为建模和机器人基础模型）提供了高质量的力与接触标注运动重建数据。

Abstract: Humanoid and legged robots interact with the environment through intermittent contacts, making accurate motion estimation fundamentally dependent on reasoning about contact dynamics. However, standard sensing pipelines-whether based on onboard proprioception with Extended Kalman Filters (EKFs) or external motion capture systems-recover only kinematics, while contact forces, contact timing, and inertial parameters remain unobserved. As a result, purely kinematic reconstructions often violate rigid-body dynamics, particularly during contact-rich motions. To enable accurate motion estimation from onboard kinematics in real-world deployment, we propose PRIME (Physically-consistent Robotic Inertial and Motion Estimation), a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) formulation that refines measured kinematics and actuator commands into a dynamically consistent trajectory while jointly estimating frictional contact forces and physically consistent inertial parameters. Our approach incorporates differentiable contact dynamics with smoothed complementarity constraints and an Anitescu-style friction model, yielding a smooth optimization problem that remains tractable across versatile contact transitions. We evaluate PRIME on contact-rich locomotion with quadrupedal robots and the Unitree G1 humanoid, demonstrating improved trajectory consistency and accurate inertial parameter identification. Beyond improving state estimation and feedback control with calibrated inertial parameters, PRIME produces force- and contact-annotated motion reconstructions from real robots in deployment, which can be used to provide high-quality data for downstream learning applications, including large-scale behavior modeling and robot foundation models.

</details>


### [189] [CosFly-Track: A Large-Scale Multi-Modal Dataset for UAV Visual Tracking via Multi-Constraint Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17776)
*Xiangyue Wang,Hanxuan Chen,Songsheng Cheng,Ruilong Ren,Jie Zheng,Shuai Yuan,Tianle Zeng,Hanzhong Guo,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CosFlyTrack是一个大规模多模态无人机视觉跟踪数据集，包含约12,000条轨迹、240万时间步（约334小时）和七通道对齐数据。提出MuCO多约束优化器用于连续3D空间轨迹规划。在7个视觉语言模型上微调后，跟踪成功率从零样本的约10-27%提升至78.3-95.6%（SR@1米），提升幅度达53-69个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航拍视觉语言导航数据集主要针对静态目标导航，缺乏针对无人机视觉跟踪（持续跟踪移动目标并保持可见性）的专用训练数据。

Method: 构建CosFlyTrack数据集，包含约12,000条专家/扰动轨迹（来自6,000条行人路径），240万时间步（约334小时），提供RGB、深度、语义分割、六自由度位姿、目标状态（含可见性标识）、中英双语指令、轨迹对元数据等七通道数据。提出MuCO多约束优化器，在连续三维空间中利用BVH加速碰撞与可见性查询，联合优化目标可见性、视角质量、避障、平滑性和运动学可行性。

Result: 在7个视觉语言模型上微调后，跟踪成功率（SR@1米）达78.3-95.6%，相比零样本基线提升53-69个百分点。

Conclusion: CosFlyTrack可作为动态目标跟踪智能体的有效训练资源，填补了无人机视觉跟踪领域的专用数据集空白。

Abstract: Recent aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) datasets have grown rapidly, but they primarily address goal-oriented navigation to static destinations, leaving UAV visual tracking -- continuously following a moving target while maintaining visibility -- largely without dedicated training data. We introduce CosFlyTrack, a large-scale multi-modal dataset and scalable generation pipeline for UAV visual tracking in urban environments. The dataset provides approximately 12,000 expert and perturbed UAV trajectories generated from 6,000 pedestrian paths, comprising 2.4 million timesteps (approximately 334 hours) with seven aligned data channels: RGB, metric depth, semantic segmentation, six-degree-of-freedom drone pose, target state with visibility flag, bilingual (Chinese-English) instructions, and trajectory-pair metadata. To generate high-quality expert trajectories, we develop MuCO, a multi-constraint optimizer that plans directly in continuous three-dimensional space with BVH-accelerated collision and visibility queries, jointly enforcing target visibility, viewpoint quality, collision avoidance, smoothness, and kinematic feasibility, avoiding the discretization artifacts and post-hoc smoothing of grid-based planners. Fine-tuning experiments on seven vision-language models show that CosFlyTrack improves tracking performance to 78.3 to 95.6 percent SR@1 meter, a 53 to 69 percentage point gain over zero-shot baselines, supporting the dataset as a training resource for dynamic target-following agents. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/AutelRobotics/CosFly; evaluation scripts and pre-trained checkpoints are hosted at https://huggingface.co/AutelRobotics/CosFly-Track.

</details>


### [190] [Optimal Knock-Pick Planning for Tightly Packed Tabletop Blocks With Parallel Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17800)
*Hao Lu,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在桌面网格上密集排列的均匀方块物体的抓取问题，提出了一种结合定向敲击（knock）原语和夹爪抓取（pick）的混合操作策略，并通过最大权完美匹配算法实现了最优规划，在合成环境和IsaacSim仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集排列的桌面物体场景中，平行夹爪因缺乏足够间隙而无法直接抓取，现有方法对这类非抓取可行场景缺乏系统性最优规划研究。

Method: 提出定向敲击原语，建模最优敲击-抓取变体问题；通过最小约束装置抽象来识别必要敲击，并利用最大权完美匹配在图抽象上实现多项式时间最优规划。

Result: 在合成设置和IsaacSim中针对递增网格尺寸的实验验证了该方法能高效计算最小化动作数量的最优规划。

Conclusion: 该理论观察为构建交织抓取与非抓取动作的高效操作策略提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Rearranging densely packed tabletop objects is challenging when parallel-gripper picks are infeasible without sufficient clearance around an object. This work studies the problem characteristics for practically motivated settings with uniformly sized blocks placed at planar tabletop grid locations. Since purely prehensile removal can become infeasible, a directional knock primitive is therefore introduced and the optimal knock-pick variant of the problem is formulated. The work proposes a series of abstractions wherein minimal constraining gadgets are covered to identify the necessary knocks. Utilizing a maximum-weight perfect matching on a graphical abstraction yields efficient polynomial-time computation of the optimal plan that minimizes the number of actions. Experiments are reported for increasing grid sizes in synthetic settings as well as in IsaacSim. The theoretical observations provide a promising stepping stone towards rigorously building efficient manipulation strategies that interleave prehensile and non-prehensile actions.

</details>


### [191] [Virtues of Ordered Chaos: Planning with Topple Actions in Tabletop Stack Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17815)
*Hao Lu,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究桌面场景中的堆叠重排问题，通过引入非prehensile聚合动作（特别是推倒动作）来压缩长序列中间重定位操作。提出了一种新的推倒聚合抽象模型，将任务规划转化为卵石运动问题的变体，并在IsaacSim物理仿真中验证了该方法相比纯抓取-放置动作在执行速度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统堆叠重排任务仅依赖pick-and-place动作，导致任务规划序列冗长、执行效率低下。本文旨在通过引入推倒等非prehensile聚合动作来压缩规划序列，提升桌面上堆叠重排任务的操作效率。

Method: 提出了一种有向图抽象模型（聚合推倒动作的gadget），将堆叠重排任务规划问题转化为卵石运动问题（pebble motion problem）的变体。规划器需要根据问题情况穿插pick-and-place与topple动作，生成混合任务计划。

Result: 在基于IsaacSim的物理仿真中进行基准测试，结果表明使用包含推倒动作的混合策略相比仅使用pick-and-place动作，执行速度更快。

Conclusion: 该方法初步展示了为丰富物体交互操作（包括推倒、舀取等聚合动作）建立抽象模型的显著潜力，为复杂操作任务的高效规划提供了有力依据。

Abstract: Efficient object manipulation strategies have significant impact in automation applications. In this work, the stack rearrangement in tabletop settings is studied, with a focus on augmenting the task planning domain with richer nonprehensile aggregating actions, in particular the toppling of objects from a stack to the table. Toppling can compress long sequences of intermediate relocations. Computed plans need to interleave pick-and-place actions with topple throughout its plan based on the problem. In order to generate the task plan and model an abstraction to compute solutions that include both pick-and-place and topple actions, a novel aggregating gadget for topple is introduced. Using this directed graphical abstraction, candidate task plan computation becomes a variant of the pebble motion problem, treating objects as pebbles. Benchmarks are then reported in a IsaacSim-based physics simulation. Results highlight clear benefits of achieving faster execution than solely using pick-and-place actions. Though this work primarily investigates the topple action, we demonstrate that similar abstractions can model other aggregating actions of interest, like scoop. The current work provides a preliminary, strong indication of the promising benefits of abstractions for rich object interactions in manipulation applications.

</details>


### [192] [A Dexterous and Compliant Gripper With Soft Hydraulic Actuation for Microgravity Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17851)
*William Su,Jordan Kam,Yixiao Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将灵巧且柔顺的6自由度二指夹爪DexCoHand集成到Astrobee自由飞行机器人上，用于微重力环境下的操作。在MuJoCo仿真中评估了其在国际空间站标准扶手栖息序列中的表现，相比Astrobee现有夹爪，DexCoHand在保持指令俯仰和偏航运动的同时减少了非预期的交叉轴基座运动，并通过地面硬件实验验证了其灵巧操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Astrobee现有的单自由度欠驱动柔顺爪式夹爪虽能实现在国际空间站上的栖息，但连续灵巧操作能力有限。更复杂的微重力任务需要末端执行器能够维持稳定接触，同时限制对自由飞行基座的扰动，因为接触力会直接耦合到基座运动中。

Method: 将DexCoHand（一种灵巧柔顺的二指6自由度夹爪）与Astrobee自由飞行机器人集成，在MuJoCo仿真环境中使用Astrobee的标准扶手栖息序列（包括接近、栖息及后续的俯仰和偏航运动）进行评估，并在地面进行硬件实验验证。

Result: 与Astrobee现有夹爪相比，DexCoHand在保持指令俯仰和偏航运动的同时，减少了非预期的交叉轴基座运动。地面硬件实验也验证了其灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: DexCoHand具备更适应未来智能化灵巧操作任务的潜力，为微重力环境下的复杂操作提供了更优的末端执行器方案。

Abstract: Astrobee's existing one-degree-of-freedom (DOF) underactuated compliant claw gripper enables perching on the International Space Station (ISS), but provides limited capability for continuous dexterous manipulation. More complex microgravity tasks require an end-effector that can maintain stable contact while limiting disturbance to the free-flying base, since contact forces directly couple into base motion. This article presents the integration of DexCoHand, a dexterous and compliant two-finger, 6-DOF gripper, with the Astrobee free-flying robot for microgravity manipulation. The system is evaluated in MuJoCo using Astrobee's standard handrail perching sequence, including approach, perching, and subsequent pan and tilt motions. Compared with Astrobee's existing gripper, DexCoHand preserves the commanded pan and tilt motions while reducing unintended cross-axis base motion. Hardware experiments on Earth further demonstrate DexCoHand's dexterous manipulation capabilities and its potential for more adaptable intelligent manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [193] [WorldArena 2.0: Extending Embodied World Model Benchmarking on Modality, Functionality and Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17912)
*Yu Shang,Yinzhou Tang,Yiding Ma,Zhuohang Li,Lei Jin,Weikang Su,Xin Jin,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Weizhen He,Wei Wu,Haoyi Duan,Gordon Wetzstein,Xihui Liu,Dhruv Shah,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Zhibo Chen,Jun Zhu,Yonghong Tian,Tat-Seng Chua,Wenwu Zhu,Chen Gao,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldArena 2.0 是一个扩展的具身世界模型评估基准，从模态（视觉→视觉触觉）、功能（策略评估→强化学习环境）、平台（模拟器→模拟与真实机器人）三个维度系统性地拓宽了评估范围，提供对感知质量、交互实用性和跨平台性能的全面测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型基准局限于纯视觉预测、离线应用和模拟器评估，不足以评估日益综合的具身世界模型。

Method: 沿三个维度扩展评估：1）模态维度：从纯视觉扩展到视觉触觉多模态；2）功能维度：从策略评估/规划扩展到作为交互式RL环境用于策略优化；3）平台维度：从纯模拟器扩展到多种模拟和真实机器人环境。采用标准化协议综合评估感知质量、交互实用性和跨平台性能。

Result: 提出了WorldArena 2.0基准，支持多模态感知预测、策略优化和跨平台性能评估，并提供开源基准。

Conclusion: WorldArena 2.0为具身世界模型的进展追踪提供了更全面的测试平台，弥补了现有基准在多模态、功能性和平台多样性方面的不足。

Abstract: World models have emerged as a central paradigm for embodied intelligence, enabling agents to predict action-conditioned future and reason about environmental dynamics. However, existing embodied world model benchmarks are still largely confined to vision-only prediction, offline embodied applications, and simulator-based evaluation, making them insufficient for assessing increasingly comprehensive world models. In this work, we introduce WorldArena 2.0, an expanded benchmark that systematically broadens embodied world model evaluation along three dimensions: modality, functionality, and platform. Along the modality dimension, WorldArena 2.0 extends evaluation from vision-only to visuotactile modalities, enabling assessment of multimodal perception and prediction. Along the functionality dimension, it extends beyond policy evaluation and planning to assess world models as interactive RL environments for policy optimization. Along the platform dimension, it moves beyond simulator-only evaluation to a diverse suite of simulated and real-world robotic settings across multiple embodiments. Under a standardized protocol, WorldArena 2.0 comprehensively evaluates perceptual quality, interactive utility, and cross-platform performance, providing a comprehensive testbed for tracking progress toward embodied world models. The benchmark is available at: https://world-arena.ai.

</details>


### [194] [Learning-Based Adaptive Control for Surgical Robotic Exposure Task on Deformable Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17927)
*Jiayi Liu,Kaiqi Wei,Yiwei Wang,Huan Zhao,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的自适应控制框架，实现软体组织自主牵拉以暴露手术区域，通过视觉边界监测和深度变形估计模型，零样本适应类似任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术中目标区域常被覆盖组织遮挡，而覆盖组织的不规则几何形状、非线性生物力学特性以及术中目标区域可见性有限，给自主执行组织牵拉带来了显著挑战。

Method: 构建了组织牵拉任务的真实模型；提出基于学习的自适应控制框架；通过监测组织视觉边界的变化在线优化控制输入；利用基于仿真数据训练的深度变形估计模型识别最优抓取点，确保自适应控制器的收敛性与安全性。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该框架展现出对类似任务的零样本适应能力，能够完成从初始抓取点选择到目标区域完全暴露的自主牵拉全过程。

Conclusion: 该框架具有应用于实际手术辅助场景的潜力。

Abstract: In various surgical procedures, regions of interest (ROIs) such as organs or lesions are often occluded by overlying tissues, requiring surgeons to achieve adequate exposure for precise intervention. However, the irregular geometry, nonlinear biomechanical properties of overlying tissues, and limited intraoperative visibility of the ROI pose significant challenges to the autonomous execution of tissue retraction. To address this, we formulate a realistic model of the tissue retraction task and propose a learning-based adaptive control framework for achieving ROI exposure. The method optimizes control inputs online by monitoring changes in the visual boundary of the tissue, while leveraging a deep deformation estimation model trained on simulation data to identify the optimal grasping point and ensure the convergence and safety of the adaptive controller. Through simulations and real-world experiments on different deformable materials, it has been demonstrated that this framework exhibits zero-shot adaptation to similar tasks and can complete the autonomous retraction process, from initial grasp selection to full ROI exposure. Therefore, it has the potential to be applied in actual surgical assistance scenarios.

</details>


### [195] [Transfer Learning for Customized Car Racing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17928)
*Benedict Florance Arockiaraj,Richard Chang,Wesley Yee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了深度强化学习中的迁移学习技术，在OpenAI赛车环境中，通过在一个赛道训练智能体，然后零样本迁移或微调到其他赛道，比较了基于模型和无模型方法的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究迁移学习在深度强化学习中的应用，旨在通过利用一个任务中学到的知识来加速解决相关任务，提高学习效率和性能。

Method: 在OpenAI Car Racing环境中，先在一个赛道上训练智能体，再通过零样本迁移或额外微调的方式迁移到其他定制目标赛道；同时比较了基于模型（model-based）与无模型（model-free）两种强化学习方法的性能差异。

Result: 基于模型的方法在性能和收敛速度上均优于无模型方法；迁移学习在大多数设置下不仅提升了目标域的性能，而且在学习过程中也表现出较高的性能水平。

Conclusion: 迁移学习能有效提升深度强化学习在赛车环境中的性能表现，基于模型的方法在该任务中比无模型方法更具优势。

Abstract: Transfer Learning, a technique where a model/agent can use the knowledge/expertise that it gained from one task and exploit that to solve another closely-related task, is often used in tackling problems in deep learning. Through this project, we explore transfer learning in the purview of deep reinforcement learning. Specifically, we want to use transfer learning to achieve the fast lap times in OpenAI's Car racing environment by training the agent on one circuit, and racing it on other customized target environments by zero-shot transfer or by additional fine-tuning. In addition, we compare the performance of model-based and model-free approaches, and observe that model-based approaches dominate in performance and converge faster than model-free approaches in this environment. We observe that transfer learning in most setups not only boosts the performance on the target domain, but also shows high performance ability during learning.

</details>


### [196] [TacSE3: Equivariant SE(3) Motion Estimation from Low-Texture Visuotactile Images for In-Gripper Tracking and Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17929)
*Zhongyuan Liao,Junzhe Wang,Qingyang Liu,Zhenmin Huang,Jun Ma,Yi Cai,Fei Meng,Haobo Liang,Michael Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacSE3，一种将低纹理视觉触觉观测解耦为三维力场并估计SE(3)刚体运动的方法，通过双传感器感知减少平移-旋转歧义，为下游操作提供轻量级补偿信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在手内操作中需要在频繁视觉遮挡下可靠跟踪物体运动，但低纹理视觉触觉图像缺乏足够的稳定对应点，传统的图像或几何匹配方法效果不佳。

Method: 将低纹理视觉触觉观测转化为解耦的三维力场，从接触质心运动推导平面平移，从剪切相关触觉响应估计旋转，并使用DM-Tac指尖传感器进行双传感器感知。

Result: 双传感器感知减少了平移-旋转歧义，支持跨轴和跨物体几何形状的旋转跟踪，提供轻量级补偿信号，在不重新训练基础策略的情况下提高了下游操作的抗干扰能力。

Conclusion: TacSE3提供了一种物理可解释的触觉信号用于夹爪内跟踪与补偿，有效提升了机器人操作任务中的扰动容错性。

Abstract: Robotic in-hand manipulation requires reliable object-motion tracking under frequent visual occlusion, yet low-texture visuotactile images provide few stable correspondences for conventional image- or geometry-matching methods. This paper presents TacSE3, a tactile motion-estimation pipeline that converts low-texture visuotactile observations into a decoupled three-dimensional force field and estimates incremental rigid-body motion on SE(3). The method derives planar translation from contact-centroid motion and estimates rotation primarily from shear-related tactile responses, yielding a physically interpretable signal for in-gripper tracking and compensation. Experiments with paired DM-Tac fingertip sensors show that dual-sensor sensing reduces translation-rotation ambiguity, supports rotation tracking across axes and object geometries, and provides a lightweight compensation signal that improves disturbance tolerance in downstream manipulation tasks without retraining the base policy.

</details>


### [197] [Active Defense Against False Data Injection Attacks in Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17950)
*Gabriele Gualandi,Carl Mikael Larsson,Alessandro V. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人操纵器在有限时域虚假数据注入攻击下的弹性问题，提出了两种防御方法：异常感知虚拟阻尼和可操作度降低，并提供了概率保证。在7自由度冗余机械臂上的仿真表明，所提方法相比传统卡方阈值检测器，能显著降低攻击影响，同时在无攻击时保持标称任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统易受虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），反馈线性化特性使系统存在积分器脆弱性，攻击者可以隐蔽地篡改传感器信号导致末端执行器产生显著偏差而不触发警报。现有基于阈值的异常检测系统（如卡方检测器）防御效果有限，亟需更有效的弹性防御方法。

Method: 形式化了两种防御方法：1）异常感知虚拟阻尼（anomaly-aware virtual damping），通过引入阻尼项抑制攻击造成的异常运动；2）可操作度降低（manipulability reduction），通过降低机械臂的可操作度来限制攻击者可利用的自由度。两种方法均提供标称任务执行的概率保证。

Result: 在7自由度冗余机械臂上的仿真结果表明，所提出的两种防御方法相比仅使用基于卡方阈值的异常检测系统，能显著降低虚假数据注入攻击的影响，同时在无攻击时能够保持标称任务的执行性能。

Conclusion: 本文为机器人操纵器应对有限时域虚假数据注入攻击提供了有效的弹性防御方案，在保障安全性的同时兼顾了标称任务性能，为实际机器人系统的安全部署提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Robotic systems are vulnerable to False Data Injection Attacks (FDIAs), where adversaries corrupt sensor signals to gain malicious control. Feedback linearization exposes robotic systems to integrator vulnerability, making them susceptible to stealthy attacks that can cause significant deviations in end-effector behavior without raising alarms. This paper addresses the resilience of manipulators against finite-horizon FDIAs by formalizing two defense methods, namely anomaly-aware virtual damping and manipulability reduction, with probabilistic guarantees on nominal task execution. Simulations on a 7-DOF redundant manipulator show that the proposed defenses substantially reduce the impact of FDIA compared to using solely a threshold-based ADS like the Chi-squared, while preserving nominal task performance in the absence of attack.

</details>


### [198] [FUSE: A Framework for Unified State Estimation in Robotic SLAM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18047)
*Wei Wu,Honglin Chen,Wenhan Cao,Yao Lyu,Jiangtao Li,Tao Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FUSE是一个用于机器人SLAM系统的统一状态估计框架，通过将时间处理、局部几何关联、估计器构建和地图更新策略分离到标准化接口中，实现了混合速率传感下的灵活设计。基于LiDAR-IMU的实例化在418米闭环走廊序列上实现了1.626米的端到端轨迹误差，相比Faster-LIO降低了7.9%的相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧耦合SLAM方案将时间处理、局部几何关联、估计器构建和地图更新策略紧密耦合在特定方法中，导致难以独立修改某一设计选择而不需重新设计整个状态估计过程。本文旨在提出一个统一框架来解耦这些组件。

Method: FUSE框架围绕观测摄入、传播、更新和状态查询四个接口组织状态估计，将时间处理、残差就绪的局部几何关联、估计器构建和地图更新策略分离。通过LiDAR-IMU实例化验证框架在混合速率传感和方向退化条件下的表现，集成高频惯性传播、LiDAR触发的几何更新、残差筛选和退化感知校正。

Result: 在418米闭环走廊序列上，端到端轨迹误差为1.626米，相对Faster-LIO基线降低了7.9%的相对误差。该框架能够有效在弱可观测方向上正则化更新。

Conclusion: FUSE作为一种组织状态估计设计选择的框架是有效的，其LiDAR-IMU实例化展示了框架在混合速率传感和退化场景下的优越性能。

Abstract: Tightly coupled SLAM formulations under mixed-rate sensing often bind temporal processing, local geometric association, estimator formulation, and map-update policy into method-specific designs. Such binding makes it difficult to vary one design choice without re-engineering the rest of the state-estimation process. This paper presents FUSE, a framework for unified state estimation in robotic SLAM systems. FUSE organizes the state-estimation interface around observation ingestion, propagation, update, and state query, and uses this interface to separate temporal processing, residual-ready local geometric association, estimator formulation, and map-update policy. A LiDAR--IMU instantiation is developed to examine the framework under mixed-rate sensing and directional degeneracy, where high-rate inertial propagation, LiDAR-triggered geometric update, residual screening, and degeneracy-aware correction operate through the same interface boundaries. On a 418 m loop-corridor sequence, the instantiation reports a 1.626~m end-to-end trajectory error, corresponding to a 7.9% relative error reduction compared with Faster-LIO, the lowest-error baseline on this sequence. The results support FUSE as a framework for organizing state-estimation design choices and show how the evaluated instantiation regularizes updates along weakly observable directions.

</details>


### [199] [Bench2Drive-Robust: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving under Deployment Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18059)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Zhenghao Jin,Yanlun Peng,Xianda Guo,Haoran Liu,Shaofeng Zhang,Xingjun Ma,Zuxuan Wu,Junchi Yan,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bench2Drive-Robust 是首个面向部署场景的闭环端到端自动驾驶鲁棒性基准，系统评估了摄像头流故障、自车状态估计误差和计算延迟三类部署扰动对驾驶性能的影响，发现这些扰动会显著降低闭环驾驶性能，且传统图像级 corruption 评估无法完全捕捉这些鲁棒性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶鲁棒性基准主要研究图像级扰动（如恶劣天气、相机退化）对感知模块和开环规划的影响，但部署中存在的系统级缺陷（如推理延迟、自车状态估计误差）在闭环端到端评估中研究不足，这些缺陷会通过反馈回路累积并破坏控制稳定性。

Method: 构建了设备中心（device-centric）的鲁棒性基准，系统评估三类部署扰动：摄像头流故障（丢帧、局部遮挡）、自车状态估计误差（GPS噪声、速度/里程计误差）以及计算引起的控制延迟（模型推理延迟），在闭环环境下测试多种端到端驾驶方法在不同扰动严重程度下的表现。

Result: 部署相关的扰动会显著降低闭环驾驶性能，揭示了传统图像级 corruption 评估未能完全捕捉的鲁棒性挑战。

Conclusion: Bench2Drive-Robust 建立了闭环评估协议，展示了部署扰动的实质性影响，定义了端到端自动驾驶中的实际鲁棒性问题，鼓励未来研究部署感知的鲁棒驾驶系统。

Abstract: Robustness is a critical requirement for deploying autonomous driving systems in the real world. Existing robustness benchmarks for autonomous driving have made important progress in studying the effects of image-level corruptions, such as adverse weather or camera degradation, on perception modules and open-loop planning outputs. However, deployment can also involve system-level imperfections, such as inference latency and ego-state estimation errors, which remain less studied in closed-loop E2E-AD evaluation. These imperfections can accumulate through the feedback loop and destabilize control. In this work, we present Bench2Drive-Robust, to our knowledge the first device-centric robustness benchmark for closed-loop end-to-end autonomous driving under realistic deployment perturbations. We systematically evaluate deployment-oriented perturbations arising from three major sources: camera-stream failures (frame drop, partial observation), ego-state estimation errors (GPS noise, and speed or odometry errors), and compute-induced control delay (model inference delay). We evaluate representative end-to-end driving methods and analyze their robustness under different perturbation severities. Our results show that these deployment-related perturbations can substantially degrade closed-loop driving performance, revealing robustness challenges that are not fully captured by conventional image-level corruption evaluations. By establishing a closed-loop evaluation protocol and demonstrating the substantial impact of these deployment-oriented perturbations, Bench2Drive-Robust defines practical robustness problems for end-to-end autonomous driving and encourages further research on deployment-aware robust driving systems.

</details>


### [200] [4DLidarOpen: An Open 4D FMCW Lidar Dataset for Motion-Aware Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18074)
*Kane Qian,Xin Zhao,Yining Shi,Rujun Yan,Zhengqing Pan,Kaojin Zhu,Mengmeng Yang,Kai Sun,Diange Yang,Kun Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了4DLidarOpen，一个面向自动驾驶的大规模开放多模态数据集，以4D FMCW激光雷达为核心，提供了点云径向速度测量等独特数据，并建立了多个基准任务。实验表明4D FMCW激光雷达的速度信息能显著提升动态场景理解与运动感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集多基于传统ToF激光雷达，仅能提供几何测量，缺乏速度信息，难以有效支持动态场景理解和运动感知。为弥补这一空白，作者构建了首个大规模包含4D FMCW激光雷达速度测量的开放多模态数据集。

Method: 数据集在北京复杂城区采集，包含前向4D FMCW激光雷达（提供逐点径向速度）、旋转/固态/盲区多种激光雷达、环视相机、6-DOF车辆位姿。采用混合标注策略：大规模自动标注支持可扩展训练，人工专家精标注形成训练集和验证集。基于该数据集建立了3D检测、BEV分割、光流预测、运动预测与规划等基准任务。

Result: 实验表明，4D FMCW激光雷达的直接速度测量为动态场景理解提供了互补的运动线索。相比仅几何感知，速度感知表示显著提升了运动相关感知性能以及下游预测与规划任务，尤其针对弱势道路使用者和快速移动物体的场景。

Conclusion: 4D FMCW激光雷达是一种有前景的运动感知自动驾驶传感模态。数据集和评估工具已公开发布，支持4D场景理解、多激光雷达融合、速度感知感知与规划等研究方向。

Abstract: We present 4DLidarOpen, a large-scale open multi-modal dataset for autonomous driving, centered on 4D frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) Lidar sensing. Unlike conventional time-of-flight Lidar datasets that mainly provide geometric measurements, 4DLidarOpen includes point-wise radial velocity measurements from a forward-facing 4D FMCW Lidar, together with multiple Lidars of different types, including rotating, solid-state, and blind-spot variants, surround-view cameras, and 6-DOF ego-vehicle poses. The dataset was collected in complex urban environments in Beijing and covers dense pedestrian interactions, congested traffic, high-speed driving, and unprotected maneuvers.
  4DLidarOpen provides synchronized multi-sensor data and 3D bounding-box annotations with persistent track IDs across five object categories. A hybrid annotation strategy is adopted, where large-scale auto-labeled data support scalable training and human experts refine annotations for the human-annotated training and validation sets. Based on this dataset, we establish benchmarks for 3D object detection, birds-eye view (BEV) segmentation and flow prediction, and motion forecasting with planning.
  Extensive experiments show that direct velocity measurements from 4D FMCW Lidar provide complementary motion cues for dynamic-scene understanding. Compared with geometric-only sensing, the velocity-aware representation improves motion-related perception and downstream forecasting and planning, especially in scenarios involving vulnerable road users and fast-moving objects. These results indicate that 4D FMCW Lidar is a promising sensing modality for motion-aware autonomous driving. The dataset and evaluation toolkit are publicly released to support research on 4D scene understanding, multi-Lidar fusion, and velocity-aware perception and planning.

</details>


### [201] [Fixed External Cameras as Common Prior Maps for Active 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18184)
*Giorgia Modi,Davide Buoso,Giuseppe Averta,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖RGB相机的主动增量式3D场景图生成框架，通过融合固定外部相机（作为通用先验地图）和机器人搭载相机的观测，在无需硬件修改的情况下提升场景语义重建效率，实验表明单张外部相机即可使初始物体召回率提升最高79%，并显著改善后续主动探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用环境中已有的固定外部RGB相机（如监控摄像头）提供的宽视场先验信息，弥补机器人搭载相机视野受限的不足，实现更高效、更完整的3D场景语义重建。

Method: 提出一个硬件无关的RGB-only框架，包含两个核心部分：1) 将固定外部相机与机器人机载相机统一视为RGB观测源，通过前馈3D重建模型进行相同处理；2) 基于图结构的主动语义探索机制，利用部分构建的场景图引导机器人前往语义不确定性高的区域进行增量式补全。

Result: 借助单张外部相机先验，初始物体召回率提升最高79%；先验地图提供的丰富上下文显著提升了后续主动探索的效率。

Conclusion: 利用固定外部相机作为通用先验地图是一种低成本、高效的方式，能够显著增强自主机器人系统的语义场景理解能力。

Abstract: Commonly available prior information, such as BIM models, floor plans, and remote sensing images, can provide valuable geometric and semantic context for autonomous robotic systems. In this paper, we treat observations from fixed external RGB cameras as Common Prior Maps (CPMs): wide-field views of the environment that initialize a semantic and geometric scene prior before any robot motion begins. We present an RGB-only framework for active, incremental 3D scene graph (3DSG) generation that seamlessly fuses observations from both onboard robot cameras and fixed external cameras within a single hardware-agnostic pipeline. By relying solely on RGB observations processed by a feed-forward 3D reconstruction model, the system treats all cameras - onboard or external - identically, requiring no hardware modifications. A graph-based active semantic exploration framework then directly leverages the partial scene graph to guide the robot toward regions of high semantic uncertainty, progressively completing and refining the prior. Experiments demonstrate that bootstrapping the scene graph with even a single external camera increases initial object recall by up to +79%, and that the richer context of the prior significantly improves the efficiency of subsequent active exploration.

</details>


### [202] [RGB-only Active 3D Scene Graph Generation for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18197)
*Giorgia Modi,Davide Buoso,Giuseppe Averta,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖RGB输入的主动式3D场景图增量构建框架，统一了感知与规划，在无需深度传感器的情况下实现了与深度基线相当的F1分数，并通过语义驱动的视点选择在相同探索预算下检测到两倍于几何基线方法的物体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图生成方法依赖专用深度传感器（LiDAR或RGB-D相机），限制了其在仅配备RGB相机的平台（如固定外部基础设施）上的部署；同时现有流程通常基于被动采集的观测轨迹，而非根据部分构建的场景表示主动选择视点，未能有效利用图结构中编码的语义和空间信息。

Method: 提出一个全视觉框架，统一感知与规划，围绕共享的结构化表示（包含物体语义、3D几何、关系上下文及多视角信息）进行主动增量式场景图构建。该框架仅依赖RGB观测，可同时接入机器人机载相机和固定外部相机的输入。

Result: 在Replica数据集上，纯RGB流程与使用真实深度的基线方法达到F1分数相当；在ReplicaCAD上的主动探索实验中，语义驱动的视点选择在相同探索预算下检测到的物体数量是几何前沿基线的两倍以上；外部相机设置表明互补RGB视图能有效引导场景图构建并提升上下文理解。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了仅依赖RGB输入即可实现高质量的3D场景图生成，且主动探索策略能显著提升检测效率，多视角RGB融合可进一步降低探索成本。

Abstract: Current approaches to 3D scene graph generation rely on dedicated depth sensors, such as LiDAR or RGB-D cameras, for metric 3D reconstruction. This limits deployment to specialized robotic platforms and excludes settings where only RGB cameras are available, such as fixed external infrastructure. Existing pipelines also typically operate on passively collected observation trajectories, rather than selecting viewpoints based on the partially built scene representation, and therefore fail to effectively exploit the semantic and spatial information encoded within the graph during exploration. This paper presents a fully visual framework for the active, incremental construction of 3D scene graphs from RGB input only, addressing both limitations. The proposed approach unifies perception and planning around a shared structured representation that captures object semantics, 3D geometry, relational context, and information from multiple viewpoints. Because the framework is hardware-agnostic and relies only on RGB observations, it can incorporate inputs from both onboard robot cameras and fixed external cameras within the same representation. Experiments on the Replica dataset show that the RGB-only pipeline achieves F1-score parity with baselines using ground-truth depth. Active exploration experiments on ReplicaCAD further show that semantic-driven viewpoint selection detects more than twice as many objects as a geometric frontier-based baseline under the same exploration budget. Finally, the external-camera setting demonstrates that complementary RGB views can effectively bootstrap the scene graph and improve contextual understanding at no additional exploration cost.

</details>


### [203] [Assessing Localization Technologies for Pedestrian Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18295)
*Joshua Varughese,Joseba Gorospe,Novel Certad,Cristina Olaverri-Monreal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估了超宽带（UWB）和蓝牙6.0技术在行人定位中的精度，并与全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）进行对比。实验结果表明，UWB和蓝牙6.0在特定场景下可作为GNSS的可行替代或补充方案，提升行人碰撞预警系统的态势感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代智能交通系统需要可靠的行人定位与碰撞预警，传统GNSS在部分场景下精度不足，因此探索UWB和蓝牙6.0等高精度测距与低延迟通信技术的定位性能。

Method: 通过实验评估UWB和蓝牙6.0的定位精度、对环境条件的鲁棒性，并与GNSS进行基准对比。

Result: UWB和蓝牙6.0在特定场景下能够实现高精度定位，可作为GNSS的可行替代或补充，提升态势感知和及时的行人预警能力。

Conclusion: UWB和蓝牙6.0技术有望成为车辆碰撞预警系统中GNSS的有效补充，改善行人定位的可靠性与实时性。

Abstract: Robust pedestrian safety is crucial to the next-generation of intelligent transportation systems. Such systems rely on active pedestrian localization and predictive collision alerts. Pedestrian localization can be supported by Ultra-Wideband technology and Bluetooth 6.0, which offer high-precision ranging and low-latency communication, making them promising candidates for vehicular collision warning systems. This paper assesses the localization accuracy of these technologies for pedestrian alerting and benchmarks their performance against Global Navigation Satellite Systems. Experimental evaluations performed in this paper focused on key performance metrics, including localization accuracy and robustness to environmental conditions. Preliminary results suggest that Ultra-Wideband and Bluetooth 6.0 can serve as viable alternatives or complements to Global Navigation Satellite Systems in certain scenarios, improving situational awareness and enabling timely pedestrian alerts.

</details>


### [204] [REBAR: Reference Ethical Benchmark for Autonomy Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18423)
*Jonathan Diller,David Barnes,Rebekah Bogdanoff,Rhett Collier,Roddy Collins,Keith Fieldhouse,Yonatan Gefen,Cameron Johnson,Anuriha Kodali,Brad Kriel,Varun Murali,James Niehaus,Mish Sukharev,Joseph VanPelt,Anthony Hoogs,Vijay Kumar,Arslan Basharat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REBAR（参考伦理自主性就绪基准），一个用于自主系统的量化测试与评估框架。该框架通过神经符号大语言模型计算场景伦理难度、LLM驱动的大规模测试实例生成以及逼真仿真环境，将运行指标映射为可计算的自主性就绪水平(ARL)评分体系，为自主系统的伦理与法律合规性提供客观、可重复的量化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前伦理具身AI框架大多是定性的，侧重于系统设计层面的安全护栏或红队测试，缺乏可计算的量化指标来评估自主系统的伦理与法律合规性，无法为用户提供可操作的决策依据或可解释的推理过程。

Method: REBAR框架包含三个关键创新：(1) 神经符号大语言模型方法，用于计算和解释场景的伦理难度；(2) LLM驱动的大规模测试实例生成；(3) 多功能逼真仿真环境。通过上述管道对白盒自主解决方案进行评估，将运行指标映射为可计算的自主性就绪水平(ARL)评分。

Result: REBAR能够提供客观、可重复的基准评分，将抽象的伦理原则转化为可验证、可问责的自主性量化指标。

Conclusion: 自主系统日益先进，客观的伦理合规量化指标对于告知用户系统局限性、确保滥用者问责至关重要。REBAR填补了抽象原则与可验证自主性之间的量化评估空白。

Abstract: As autonomous systems grow more advanced, objective metrics to evaluate their ethical and legal compliance are critical for informing end users of their limitations and ensuring accountability of those who misuse them. Current ethical embodied AI frameworks remain mostly qualitative, focusing on system design (through safety guardrails or targeted red teaming), and the realized guardrails often directly disallow unsafe behavior without providing the user with an override or interpretable reason. Instead, there is a need for computable metrics through rigorous testing that allow a user to determine the applicability of the system to the task. To address this gap, we introduce the Reference Ethical Benchmark for Autonomy Readiness (REBAR), a quantitative test and evaluation framework for autonomous systems. REBAR maps operating metrics into a computable Autonomy Readiness Level (ARL) rubric that can quantify ethical performance. Key innovations of the framework include a neuro-symbolic Large Language Model (LLM) approach to calculate and explain the ethical difficulty of scenarios, LLM-driven at-scale generation of test instances, and a versatile, photorealistic simulation environment. By evaluating white-box autonomy solutions through this rigorous testing pipeline, REBAR delivers an objective and repeatable benchmark score, bridging the gap between abstract principles and verifiable, accountable autonomy.

</details>


### [205] [Bidirectional Optical sensors for Actuation Tracking (BOAT) in soft lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18482)
*Petr Trunin,Carolina Gay,Anderson Brazil Nardin,Trevor Exley,Diana Cafiso,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双向光学传感器(BOAT)，通过共印刷的图案化波导嵌入晶格结构，利用弯曲引起的输出信号变化区分压缩与伸展状态，实验验证了高重复性响应，并实现了数字孪生同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人中晶格结构日益普及，但缺乏能够监测其全局变形（特别是压缩和伸展）的高级传感方案。

Method: 设计基于两个图案化波导的椭圆几何结构BOAT传感器，与气动人造肌肉驱动的晶格结构共印刷；通过数值仿真优化波导结构及传感单元设计；在+50 kPa至-40 kPa压力范围内进行100次连续循环标定实验。

Result: 实验标定显示高度可重复的响应，能可靠区分伸展与压缩状态；传感器反馈成功用于实现数字孪生，实现传感单元与虚拟副本的连续同步。

Conclusion: BOAT是一种可靠且有效的软体晶格机器人系统变形监测方法。

Abstract: The growing adoption of lattice-based structures in soft robotics creates a need for advanced sensing solutions capable of monitoring their global deformation, particularly compression and extension. In this work, we address this challenge by introducing a novel optical sensor based on two patterned waveguides arranged in an ellipsoidal geometry. This Bidirectional Optical sensor for Actuation Tracking (BOAT) is seamlessly co-printed with a lattice structure actuated by an embedded pneumatic artificial muscle (PAM), and its performance is assessed. During PAM elongation or contraction, the bending of the embedded BOAT waveguides induces output signal variations that enable a clear discrimination between compression and extension states.
  The designs of both each specific waveguide structure (by surface patterning) and of the sensorized lattice-based unit embedding two BOATs are supported by numerical simulations. Experimental calibration over 100 consecutive pressure cycles ranging from +50 kPa to $-$40 kPa demonstrates a highly repeatable response, allowing a reliable distinction between extension and compression.
  Finally, sensor feedback is used to implement a digital shadow, enabling continuous synchronization between the whole sensorized unit and its virtual counterpart. These results establish BOAT as a powerful and reliable approach for deformation monitoring in soft lattice-based robotic systems.

</details>


### [206] [Unified Walking, Running, and Recovery for Humanoids via State-Dependent Adversarial Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18611)
*Yidan Lu,Yichao Zhong,Liu Zhao,Wanyue Li,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一强化学习框架，使单策略能在Unitree G1人形机器人上执行行走、奔跑和跌倒恢复，无需显式模式切换命令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要显式模式切换或在单一技能间切换，缺乏统一控制多技能（行走、奔跑、跌倒恢复）的单一策略。

Method: 扩展AMP框架，用状态相关门控将训练过渡路由到两个判别器：恢复判别器（身体倾斜>37°时激活）和速度条件运动判别器（覆盖行走和奔跑）。仅用3个LAFAN1参考片段。

Result: 单个ONNX策略以50Hz运行，无需运行时模式逻辑；硬件实验成功实现俯卧和仰卧跌倒恢复及行走-奔跑平滑过渡。

Conclusion: 状态相关门控和双判别器设计有效统一了多技能控制，简化了部署流程。

Abstract: We propose a unified reinforcement learning framework that enables a single policy to perform walking, running, and fall recovery on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot, validated on physical hardware without any explicit mode-switching command at deployment. The framework extends Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP) by replacing the conventional global reference distribution with a state-dependent gate that routes each training transition to one of two discriminators: a dedicated recovery discriminator and a velocity-conditioned locomotion discriminator that jointly covers walking and running. The gate is defined by a single fixed threshold on projected gravity: the recovery discriminator is activated when body tilt exceeds approximately $37^\circ$ from vertical ($|g_z+1|>0.6$); otherwise the locomotion discriminator is used, with the normalized commanded velocity serving as a condition that selects the appropriate reference trajectory between walk and run clips. Only three LAFAN1 reference clips are required to regularize the complete behavior set. At deployment, a single frozen ONNX policy executes at 50\,Hz with no runtime mode logic; hardware experiments demonstrate successful recovery from both prone and supine falls and smooth walk-to-run transitions under the same controller.

</details>


### [207] [ManiSoft: Towards Vision-Language Manipulation for Soft Continuum Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18617)
*Ziyu Wei,Luting Wang,Chen Gao,Li Wen,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiSoft是一个面向软体机械臂的视觉语言操作基准，包含定制仿真器、四个任务、6300个场景及专家轨迹，并评测了三种策略模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言操作研究主要针对刚性机械臂，其固定形态在杂乱或狭小空间适应性差。软体机械臂虽具变形能力，但存在本体感知不可靠、分布式底层驱动等挑战，缺乏系统化的研究基准。

Method: 构建了耦合弹性力约束的软体动力学仿真器；设计了四项任务（基础末端协调到避障）；通过高层规划器分解任务为航点序列，结合低层强化学习策略生成扭矩指令，自动化生成6300个场景及专家轨迹。

Result: 三种策略模型在干净场景表现良好，但在随机化场景下性能显著下降。失败主要源于本体感知状态的视觉估计不准确，以及对变形能力在自适应避障中的利用不足。

Conclusion: ManiSoft可作为连接刚性与软体机械臂在视觉语言操作领域的桥梁，为软体臂操作研究提供有价值的测试平台。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language manipulation research targets rigid robotic arms, whose fixed morphology limits adaptability in cluttered or confined spaces. Soft robotic arms offer an appealing alternative due to their deformability, but confront challenges such as unreliable proprioception and distributed low-level actuation. To investigate these challenges, we introduce \ManiSoft, a benchmark for vision-language manipulation with soft arms. ManiSoft features a tailored simulator that couples realistic soft-body dynamics with contact-rich interactions via an elastic force constraint. On this basis, ManiSoft defines four tasks, each highlighting distinct aspects of deformable control, from basic end-effector coordination to obstacle avoidance. To support policy training and evaluation, \ManiSoft{} includes an automated pipeline that generates $6{,}300$ diverse scenes and corresponding expert trajectories. To produce high-quality trajectories at scale, we first employ a high-level planner to decompose each task into a sequence of waypoints, followed by a low-level reinforcement learning policy that generates torque commands to track waypoints. Benchmarking three representative policy models shows relatively promising results in clean scenes but substantial performance drop under randomization. Visualization analysis indicates that failures stem primarily from inaccurate visual estimation of proprioceptive state and limited exploitation of deformability for adaptive obstacle avoiding. We anticipate ManiSoft to serve as a valuable testbed, bridging the gap between rigid and soft arms in the context of vision-language manipulation. Out codes and datasets are released at https://buaa-colalab.github.io/ManiSoft.

</details>


### [208] [Data-Driven Dynamic Modeling of a Tendon-Actuated Continuum Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18720)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Bjørn Kåre Sæbø,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario Di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对肌腱驱动连续体机器人的数据驱动系统辨识方法（N4SID、ARX、SINDYc）进行了比较研究。实验发现，尽管机器人关节数量众多，但由于关节间存在强运动学依赖，一个二自由度动态模型即可准确捕获系统动力学。模型经实验验证并用于模型预测控制器设计，证明了其在实时控制中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人的动力学建模面临非线性、高维度和摩擦主导动力学等挑战，传统建模方法难以胜任，因此需要探索数据驱动的系统辨识方法。

Method: 本文比较了三种数据驱动系统辨识方法——N4SID（子空间辨识）、ARX（自回归外生模型）和SINDYc（带控制的稀疏辨识非线性动力学），对CERN开发的具有滚动关节的肌腱驱动连续体机器人进行动力学建模。

Result: 实验表明，尽管机器人具有大量关节，但由于关节间存在强运动学耦合，仅需二自由度动态模型即可准确描述系统动力学行为。

Conclusion: 所辨识的模型经实验数据验证有效，并成功应用于模型预测控制器（MPC）设计，证明了其在实时控制中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Developing dynamic models for tendon-driven continuum robots is challenging due to their nonlinear, high-dimensional, and friction-dominated dynamics. This paper presents a comparative study of data-driven system identification methods, including N4SID, ARX, and SINDYc, for modeling a tendon-actuated continuum robot with rolling joints developed at CERN. Despite the high number of joints of the robot, experimental analysis reveals that a two-degree-of-freedom dynamic model can accurately capture the system dynamics, owing to strong kinematic dependencies between the joints. The models are validated against experimental data, and used in the design of a model predictive controller, demonstrating their feasibility for real-time control.

</details>


### [209] [Dexora: Open-source VLA for High-DoF Bimanual Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18722)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jingrui Pang,Zhuo Yang,Kun Li,Minwen Liao,Saining Zhang,Guoxuan Chi,Jinbang Guo,Huan-ang Gao,Modi Shi,Dongyun Ge,Yao Mu,Jiayuan Gu,Rui Chen,Hao Dong,Huazhe Xu,Li Yi,Yixin Zhu,Hang Zhao,Pengwei Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Guocai Yao,Jianyu Chen,Hongyang Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dexora 是首个开源的原生支持双臂双手高自由度操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统，通过混合遥操作流水线收集大规模训练数据（含10万条仿真轨迹和1万条真实遥操作轨迹），并采用数据质量感知的训练策略（离线判别器加权扩散Transformer策略），在基础任务和灵巧操作任务上均优于现有VLA基线（灵巧任务成功率66.7% vs 51.7%），并展现出良好的跨分布和跨形态泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统局限于双夹爪控制或单臂灵巧手操作，缺乏同时支持双臂双手高自由度操作的开源VLA系统。

Method: 1) 设计混合遥操作流水线：外骨骼背包捕捉粗粒度手臂运动学，Apple Vision Pro进行无标记手部追踪；2) 构建物理平台及MuJoCo数字孪生；3) 收集匹配形态的合成数据（10万条仿真轨迹）和真实数据（1万条遥操作轨迹）；4) 提出数据质量感知训练：离线判别器生成片段级权重，为扩散Transformer策略训练降低低质量演示的权重。

Result: 灵巧任务平均成功率66.7%（vs基线51.7%），基础任务成功率90%，表现出鲁棒的跨分布和跨形态泛化能力。

Conclusion: Dexora成功实现了首个开源的双臂双手VLA系统，验证了混合遥操作数据采集与数据质量感知训练的有效性，消融实验证实了真实数据和判别器机制对灵巧操作的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently become a central direction in embodied AI, but current systems are restricted to either dual-gripper control or single-arm dexterous hand manipulation. While low-dimensional gripper control can often be handled with simpler methods, high-dimensional dexterous hand control benefits greatly from full end-to-end VLA learning. In this work, we introduce Dexora, the first open-source VLA system that natively targets dual-arm, dual-hand high-DoF manipulation. We design a hybrid teleoperation pipeline that decouples gross arm kinematics (captured with a custom exoskeleton backpack) from fine finger motion (markerless hand tracking via Apple Vision Pro), and that drives both a physical dual-arm dual-hand platform and an identical MuJoCo digital twin. Using that interface, we assemble a large training corpus: an embodiment-matched synthetic corpus (100K simulated trajectories, 6.5M frames) and a real-world dataset of 10K teleoperated episodes (2.92M frames). To mitigate noisy teleoperation demonstrations, we propose a data-quality-aware training recipe: an offline discriminator provides clip-level weights for diffusion-transformer policy training, down-weighting low-quality demonstrations. Empirically, Dexora outperforms competitive VLA baselines on both basic and dexterous benchmarks (e.g., average dexterous success 66.7% vs. 51.7%), attains 90% success on basic tasks, and shows robust out-of-distribution and cross-embodiment generalization. Ablations confirm the importance of real data and the discriminator for dexterity.

</details>


### [210] [DexHoldem: Playing Texas Hold'em with Dexterous Embodied System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18727)
*Feng Chen,Tianzhe Chu,Li Sun,Pei Zhou,Zhuxiu Xu,Shenghua Gao,Yuexiang Zhai,Yanchao Yang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexHoldem是一个基于德州扑克操作的现实世界灵巧操作基准，包含1,470个遥操作演示、14种操作原语，并评估物理策略执行、智能感知和闭环决策路由能力。实验显示π₀.₅在任务完成率上最高(61.2%)，Opus 4.7在严格问题级准确率上最佳(34.3%)，揭示了感知与策略误差在闭环部署中的累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作评估过度依赖孤立的基本技能测试，缺乏对系统级能力（感知、决策、执行与场景维护的闭环整合）的全面评估。作者旨在构建一个真实物理环境下的系统级基准，以衡量具身系统在完整操作循环中的综合表现。

Method: 设计德州扑克灵巧操作基准DexHoldem，包含14种操作原语和1,470个遥操作演示。建立物理策略基准（评估原语执行成功率与场景保持率）和智能感知基准（评估从视觉恢复结构化游戏状态的能力），并通过三个案例研究实现完整的具身智能体闭环。

Result: π₀.₅在原语执行中任务完成率最高(61.2%)；π₀.₅与π₀在场景保持成功率上持平(47.5%)。Opus 4.7在严格问题级准确率最佳(34.3%)，GPT 5.5在平均字段级准确率最佳(66.8%)。案例研究显示等待、恢复调度、人类帮助请求和重复执行揭示了闭环部署中误差累积问题。

Conclusion: DexHoldem作为首个整合桌面灵巧执行、智能感知和具身决策路由的物理系统级基准，揭示了当前方法在闭环场景中感知与策略误差累积的关键瓶颈，为未来灵巧操作系统评估提供了标准化的物理测试平台。

Abstract: Evaluating embodied systems on real dexterous hardware requires more than isolated primitive skills: an agent must perceive a changing tabletop scene, choose a context-appropriate action, execute it with a dexterous hand, and leave the scene usable for later decisions. We introduce DexHoldem, a real-world system-level benchmark built around Texas Hold'em dexterous manipulation with a ShadowHand. DexHoldem provides 1,470 teleoperated demonstrations across 14 Texas Hold'em manipulation primitives, a standardized physical policy benchmark, and an agentic perception benchmark that tests whether agents can recover the structured game state needed for embodied decision making. On primitive execution, $π_{0.5}$ obtains the highest task completion rate ($61.2\%$), while $π_{0.5}$ and $π_0$ tie on scene-preserving success rate ($47.5\%$). On agentic perception, Opus 4.7 obtains the best strict problem-level accuracy ($34.3\%$), while GPT 5.5 obtains the best average field-wise accuracy ($66.8\%$), exposing a gap between isolated visual sub-capabilities and complete routing-relevant state recovery. Finally, we instantiate the full embodied-agent loop in three case studies, where waiting, recovery dispatches, human-help requests, and repeated primitive execution reveal how perception and policy errors accumulate during closed-loop deployment. DexHoldem therefore evaluates dexterous tabletop execution, agentic perception, and embodied decision routing in a shared physical setting. Project page: https://dexholdem.github.io/Dexholdem/.

</details>


### [211] [Robo-Cortex: A Self-Evolving Embodied Agent via Dual-Grain Cognitive Memory and Autonomous Knowledge Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18729)
*Nga Teng Chan,Yi Zhang,Yechi Liu,Renwen Cui,Fanhu Zeng,Zeyuan Ding,Xiancong Ren,Zhang Zhang,Qifeng Chen,Jian Liu,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robo-Cortex 是一个自我进化的机器人框架，通过持续反思-适应循环、自主知识归纳（AKI）、双粒度认知记忆（SRM+LPM）和多模态 Imagine-then-Verify 循环，使机器人能够从过去交互中抽象出导航启发式策略，显著提升未知环境下的导航成功率和探索效率（SPL 提升最高 +15.30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹驱动或反应式策略在未知环境中存在"经验性失忆"问题，无法从过去交互中综合出可泛化的导航策略。

Method: 提出 Robo-Cortex 框架，包含：(1) 自主知识归纳（AKI）机制，将多模态轨迹蒸馏为结构化导航启发式库；(2) 双粒度认知记忆系统，含短期反思记忆（SRM）和长期原则记忆（LPM）；(3) 多模态 Imagine-then-Verify 循环，结合世界模型模拟和 VLM 评估器验证行动方案。

Result: 在 IGNav、AR、AEQA 上，SPL 比最强基线提升最高 +4.16%，在未知环境启发式迁移下 SPL 提升最高 +15.30%，真实机器人实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: Robo-Cortex 通过从被动执行到主动策略演变的范式转换，有效解决了未知环境中的导航泛化问题。

Abstract: The ability to navigate and interact with complex environments is central to real-world embodied agents, yet navigation in unseen environments remains challenging due to "experiential amnesia," where existing trajectory-driven or reactive policies fail to synthesize generalizable strategies from past interactions. We propose Robo-Cortex, a self-evolving framework that enables robots to autonomously induce navigation heuristics and refine cognitive strategies through a continuous reflection-adaptation loop. By abstracting success patterns and failure pitfalls into natural-language heuristics, Robo-Cortex enables a transition from passive execution to active strategy evolution. Our core innovation is an Autonomous Knowledge Induction (AKI) mechanism that distills multimodal trajectories into a structured Navigation Heuristic Library for knowledge generalization. The architecture further incorporates a Dual-Grain Cognitive Memory system, comprising a Short-term Reflective Memory (SRM) for real-time local progress analysis, and a Long-term Principle Memory (LPM) that abstracts past trajectories into reusable guiding and cautionary principles. To ensure robust decision-making, we introduce a multimodal Imagine-then-Verify loop, where a world model simulates potential outcomes and a VLM-based evaluator validates action plans. Extensive evaluations on IGNav, AR, and AEQA show that Robo-Cortex consistently outperforms strong baselines in both task success and exploration efficiency, with gains of up to +4.16% SPL over the strongest prior method and up to +15.30% SPL under heuristic transfer to unseen environments. Preliminary real-world robotic experiments further support the effectiveness of Robo-Cortex in physical settings.

</details>
